On BusinessCaseBench, frontier AI models already score highly against instructor rubrics, and capability within one model family improves substantially over two years. These results provide strong evidence that AI performance on this class of work is already high and rapidly improving, with implications for business schools, where case pedagogy trains undergraduates and MBAs in this kind of analytical reasoning, and for entry-level professional roles, where such skills have historically anchored early-career work.

</details>


### [100] [Frontier Language Models Struggle to Copy: Text Can Be Better Viewed in 2D](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16072)
*Haodong Wen,Yiran Zhang,Yingfa Chen,Kaifeng Lyu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文发现即使最先进的大语言模型也无法准确复制输入字符串，归因于Transformer位置编码的归纳偏差偏好基于局部上下文匹配的捷径。为此提出2D-RoPE，将文本组织成二维网格，使复制任务简化为按固定列偏移检索，显著提升复制能力并在大规模预训练中保持一致优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿大模型在复杂推理任务上表现出色，却无法可靠地复制位于上下文窗口内的输入字符串，暴露了Transformer架构在简单操作上的根本缺陷，亟需改进位置编码机制。

Method: 提出2D-RoPE位置编码，将文本从一维序列重新组织为二维网格，为每个token分配行ID和列ID，使复制任务转化为在固定列偏移下检索输入token，消除了对局部上下文匹配捷径的依赖。

Result: 在合成复制实验中，使用2D-RoPE的浅层Transformer在超出训练长度数百倍的输入上实现完美复制，而标准位置编码性能远落后；在DCLM大规模预训练（模型规模达1.4B参数）中，2D-RoPE语言模型在复制任务上持续保持优势。

Conclusion: 将文本视为二维结构能够为语言建模带来益处，有望推动未来工作进一步探索二维位置编码的潜力。

Abstract: While large language models (LLMs) can solve advanced reasoning problems in seconds, we show that even frontier models fail to perform a much simpler operation: exactly copying an input string that lies well within their context windows. We attribute this failure to positional encodings in Transformer architectures, whose inductive bias favors copying through a shortcut based on matching local contexts rather than carefully locating the corresponding input positions. To address this issue, we introduce 2D-RoPE, which organizes text into a 2D grid rather than a 1D sequence and assigns each token a row ID and a column ID. Under this view, copying becomes simply retrieving input tokens at a fixed column offset, which makes the task easy to learn. In synthetic copy experiments, shallow Transformers with 2D-RoPE achieve perfect copying at input lengths hundreds of times longer than those seen during training, whereas standard positional encodings fall far behind. We further show that the advantage of 2D-RoPE language models on copy tasks consistently holds in large-scale pretraining on DCLM with model sizes up to 1.4B parameters. Overall, our results suggest that viewing text in 2D can benefit language modeling, and we hope this encourages future work to further explore the potential of 2D positional encodings.

</details>


### [101] [Controlling Implicit Shortcut Reliance in L2 Spoken English Auto-markers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16085)
*Shilin Gao,Mark J. F. Gales,Kate M. Knill*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对语言能力评估系统中分类器过度依赖输入中的“捷径”特征（如音频或文本的某些易被利用的特征）导致考生可通过操纵这些特征而非真正提高能力来获得高分的问题，提出了一种新的训练准则以减少分类器对捷径的依赖。通过在基于音频和基于语音识别文本的两种评估系统上验证，表明该准则能降低模型与可被利用特征的相关性，使其更接近人类评分者的参考相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于音频或文本直接输入的语言能力评估系统（如Transformer）虽能学习复杂的非线性映射，但也容易学习“捷径”，即过度依赖输入的某些特定特征。这导致考生可能通过操纵这些捷径特征来提高分数，而非真正提升语言能力，存在学术舞弊风险。因此需要一种训练方法减少模型对捷径的依赖。

Method: 提出一种新的训练准则，能够减少分类器对捷径特征的过度依赖。具体通过修改训练目标，使模型对可被利用的特征（如音频中的某些声学特征或识别文本中的词汇特征）的相关性降低至接近人类评分的参考水平。

Result: 在基于音频和基于语音识别文本的两种评估系统上，原始模型与可被利用特征的相关性高于人类评分的参考值，表明存在过度依赖。使用新训练准则后，相关性显著降低，接近人类评分者的参考相关性。

Conclusion: 提出的训练准则能有效减少语言能力评估系统对捷径特征（可被利用于舞弊）的依赖，使模型行为更接近人类评分者，从而降低学生通过操纵特征而非真正提高能力来获分的可能性。

Abstract: Increasingly, speech and language processing tasks take either audio or text directly rather than extracting features from these as the input to the classifier or regressor. Often these systems make use of complex, for example transformer-based, processes that have the ability to derive highly non-linear mappings between the input and the output. Unfortunately these systems can also learn ''shortcuts'' where the classifier is overly reliant on particular aspects of the input to yield the output. For the task of language proficiency assessment, this over-reliance can enable learners to increase their score by exploiting the shortcut rather than improving their ability. This paper introduces a novel training criterion that is able to reduce the classifier's reliance on shortcuts, thus for example limiting this option for malpractice in language assessment. This process is illustrated on two forms of assessment system, one based on the audio the other on the speech recognition text. The results show that, for both systems, there is higher correlations with features that could be exploited for malpractice than expected from the human reference, indicating an over-reliance on these features. By introducing the modified training criterion, this correlation can be reduced to be closer to the reference correlation.

</details>


### [102] [Rate-Utility Frontiers for Language Encodings: Comparing Tokens, Bytes, and Pixels Under Controlled Linguistic Content](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16117)
*Ingo Ziegler,Martin Krebs,Desmond Elliott*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过控制瓶颈宽度，在13种语言、5种文字上比较了token、byte和pixel三种编码方式在率-效用前沿上的表现。发现没有一种编码在所有任务和能力区间上占优：pixel保留表面形式最佳，byte保留跨语言对齐最佳（尤其同文字多语言场景），token支持主题预测最佳。编码选择取决于任务、语言混合、能力区间和计算预算的率-效用权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言模型编码方式（token、byte、pixel）通常在不对等的建模约束下比较，不同语言暴露的语言内容量不同。本文希望在控制内容和下游能力的前提下，公平比较每种编码真正保留了哪些信息。

Method: 使用经过验证的平行句子，覆盖13种语言和5种文字，通过共享瓶颈（sweep宽度）绘制率-效用前沿。分离了三个常被混淆的量：编码创建的输入位置数、编码后的潜在容量、压缩后保留的任务相关信息。评估了三种效用：表面形式保留、跨语言句子对齐、主题分类。

Result: 没有一种编码在所有任务和能力区间上占优。Pixel保留表面形式最好，byte在跨语言对齐上表现最佳（尤其同文字多语言场景），token在主题预测上最优。这些表现不能仅用序列长度解释——短输入可能丢弃有用信息，长输入可能保留易于压缩的信息。

Conclusion: 编码选择不是对token、byte或pixel的固定偏好，而是取决于任务、语言混合、能力区间和计算预算的率-效用权衡。

Abstract: Language models encode text as subword tokens, raw bytes, or rendered pixels, but these encodings are usually compared under modeling constraints that expose different amounts of linguistic content to models across different languages. We instead ask what each encoding preserves when both the content and the downstream capacity are controlled. Using verified parallel sentences across thirteen languages and five scripts, we compare tokens, bytes, and pixels through a shared bottleneck whose width is swept to trace rate-utility frontiers. This separates three quantities that are often conflated: the number of input positions an encoding creates, the latent capacity available after encoding, and the task-relevant information that survives compression. We evaluate three utilities: surface form preservation, cross-lingual sentence alignment, and topic classification. No encoding dominates across tasks or capacity regimes. Pixels preserve surface form best, bytes preserve cross-lingual alignment best, especially in same-script multilingual settings, and tokens support topic prediction best. These performances are not explained by sequence length alone. Short inputs can discard useful meaning, while long inputs can preserve information that compresses well. Choosing an encoding is therefore not a fixed preference for tokens, bytes, or pixels, but a rate-utility tradeoff that depends on the task, language mix, capacity regime, and compute budget.

</details>


### [103] [ToolSciVer: Multimodal Scientific Claim Verification with Visual Tool Augmented Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16131)
*Binglin Zhou,Peng Shi,Ryo Kamoi,Nan Zhang,Rui Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ToolSciVer是首个面向多模态科学声明验证的工具增强框架，通过三种类型感知视觉工具（表格行列聚焦、图表结构解析、高分辨率区域缩放）结合GRPO策略优化，在五个VLM上超越四个基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法难以定位关键视觉证据、准确读取结构化科学视觉元素，以及将多模态观测整合为可靠推理，导致多模态科学声明验证效果不佳。

Method: 提出ToolSciVer框架，为VLM配备三种类型感知视觉工具（表格行列聚焦、图表结构解析、高分辨率区域缩放），将密集科学视觉转化为显式声明相关证据；采用GRPO策略优化，复合奖励函数包含答案正确性、格式有效性、长度控制、工具使用效率及工具有效性惩罚。

Result: 在SciVer和MuSciClaims数据集上，对三个模型家族的五个VLM（Qwen、InternVL、Gemma）进行实验，相比基于提示和基于RL的工具使用方法等四个基线，取得了更优性能。

Conclusion: 学习型、类型感知的工具使用策略对于科学声明验证任务具有显著有效性。

Abstract: Multimodal Scientific Claim Verification (MSCV) requires models to verify scientific claims using visually grounded evidence from papers, including figures, tables, charts, and textual context. However, existing methods often fail because they struggle to locate decisive visual evidence, accurately read structured scientific visuals, and integrate multimodal observations into reliable reasoning. We introduce ToolSciVer, the first tool-augmented framework for MSCV to our knowledge. ToolSciVer equips a VLM with three type-aware visual tools, table row/column focus, chart-to-structure parsing, and high-resolution region zoom, which convert dense scientific visuals into explicit, claim-facing evidence, and trains the policy with Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) under a composite reward of answer correctness, format validity, length control, tool-use efficiency, and tool-validity penalties. Experiments on SciVer and MuSciClaims datasets on five VLMs from three model families (Qwen, InternVL, Gemma) demonstrate that our method achieves superior performance compared to four competitive baselines including prompting-based and RL-based tool-use methods, highlighting the effectiveness of learned, type-aware tool use for scientific claim verification.

</details>


<div id='cs.MM'></div>

# cs.MM [[Back]](#toc)

### [104] [Proof-Carrying Multimodal Timelines: Finite-Trace Modal Certificates for Video-Audio Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15285)
*Faruk Alpay,Hamdi Alakkad*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 提出一种基于有限迹模态监控的视频-音频一致性检测框架，通过原子轨迹（视觉、音频、字幕）和公式库定义一致性属性，并生成可复现的证书作为检测结果的见证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态视频系统仅提供片段级分数，无法定位导致视频不连续的局部时间故障；需要一种能精确指出违规位置并提供可复现见证的方法。

Method: 将视频-音频一致性形式化为有限迹模态监控问题，使用同步的视觉、音频和字幕/OCR原子轨迹；构建公式库涵盖语音-说话者一致、视听事件一致、字幕-视频一致、场景连续性和剪辑时间冲击；生成包含轨迹哈希、公式标识、裁决、违规索引、缺陷分数、首个反例窗口、执行引擎和证书哈希的证书；解码MP4视频，提取CLIP视觉原子和AST音频原子，执行密集反事实扰动扫描，输出CSV轨迹和JSON证书并渲染图表。

Result: 实现了可独立重建的证书机制，证书不是正确性证明而是可复现的见证，确保在原子轨迹固定后有限迹检查器能独立重构相同结果。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了比简单分数更细粒度的视频一致性验证，通过形式化方法和证书机制增强了可复现性和可审计性。

Abstract: Multimodal video systems often expose clip-level scores while hiding the local temporal failure that makes a video inconsistent. We formulate video-audio consistency as finite-trace modal monitoring over synchronized visual, audio, and subtitle/OCR atoms. A formula library specifies speech-speaker agreement, audio-visual event agreement, subtitle-video agreement, scene continuity, and edit-induced temporal shock. A certificate records the trace hash, formula identifier, verdict, violating indices, defect score, first counterexample window, execution engine, and certificate hash. The certificate is not a proof that a detector is correct; it is a reproducible witness that the finite-trace checker can independently reconstruct once the atom trace is fixed. The artifact decodes real MP4 video, extracts CLIP visual atoms, extracts AST audio atoms, runs dense counterfactual perturbation sweeps, emits CSV traces and JSON certificates, and renders manuscript figures from those files.

</details>


### [105] [AV-JEPA: Extending LeJEPA to Audio-Visual Self-Supervised Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15295)
*Benjamin Robson,Santeri Mentu,Wenshuai Zhao,Arno Solin*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: AV-JEPA是一种基于LeJEPA扩展的多模态自监督学习方法，通过早期融合Vision Transformer和模态丢弃掩码机制，结合SIGReg目标函数实现音频-视觉对齐，无需解码器、EMA教师或对比负样本，在VGGSound和AudioSet上取得竞争性分类性能，并支持零样本音视频检索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有音频-视觉自监督学习方法通常依赖复杂的架构设计（如解码器、EMA教师、多损失项和对比负样本），作者希望探索一种更简洁优雅的多模态对齐方案，消除上述复杂组件，同时保持或提升性能。

Method: 采用早期融合Vision Transformer作为主干网络，通过模态丢弃（modality dropout）作为掩码策略，训练模型对齐全局视图与各模态局部视图的嵌入表示，并引入SIGReg目标函数以鼓励潜在空间分布达到理论最优，从而实现跨模态对齐。

Result: 在VGGSound上达到57.1% top-1分类准确率，在AudioSet上达到32.7 mAP，并支持零样本音频-视频检索。

Conclusion: AV-JEPA证明了无需复杂组件（解码器、EMA教师、多损失项、对比负样本）即可实现有效的音频-视觉自监督学习，为多模态表征学习提供了一种简洁而高效的架构范式。

Abstract: We present AV-JEPA, an elegant multimodal extension of LeJEPA to audio-visual self-supervised learning. Using an early-fusion Vision Transformer and modality dropout as masking, the model is trained to align the embeddings of global and per-modality local views, while the SIGReg objective encourages a theoretically optimal distribution. This achieves cross-modal alignment in the latent space, resulting in a remarkably clean architecture with no decoder, EMA teacher, complex multi-term losses, or contrastive negatives. The proposed AV-JEPA backbone delivers competitive classification performance on VGGSound (57.1% top-1) and AudioSet (32.7 mAP) and supports zero-shot audio-video retrieval out of the box.

</details>


### [106] [MLLM-DataEngine: Closing the Loop of Multimodal Instruction Tuning Data Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15299)
*Zhiyuan Zhao,Bin Wang,Linke Ouyang,Yiqi Lin,Pan Zhang,Xiaoyi Dong,Jiaqi Wang,Conghui He*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 提出MLLM-DataEngine，一个连接数据生成、模型训练和评估的闭环系统，通过迭代分析模型弱点并生成针对性增量数据来提升多模态大语言模型能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统指令微调数据集的采集方法与模型评估是分离的，缺乏针对性，难以有效提升多模态大语言模型在特定短板上的能力。

Method: 构建闭环系统，包含自适应Bad-case采样模块（分析模型弱点并灵活调整增量数据生成）和基于GPT-4的高质量数据生成（通过代表性上下文示例和丰富信息确保数据质量）。

Result: 实验表明MLLM-DataEngine能够以自动化和有针对性的方式提升MLLM能力，无需人工参与。

Conclusion: MLLM-DataEngine有望成为多模态大语言模型数据策展的通用解决方案。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose MLLM-DataEngine, a novel closed-loop system that bridges data generation, model training, and evaluation. Within each loop iteration, the MLLM-DataEngine first analyzes the weakness of the model based on the evaluation results, then generates a proper incremental dataset for the next training iteration, and enhances the model capability iteratively. Compared with previous instruction fine-tuning dataset collection methods which are separate from the benchmarking, MLLM-DataEngine shows better targeting and can improve MLLMs's capabilities more effectively. Firstly, we propose an Adaptive Bad-case Sampling module, which can effectively analyze model weakness based on the benchmarking results and adjust the generation of incremental datasets flexibly. Secondly, in order to ensure high-quality data for specific capability types, the most representative in-context examples and abundant information are provided to GPT-4, which helps GPT-4 fully comprehend the model's weakness and further guarantees high-quality generated data. Through extensive experiments, we find MLLM-DataEngine could boost the MLLMs capability in a targeted and automatic manner without human participants. We hope MLLM-DataEngine could be a general solution for the following MLLMs data curation. Code, data, and model are available at https://github.com/opendatalab/MLLM-DataEngine.

</details>


### [107] [SPEED: One-Step Pixel Diffusion for High-quality Video Frame Interpolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15585)
*Zihao Zhang,Haoyu Zhao,Siqian Yang,Yidi Wu,Yudong Jiang,Zuxuan Wu*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 提出SPEED，一种一步式像素扩散框架，用于高质量视频帧插值（VFI）。通过渐进式多阶段架构、动态补丁缩放、噪声仅更新注意力机制以及漂移感知时间步采样策略，解决了潜在扩散中细节丢失和多步采样高延迟、高内存消耗的问题，在多个基准上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散模型的VFI方法存在两个关键局限：1）潜在扩散在从潜在表示重建到像素空间时会丢失细粒度细节；2）多步采样带来极高的内存消耗和推理延迟。因此需要一种既能保持细节又能实现高效推理的VFI方法。

Method: SPEED的核心创新包括：1）渐进式多阶段架构+动态补丁缩放，有效学习多尺度运动、结构和外观表征；2）噪声仅更新注意力机制（Noise-Update-Only Attention），防止干净条件帧的语义退化并减少近50%计算开销；3）漂移感知时间步采样策略（Drift-aware Timestep Sampling）配合定制训练目标，直接在像素空间预测图像，实现一步推理而不牺牲生成质量。

Result: 在SNU-FILM上，SPEED将LPIPS降低8.8%，同时推理速度提升63.3%，内存使用降低10.6%。在具有挑战性的4K基准上，LPIPS相比之前方法提升高达51.5%。

Conclusion: SPEED通过一步式像素扩散框架，在保持高质量帧插值的同时大幅提升了推理效率和内存效率，在多个基准上达到最优性能，证明了直接像素空间扩散在VFI任务中的潜力。

Abstract: Despite the success of diffusion models in Video Frame Interpolation (VFI), existing methods still suffer from two critical limitations. First, latent diffusion inevitably loses fine-grained details when reconstructing images from latent representations back to the pixel space. Second, multi-step sampling incurs prohibitive memory consumption and inference latency. To address these issues, we propose SPEED, a one-step pixel diffusion framework for high-quality VFI. Specifically, SPEED employs a progressive multi-stage architecture with dynamic patch scaling to effectively learn multi-scale motion, structural, and appearance representations. Furthermore, we propose a novel Noise-Update-Only Attention mechanism to prevent semantic degradation of the clean condition frames while reducing the computational overhead by nearly 50%. Besides, we introduce a Drift-aware Timestep Sampling strategy coupled with a tailored training objective to directly predict images in the pixel space, enabling one-step inference without compromising the quality of the generated frames. Extensive experiments show that SPEED achieves state-of-the-art performance. On SNU-FILM, SPEED reduces LPIPS by 8.8% while delivering 63.3% faster inference and 10.6% lower memory usage. On challenging 4K benchmarks, it further surpasses prior methods by up to 51.5% in LPIPS.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [108] [Structure of the Circular-Dyadic Convolution Error](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15293)
*Ben Fauber,Alireza Moradzadeh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了用Hadamard变换替代DFT计算卷积时产生的代数误差，给出了误差的三个互补特征：两输入两输出位置完全无误差、误差算子近满秩但零空间对数维、期望误差由单一对齐标量决定且封闭形式可求。总体上，替代误差会使输出能量渐近加倍，除非滤波器位于通用零误差子空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hadamard变换因其实值符号翻转而比FFT计算的DFT更高效，但直接替代DFT进行卷积会引入代数误差。为了理解该误差的性质并可能加以利用或规避，需要对其结构、可预测性和影响因素进行系统刻画。

Method: 通过解析推导，研究了误差的精确抵消位置、误差算子的秩与零空间维数，以及通过随机滤波器平均得到期望误差的闭式表达式。分析了滤波器在不同子空间下的误差行为。

Result: (1) 两个输入和两个输出位置完全无误差，且任何输出重排序都无法消除此误差；(2) 误差算子几乎满秩，零空间仅对数维；(3) 期望误差由单个对齐标量决定，可解析表达。一般滤波器会使输出能量渐近加倍，但通用零误差子空间中的滤波器不产生误差。

Conclusion: 替代误差具有结构化、可预测的特性，并受对齐程度控制。这为理解Hadamard替代DFT的代价提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Dyadic and circular convolution can both be computed in $O(N\log N)$ time using the Hadamard transform and the FFT-computed discrete Fourier transform (DFT), respectively. The Hadamard transform is preferable for its real-valued sign flips, yet its substitution for the DFT introduces algebraic error. We present three complementary results that characterize this error. First, we identify exact error cancellation: two input and two output positions are universally error-free, and no reordering of the output can eliminate this error. Second, the error operator is nearly full rank, while its null space has only logarithmic dimension. Third, the expected error is governed by a single alignment scalar, with a closed-form expression obtained by averaging over random filters. In general, the substitution error asymptotically doubles the output energy, except for filters in the universal zero-error subspace, which incur no error. Collectively, these results show that the substitution error is structured, predictable, and governed by alignment.

</details>


### [109] [Position: Quantum Program Generation Must Prioritize Validity Over Probabilistic Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15313)
*Junhao Song,Yu Zhou,William Knottenbelt,Yudong Cao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文是一篇立场论文，认为将规模假设（增加模型参数带来涌现推理能力）应用于通用量子电路合成是错误的。量子电路需要严格遵守数学约束，存在显著的语法-语义鸿沟。训练未经验证的量子程序导致模型学习语法但无法捕捉希尔伯特空间的物理语义。由于有效电路设计随量子比特数指数衰减，事后过滤在数学上不可行。作者提出从以人为中心的副驾驶转向以验证器为中心的智能体，将层次约束、拓扑掩码和符号代理直接集成到生成过程中。分析表明仅靠规模无法弥合有效性差距，验证感知架构为模块化量子程序生成提供了可行路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 质疑缩放假说在量子电路合成中的适用性，指出概率范式无法处理量子电路的严格数学约束和语法-语义鸿沟，训练未验证数据导致模型无法学习物理语义，且事后过滤不可行。

Method: 提出从以人为中心的副驾驶（copilot）转向以验证器为中心的智能体（verifier-centric agents），在生成过程中直接集成层次化约束、拓扑掩码和符号代理。

Result: 分析表明仅靠模型规模无法弥合有效性差距，验证感知架构是可行的路径。

Conclusion: 生成方法应编码量子信息的任务特定规则，而不是仅依赖模仿学习。

Abstract: The scaling hypothesis assumes that increasing model parameters yields emergent reasoning capabilities. This position paper argues that applying this probabilistic paradigm to generic quantum circuit synthesis is a directional error. Unlike natural languages, quantum circuits require strict adherence to mathematical constraints that manifest a significant syntax-semantics gap. Training on unverified quantum programs means that models learn syntax but fail to capture the physical semantics of the Hilbert space. Since the valid subset of circuit designs decays exponentially with the number of qubits, post-hoc filtering is mathematically intractable. We propose a pivot from human-centric copilots to verifier-centric agents. We integrate hierarchical constraints, topological masks, and symbolic proxies directly into generation. Our analysis suggests that scale alone cannot bridge the validity gap. Verification-aware architectures offer a viable path for modular quantum program generation. These considerations point toward generation methods that encode task-specific rules of quantum information, rather than relying on imitation alone.

</details>


### [110] [A Transportable Threshold-Based Framework for Interpretable Classification of Medical Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15394)
*Antony Garcia,Adrian Noriega,Gabrielle Britton,Xinming Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于伯努利朴素贝叶斯（BNB）的完全可解释、基于规则的临床分类框架，通过监督式χ²引导的统计二值化处理连续变量，在三个基准数据集上取得了与复杂模型相当的AUC性能（Pima: 0.800, Wisconsin: 0.984, Heart Failure: 0.919），并通过校准分析进一步提升了概率可靠性。作者通过完整工作示例证明，模型推理仅需参考表和基础算术即可复现，无需软件或专有工具，从而为医疗AI的可信性和泛化性提供了实用方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑盒模型因缺乏可解释性和可重复性，限制了人工智能在医学领域的应用。现有方法无法在保持透明性的同时有效处理连续医疗数据，因此需要一种统计上可解释、规则明确且无需专有工具的临床分类框架。

Method: 采用伯努利朴素贝叶斯（BNB）模型，通过监督式χ²引导的统计二值化将连续变量转化为二值特征（选择与临床结局关联最显著的阈值），使BNB能够有效处理连续医疗数据并保持透明性。评估包括区分度（AUC）和概率可靠性（Brier分数、校准截距/斜率、事后贝塔校准），并使用泄漏安全的交叉验证校准分析。

Result: 在Pima印第安人糖尿病（AUC=0.800）、威斯康星乳腺癌（AUC=0.984）和心力衰竭预测（AUC=0.919）三个基准数据集上取得了良好性能。校准分析表明概率校准得到改善。通过完整工作示例证明，模型推理仅需参考表和基本算术即可复现。

Conclusion: 该统计可解释框架能够在提供明确、临床有意义的决策规则和校准风险估计的同时，达到与更复杂模型相当的性能。该工作为在真实医疗环境中支持可信赖和可泛化的AI提供了实用方法，促进了模型的可重复性和透明性。

Abstract: Black-box models limit the adoption of artificial intelligence in medicine due to their lack of interpretability and reproducibility. We introduce a statistically grounded framework that provides fully interpretable, rule-based clinical classification using the Bernoulli Naïve Bayes (BNB) model. The method applies supervised $χ^2$-guided statistical binarization to continuous variables, identifying thresholds that maximize association with clinical outcomes within the training data. This transformation allows BNB to operate effectively on continuous medical data without sacrificing its inherent transparency. The approach was evaluated on three benchmark datasets, Pima Indians Diabetes, Wisconsin Breast Cancer, and Heart Failure Prediction, achieving area-under-the-curve (AUC) scores of 0.800 for the Pima analysis, 0.984 for Wisconsin Breast Cancer, and 0.919 for Heart Failure Prediction. In addition to discrimination, probabilistic reliability was assessed using leakage-safe cross-validated calibration analysis including Brier score, calibration intercept/slope, and post-hoc beta calibration, which improved probability calibration across datasets. These results suggest that a statistically interpretable framework can achieve performance comparable to more complex models while providing explicit, clinically meaningful decision rules and calibrated risk estimates. To illustrate this transparency concretely, a complete worked example demonstrates that model inference can be reproduced using only a reference table and basic arithmetic, without access to software or proprietary tools. This work offers a practical approach to supporting trustworthy and generalizable AI in real-world healthcare settings.

</details>


### [111] [Regularity-Aware Stochastic MGDA with Adaptive Conflict-Avoidant Update Direction Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15412)
*Chentong Huang,Lisha Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: This paper identifies that the conflict-avoidant (CA) direction in multi-objective learning is only 1/2-Hölder continuous, causing slow convergence of stochastic MGDA. Under regularity conditions, it becomes Lipschitz continuous, motivating a new method (MoRe) that switches between CA update (when gradient conflict is large) and linear scalarization (otherwise). This improves the nonconvex convergence rate from O(T^{-1/4}) to O(T^{-1/2}) while maintaining per-iterate conflict avoidance.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Vanilla stochastic MGDA (SMG) suffers from slow convergence due to mini-batch noise, which biases the CA direction because the CA direction is only 1/2-Hölder continuous with respect to the Jacobian. This leads to a suboptimal convergence rate. The paper aims to achieve a faster rate by exploiting additional regularity conditions that yield Lipschitz continuity.

Method: The authors first prove that the CA direction is 1/2-Hölder continuous (with the exponent being tight in the worst case) and, under extra regularity, Lipschitz continuous. Based on this, they propose MoRe: when the subproblem is regular (gradient conflict large), they use the CA direction; otherwise, they switch to a fixed scalarization weight. This adaptively combines conflict-avoidant and linear scalarization updates.

Result: The method improves the convergence rate in the nonconvex setting from O(T^{-1/4}) to O(T^{-1/2}) (ignoring log factors). It also provides per-iterate conflict-avoidance guarantees. Experiments confirm the effectiveness in multi-task learning and match the theoretical convergence behavior.

Conclusion: The proposed MoRe method successfully accelerates stochastic multi-objective learning by leveraging the Lipschitz continuity of the CA direction under regularity, achieving state-of-the-art convergence guarantees without sacrificing conflict avoidance.

Abstract: Multi-objective learning (MOL) aims to optimize multiple objectives simultaneously. The multi-gradient descent algorithm (MGDA) is a workhorse that iteratively updates along a common descent or conflict-avoidant (CA) direction across objectives. In stochastic settings, however, the vanilla stochastic MGDA method, SMG, lacks a fast convergence rate because mini-batch sampling introduces noise in the gradients. This causes bias in the update direction, which is controlled by the CA direction continuity. In this paper, we show that the CA direction is $1/2$-Holder continuous with respect to the Jacobian matrix, and the exponent $1/2$ cannot be improved in the worst case. This leads to a suboptimal convergence rate for vanilla stochastic MGDA in prior works. Nevertheless, under additional regularity conditions, we show this can be improved to Lipschitz continuity. Based on this insight, we propose a stochastic multi-objective regularity-aware (MoRe) method that exploits the Lipschitz continuity of the CA direction when the subproblem is regular, and switches to a fixed scalarization weight otherwise. Intuitively, the proposed algorithm employs CA direction update when the gradient conflict is large, and linear scalarization update otherwise. Theoretically, our method improves the convergence rate of SMG in the nonconvex setting from $\widetilde{\mathcal O}(T^{-1/4})$ to $\widetilde{\mathcal O}(T^{-1/2})$, where $\widetilde{\mathcal O}(\cdot)$ hides logarithmic factors. Meanwhile, we also establish the per-iterate conflict-avoidance guarantees. Empirically, experiments demonstrate its effectiveness in multi-task performance and verify convergence behavior consistent with the established theoretical rate.

</details>


### [112] [AI Trading: Evaluating Large Language Models for Technical Market Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15414)
*Geofrey Ntale*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文系统比较了GPT-4 Turbo、Claude 3 Opus、Gemini 1.5 Pro、Llama 3 70B和FinGPT五种大语言模型在技术市场分析中的能力，涵盖K线模式识别、多空信号生成、回测和财报理解等任务，发现GPT-4 Turbo在通用模型中表现最佳，而FinGPT因领域微调在风险调整收益上具有竞争力，但所有模型均存在数值幻觉、上下文窗口限制和横盘市场表现不稳定等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估大语言模型在金融技术分析中的实际能力，为AI交易系统的稳健部署提供指导。

Method: 选取五种LLM（GPT-4 Turbo、Claude 3 Opus、Gemini 1.5 Pro、Llama 3 70B、FinGPT），在四个结构化任务（K线模式识别、方向信号生成、信号质量回测、财报理解）上进行系统比较，使用夏普比率、最大回撤、索提诺比率、信息系数、F1分数、BLEU分数等量化指标评估，并模拟执行管线回测。

Result: GPT-4 Turbo在通用模型中取得最高年化回报和夏普比率；FinGPT因领域微调在风险调整收益上具有竞争力；两者在测试条件下均优于被动标普500基准。但所有模型都存在数值幻觉、上下文窗口限制和横盘市场表现不一致等失败模式。

Conclusion: LLM在AI交易系统中具有潜力，但需要谨慎的任务分解、严格的回测协议和领域微调策略才能稳健部署。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have emerged as powerful tools for processing the heterogeneous information environments of modern financial markets. This paper presents a systematic, comparative evaluation of five prominent LLMs: GPT-4 Turbo, Claude 3 Opus, Gemini 1.5 Pro, Llama 3 70B, and the domain-specialized FinGPT, with respect to their capacity for technical market analysis. The evaluation spans four structured tasks: candlestick pattern recognition from OHLCV data, directional signal generation (BUY/SELL/HOLD), backtesting of signal quality through a simulated execution pipeline, and financial report comprehension. Our experimental framework employs rigorous quantitative metrics, including Sharpe ratio, maximum drawdown, Sortino ratio, information coefficient, F1-score, and BLEU score. Findings from simulated backtesting indicate that GPT-4 Turbo achieves the highest annualized return and Sharpe ratio among general-purpose models, while FinGPT demonstrates competitive risk-adjusted performance due to domain-specific fine-tuning. Both models outperform a passive S&P 500 benchmark under the tested conditions. The study identifies persistent failure modes across all evaluated models, including numerical hallucination, context-window limitations, and inconsistent performance in sideways market regimes. We conclude that while LLMs hold genuine promise within AI trading systems, robust deployment requires careful task decomposition, rigorous backtesting protocols, and domain-aware fine-tuning strategies.

</details>


### [113] [qZACH-ViT: Quantization-Aware Intrinsic Explanations with Recursive Attribution-Stabilized Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15421)
*Athanasios Angelakis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 介绍了 qZACH-ViT，一种量化感知的零令牌、无位置 ZACH-ViT 主干网络扩展，以及 RASO（递归归因稳定优化），通过范数匹配分类与归因梯度并移除冲突成分。在 MedMNIST 七个数据集上，INT8 量化的 qZACH-ViT 全面优于 FP32 基线，预测一致性达 99.9751%，归因图高度相似，ONNX 模型缩小 70% 并加速 1.41–2.39 倍，RASO 进一步提升了稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 紧凑型医学图像分类器需要同时实现高效性和可解释性，但这两个目标通常被分开处理，缺乏兼顾二者的统一方案。

Method: 提出 qZACH-ViT（量化感知零令牌视觉 Transformer）和 RASO（递归归因稳定优化）。qZACH-ViT 在无 CLS 令牌、无位置编码的 ZACH-ViT 基础上引入量化感知训练；RASO 通过范数匹配分类与归因梯度，并去除与分类冲突的归因成分。最终转换为混合精度 INT8 ONNX 图。

Result: 在 MedMNIST 七个数据集上（每类 50 张训练图像，10 个固定种子，280 次实验），qZACH-ViT+Adam 全面超越 FP32 基线，平均提升 0.0313；qZACH-ViT+RASO 平均提升 0.0368。预测一致性 99.9751%，归因图余弦相似度 0.999955，秩相关 0.9944，top-10% 重叠 0.9692。ONNX 模型缩小 70%，单线程/四线程 CPU 加速 1.41×/2.39×。RASO 显著降低充分性误差、提升输入噪声稳定性。

Conclusion: qZACH-ViT 是一种可部署的紧凑型内在可解释模型，RASO 是一种面向稳定性的优化过程，二者共同实现了高效性与可解释性的有效结合。

Abstract: Compact medical-image classifiers need efficiency and interpretable evidence, yet these goals are often addressed separately. We introduce qZACH-ViT, a quantization-aware extension of the zero-token (CLS-token-free), position-free ZACH-ViT backbone with recursive intrinsic patch-level class evidence. We also introduce Recursive Attribution-Stabilized Optimization (RASO), which norm-matches classification and attribution gradients and removes attribution components that conflict with classification. We evaluate four controlled conditions on seven MedMNIST datasets using 50 training images per class and ten fixed seeds, completing 280 runs. All 210 qZACH-ViT checkpoints are converted to executable mixed-precision ONNX INT8 graphs containing 16 signed INT8 MatMulInteger projections with INT32 accumulation. Deployed mixed-precision INT8 qZACH-ViT with Adam improves the FP32 ZACH-ViT baseline mean on all seven datasets, with a mean paired gain of 0.0313 in the dataset-specific primary metric; qZACH-ViT with RASO yields a mean gain of 0.0368. Across 964,920 source-to-INT8 test comparisons, prediction agreement is 99.9751\%, with a mean absolute primary-metric change of 0.000133 and a maximum of 0.004386. Across 3,600 matched intrinsic maps, mean cosine similarity is 0.999955, mean rank correlation is 0.9944, and mean top-10\% overlap is 0.9692. ONNX artifacts are 70.0\% smaller than source checkpoints and provide $1.41\times$ and $2.39\times$ end-to-end CPU speedups with one and four threads. RASO significantly reduces sufficiency error and improves input-noise stability over Adam with the same attribution loss, but does not dominate every predictive or explainable artificial intelligence (XAI) metric. These results establish qZACH-ViT as a deployable compact intrinsically explainable model and RASO as a targeted stability-oriented optimization procedure.

</details>


### [114] [From hyperplanes to hyperellipsoids: characterizing the inherent interpretability of linear and single-qubit mixed-state binary classification models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15433)
*Kaitlin Gili*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对比了标准线性模型与单量子比特混合态模型在二分类任务中的内在可解释性，指出后者实质上是前者的“椭球版本”（即学习超椭球而非超平面），并讨论了几何归纳偏置和特征重要性归纳偏置的差异，为无量子背景的读者提供了通往量子机器学习的可访问路径，并建议将该内容引入本科机器学习课堂。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在为没有量子背景的机器学习读者提供一个易于理解的量子机器学习入门途径，通过对比线性模型与单量子比特混合态模型的可解释性，揭示两者在几何归纳偏置和特征重要性归纳偏置上的本质区别，从而平滑地将量子ML概念引入本科教学。

Method: 采用并排对比的方式，将标准线性模型（学习超平面）与单量子比特混合态模型（学习超椭球）进行直接比较，分析两者的几何归纳偏置和特征重要性归纳偏置，并阐明后者是前者的“椭球版本”。

Result: 发现单量子比特混合态模型本质上是线性模型的“椭球版本”，即用超椭球替代超平面进行分类，两者具有不同的几何归纳偏置和特征重要性归纳偏置。

Conclusion: 该简短表征为量子机器学习教学提供了便捷的入口，建议本科机器学习课程教师利用本文内容将量子ML思想平滑地引入课堂。

Abstract: We characterize and compare the inherent interpretability offerings of a standard linear model with a single qubit mixed state model for the task of supervised binary classification. A side by side comparison reveals that a single qubit mixed state model for binary classification is just the ``ellipsoid version" of standard linear model classification. More precisely, rather than learning a hyperplane to classify data, we learn a hyperellipsoid. We discuss the consequences of the geometric inductive biases of both models, as well as how each model contains a different feature importance inductive bias. This short characterization offers an accessible route to quantum machine learning (ML) ideas for readers who have zero background in quantum and are only familiar with linear classification in ML. In support of ML pedagogy, we encourage instructors to utilize this piece to smoothly introduce quantum ML ideas into the undergraduate ML classroom.

</details>


### [115] [Stochastic Reset Pathfinding: Path-Level Regret for Cascading Bandits over Graph Paths](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15440)
*Guni Sharon,Wei Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出随机重置寻径（SRP）问题，在已知有向图上进行边成功概率未知的 episodic 学习，每次边失败会重置到起点。作者证明最优策略是开环的，归入组合级联赌博机（CCB）框架，提出 Log-Dijkstra 元算法（PathUCB 和 PathTS）。主要理论结果是 PathUCB 的路径级遗憾界，通过每条路径的复杂度 C(π) 分解遗憾，在结构图上比边级 CCB 界更优。实验支持理论，PathTS 通常表现最好，但存在对抗实例使其不收敛，建议默认使用 PathTS 但需谨慎。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子中继网络中的纠缠分发、闪电网络中的支付路由、不可靠网格网络中的投递等实际场景中，智能体需要学习在已知图中选择路径，但边成功概率未知且失败时全局重置到起点。现有模型未充分捕捉这种全局重置结构，需要新的学习框架和算法。

Method: 将 SRP 建模为组合级联赌博机（CCB）问题，证明全局重置结构使最优策略为开环策略。提出 Log-Dijkstra 元算法，基于 UCB (PathUCB) 和汤普森采样 (PathTS) 实例化。PathUCB 通过每条路径的复杂度 C(π)（结合边的前缀和后缀可靠性）导出路径级遗憾界。

Result: PathUCB 取得路径级遗憾界，比边级 CCB 界更优，尤其在结构图（多项式条源到目标路径）上信息量更大。实验显示 PathTS 通常表现最佳，但存在 PathTS 不收敛的对抗实例，与组合汤普森采样在乘法奖励问题上的已知指数障碍一致。

Conclusion: PathTS 可作为实际默认算法，但需警惕对抗实例的存在。路径级分析比边级分析在结构图上更具信息量，为后续研究提供基础。

Abstract: We introduce Stochastic Reset Pathfinding (SRP), an episodic learning problem on a known directed graph with unknown stationary edge success probabilities. In each episode, the agent commits to a source-to-goal path, and any edge failure during execution resets it to the source. SRP captures settings such as entanglement distribution in quantum repeater networks, payment routing on the Lightning Network, and delivery in unreliable mesh networks. We show that the global-reset structure makes the optimal policy open-loop, placing SRP within the combinatorial cascading bandit (CCB) framework. We propose a Log-Dijkstra meta-algorithm with UCB (PathUCB) and Thompson Sampling (PathTS) instantiations. Our main technical result is a path-level regret bound for PathUCB that decomposes regret over suboptimal paths via a per-path complexity C(pi) combining each edge's prefix and suffix reliability. The bound is complementary to the edge-level CCB bound and more informative on structured graphs with polynomially many source-to-goal paths. Experiments on quantum-network, layered-DAG, grid-world, and Erdos-Renyi domains support the theory and show that PathTS typically achieves the best empirical performance among the algorithms tested. We then exhibit an adversarial instance on which PathTS fails to converge, consistent with a known exponential obstruction for combinatorial Thompson Sampling on multiplicative-reward problems. We recommend PathTS as the practical default while cautioning that adversarial instances exist.

</details>


### [116] [Who Became Financially Vulnerable After COVID-19? A Population-Level Machine Learning Analysis Using MEPS Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15446)
*Alexey Kresin,Zien Cheng,Ammar Ahad,Ebiyomare Kelvin,Manish Sivaratri,Prabhjeet Singh,Omar Aljawfi,Olabisi Ojo,Nawar Shara*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究利用2019和2021年MEPS数据，通过逻辑回归和机器学习模型（随机森林、梯度提升）分析COVID-19前后美国医疗财务脆弱性。发现贫困状态、保险覆盖和处方药支出是主要预测因素，尽管疫情后弱势群体负担加重，但基于疫情前数据训练的模型在疫情后预测性能仅略有下降，表明核心预测因素相对稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 美国医疗成本持续引发担忧，COVID-19大流行可能加剧了这一问题。研究者希望评估大流行前后美国人群的医疗财务脆弱性变化，识别关键风险因素，并验证传统统计模型与机器学习方法在人口健康研究中的结合价值。

Method: 使用2019年和2021年Medical Expenditure Panel Survey (MEPS)数据，定义高财务负担为自付医疗支出超过家庭收入的10%。进行调查加权亚组分析以获得全国代表性估计。采用可解释的逻辑回归估计调整后的比值比，同时使用随机森林和梯度提升模型评估预测性能。通过时间泛化测试评估基于疫情前数据训练的模型在疫情后数据上的表现。

Result: 财务脆弱性与贫困状态、保险覆盖和处方药支出强相关。亚组分析显示不同人群间存在持续性差异，2021年弱势群体负担有所增加。但基于2019年数据训练的模型在2021年数据上预测性能仅适度下降，提示核心预测因素在疫情前后保持相对稳定。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了COVID-19期间美国医疗财务脆弱性的人群层面评估，展示了将可解释的统计建模与机器学习相结合在人口健康研究中的价值。研究结果可支持未来的人口健康监测、风险分层和旨在减少医疗财务障碍的政策研究。

Abstract: The cost of healthcare remains a concern in the United States and may have been influenced by disruptions associated with the COVID-19 pandemic. This study examines healthcare financial vulnerability before and after the pandemic using Medical Expenditure Panel Survey (MEPS) data from 2019 and 2021. High financial burden was defined as out-of-pocket healthcare expenditures exceeding 10% of family income. Survey-weighted subgroup analyses were performed to obtain nationally representative estimates across demographic and socioeconomic groups.
  Descriptive analyses were complemented by interpretable logistic regression and machine learning models. Logistic regression was used to estimate adjusted odds ratios, while random forest and gradient boosting models were used to evaluate predictive performance. Temporal generalization assessed whether models trained on pre-pandemic data remained predictive when applied to post-pandemic observations.
  Financial vulnerability was strongly associated with poverty status, insurance coverage, and prescription drug spending. Subgroup analyses indicated persistent disparities across population groups, with some evidence of increased burden among vulnerable populations in 2021. Despite these differences, models trained on pre-pandemic data exhibited only modest reductions in predictive performance when evaluated on post-pandemic data, suggesting that the principal predictors of healthcare financial vulnerability remained relatively stable over time.
  These findings provide a population-level assessment of healthcare financial vulnerability during the COVID-19 period and demonstrate the value of combining interpretable statistical modeling with machine learning for population health research. The results may support future population health surveillance, risk stratification, and healthcare policy research aimed at reducing financial barriers to care.

</details>


### [117] [LLM4EHR: Aligning Clinical Time Series with Medical Event Sequences via Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15447)
*Jingteng Li,Alexander Capstick,Louise Rigny,Iona Biggart,Neil J Sebire,Payam Barnaghi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LLM4EHR，一种结合领域自适应大语言模型与Transformer时间序列编码器的临床基础模型，通过正则化对比学习目标对齐ICU EHR中的临床事件与时间序列数据，在下游任务中取得竞争性能，并支持少样本迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有临床基础模型未能充分探索EHR中临床事件与时间序列观测之间的共享时间结构，导致模型鲁棒性和适应性不足，下游任务性能受限。

Method: LLM4EHR采用领域自适应的大语言模型（LLM）编码EHR事件，结合Transformer时间序列（TS）编码器，通过正则化对比学习目标对事件嵌入与TS嵌入进行时间对齐预训练，使TS表示有条件地依赖于事件嵌入。

Result: 消融实验表明学习到的EHR时间序列嵌入在多个下游临床任务上性能提升且具有竞争力；经验证明LLM4EHR可学习可迁移的临床TS嵌入，通过k-shot适应部署到新队列。

Conclusion: LLM4EHR通过更好地利用时序对齐结构，朝着构建更通用、更高效的临床基础模型迈出了一步。

Abstract: Recent research in clinical machine learning, focusing on outcome predictions in intensive care unit (ICU), has shifted from bespoke supervised models to foundation models, utilising modern representation learning methods. Here, foundation models are pre-trained on mixtures of complex clinical data modalities, useful for various downstream tasks. Existing works often utilise Electronic Health Records (EHR) to provide rich and diverse patient observations to train clinical foundation models. However, existing methods do not sufficiently explore the shared temporal structures between clinical events and time series (TS) observations recorded in EHRs. This limitation potentially leads to less robust and adaptive clinical foundation models, resulting in reduced performance on downstream tasks. To fully exploit this temporal structure, we propose LLM4EHR, a new clinical foundation model trained on ICU EHR data. Combining domain adapted large language models with a transformer TS encoder, we pre-trained LLM4EHR by temporally aligning the EHR events and TS. For this, we propose a regularised contrastive objective to learn robust EHR TS representations conditioned on EHR event embeddings produced by the domain adapted LLM. Supported by an ablation study, we find that learnt EHR TS embeddings from LLM4EHR improve performance on various downstream clinical tasks with competitive performance. Further, we empirically demonstrate that LLM4EHR learns transferable clinical TS embeddings that can be deployed to new cohorts via k-shot adaptation. These findings provide a step towards building more generalisable and performant clinical foundation models.

</details>


### [118] [Relevant and Irrelevant: A Renormalization Group Analysis of Transformer Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15449)
*Parviz Haggi-Mani,Irina Rish*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文运用威尔逊重整化群理论，将Transformer的注意力机制视为对训练后MLP残差堆栈不动点的微扰，并判定其是否为相关、边缘或无关算符。通过推导不动点偏移公式，在合成马尔可夫链数据上验证了四个可检验预测：长程相关下注意力是强相关算符，驱动表示空间的相变；短程相关下注意力为无关算符；转变由第一层注意力头主导；扰动衰减实验揭示模式选择性反转。结论：注意力的相关性并非架构固有属性，而是由数据生成过程的谱结构决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者试图从Wilson重整化群的理论视角理解Transformer中注意力机制的作用本质，回答一个核心问题：注意力机制在已训练的神经网络（MLP残差堆栈）上究竟是相关、边际还是无关的微扰算符？即它能否从根本上改变模型的表示空间几何与学习行为，其效果是否依赖数据特性。

Method: 将训练好的MLP残差堆栈视为RG不动点，注意力机制视为对该不动点的微扰算符。推导不动点偏移公式，得到四个可检验预测：固定点几何、有效秩分布、层特异性、微扰衰减谱。在具有可控相关长度的合成马尔可夫链序列数据上进行实验验证，分析长程与短程相关下的损失行为、表示空间有效秩、各层注意力头的贡献以及扰动衰减谱。

Result: (1) 长链（长程相关）下，注意力是强相关算符，MLP无法弥补的残差损失缺口被注意力闭合，表示空间发生相变，有效秩在Layer 1跳变至输入维度以上并稳定在高维平台。(2) 短链（短程相关）下，注意力是无关算符，Transformer与MLP收敛到相同损失和固定点几何，但注意力扰动衰减更快。(3) 转变由第一层第一个注意力头(L0H0)主导，其对表示空间的偏移贡献是后续任何头的4倍以上。(4) 扰动衰减显示模式选择性反转：长程相关下Transformer选择性地保留慢速马尔可夫模式（动态范围5.4倍 vs MLP的1.3倍）；短程相关下无谱选择性，但对所有模式压制均快于MLP。

Conclusion: 注意力的相关性不是架构本身的固有属性，而是由数据生成过程的谱结构决定。一阶RG微扰框架能够有效预测并解释这一差异。该工作为理解Transformer中注意力机制的本质作用提供了理论视角，揭示其是否"有用"取决于数据中是否存在可被利用的长程结构。

Abstract: Using the language of Wilsonian renormalization group theory (RG), we treat the Transformer's attention mechanism as a perturbation of the trained MLP residual-stack fixed point and ask whether it constitutes a relevant, marginal, or irrelevant operator. We derive a fixed-point shift formula and obtain four testable predictions for the fixed-point geometry, effective rank profile, layer specificity, and perturbation decay spectrum. Testing these on synthetic Markov chain sequences with controlled correlation length, we find: (1) For large chains(long correlation), attention is strongly relevant: it closes a residual loss gap the MLP cannot bridge and drives a phase transition in representation space, with effective rank jumping above input dimensionality at layer 1 and stabilizing at a high-dimensional plateau. (2) For short chains(short correlation), attention is irrelevant: the Transformer converges to the same loss and fixed-point geometry as the MLP, though it contracts perturbations faster. (3) The transition is dominated by the first-layer head (L0H0), which accounts for more than 4 times the representational shift of any subsequent head, consistent with the prediction that the relevant operator acts before the MLP begins integrating out positional variation. (4) Perturbation decay experiments reveal a regime reversal: in the long correlation regime the Transformer selectively preserves slow Markov modes (5.4 times the dynamic range in decay length vs. 1.3 times for the MLP); in the short correlation regime it suppresses all modes faster than the MLP, with no spectral selectivity. Together, these results show that the relevance of attention is not a property of the architecture but of the spectral structure of the data-generating process, and that a first-order RG perturbation framework provides a predictive account of that difference.

</details>


### [119] [Looped Latent Attention: Cross-Loop KV Compression for Looped Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15456)
*James O' Neill,Fergal Reid*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出循环潜在注意力(LLA)，一种针对循环权重共享Transformer的缓存压缩方法，利用循环索引缓存的低秩结构，存储紧凑的K/V潜变量并在需要时才重建，实现高达21.3x压缩且保持性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环权重共享Transformer虽然减少了参数，但在解码时仍需要为每个循环步骤存储独立的K/V缓存，导致缓存开销大。作者发现这些缓存具有高度结构化特性（低秩轨迹），因此可以压缩。

Method: 提出Looped Latent Attention (LLA)，一个训练后缓存编解码器。默认每头(codec)压缩循环维度，LLA-2D进一步将头折叠为一个潜变量以极限压缩。编解码器从教师激活的SVD初始化，并通过KL散度和注意力输出蒸馏微调。

Result: 在相同缓存预算下，每头LLA优于头部MLA、跨层共享、KV量化和最终循环复用等方法；在Ouro-2.6B-Thinking和Huginn-3.5B上验证；在H200上将Ouro-1.4B的批容量从32序列提升到768序列（21.3x压缩）；长数学推理中MATH-500从0.43提升到0.66。

Conclusion: 循环缓存是低秩的但不能安全地坍缩为单状态，LLA有效利用了循环轴的低秩性进行压缩，同时保持了性能。

Abstract: Looped, weight-tied Transformers reduce parameters by reusing a block, but decoding still stores a separate K/V cache for every recurrence step. We show that this loop-indexed cache is highly structured. For a fixed token, layer and head, K/V vectors trace a short low-rank trajectory across loops, while the head and layer axes remain much flatter. We introduce Looped Latent Attention (LLA), a post-training cache codec that stores compact K and V latents and reconstructs loop-specific K/V vectors only when attention reads them. The default per-head codec compresses recurrence, while LLA-2D also folds heads into one latent for the extreme-compression regime. The codec is initialized from SVD of teacher activations and refined with KL and attention-output distillation. At matched cache budget, per-head LLA outperforms head-axis MLA, cross-layer sharing, KV quantization and final-loop reuse, showing that the recurrent cache is low-rank but not safely collapsible to a single state. The same axis advantage holds on Ouro-2.6B-Thinking and transfers to Huginn-3.5B, where an SVD codec remains near-lossless to 32x in decoder-independent evaluation. The cache reduction is exact. On one H200, the latent-store path increases measured Ouro-1.4B batch capacity at 4k context from 32 to 768 sequences at 21.3x compression. For long math rollouts, on-policy refinement on student-generated prefixes raises MATH-500 at 4x from 0.43 to 0.66 and reduces no-answer generations.

</details>


### [120] [Robust Peak-cost Constrained Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15457)
*Shilpa Mukhopadhyay,Sourav Ganguly,Santosh Mohan Rajkumar,Honghao Wei,Debdipta Goswami,Arnob Ghosh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种鲁棒峰值成本约束强化学习（RP-CRL）框架，旨在最大化期望奖励的同时限制轨迹中出现的最大成本，并通过代理优化和基于积分概率度量的鲁棒值估计方法，解决了标准CMDP中零对偶间隙缺失和动态模型不匹配的问题，在理论上保证了近似约束满足，实验验证了其在动态扰动下的安全性和奖励性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准CMDP基于期望累积成本，无法捕捉单次大幅违规可能造成的灾难性后果；峰值成本约束MDP虽更贴合安全关键应用，但其对偶性质不明确（可能不存在零对偶间隙）；此外，仿真与真实环境之间的动态模型不匹配也需要鲁棒性考虑。

Method: 提出代理优化框架，将原问题转化为可解形式；基于积分概率度量（IPM）发展鲁棒值估计方法，以处理动态不确定性；通过理论分析证明适当超参数下代理解能达到与原问题相同的鲁棒奖励值，且约束违反不超过ε。

Result: 证明了峰值成本约束MDP可能不存在零对偶间隙；提出的方法在理论上保证了约束近似满足；实验表明该方法在动态扰动下有效强制执行安全性，同时保持较强的奖励性能。

Conclusion: 本文提出的RP-CRL框架为安全关键应用提供了一种既能控制峰值成本又具备鲁棒性的强化学习解决方案，弥补了现有CMDP和可达性约束方法的不足，理论分析与实验验证均证实了其有效性。

Abstract: We study robust peak-cost constrained reinforcement learning (RP-CRL), where the objective is to maximize expected reward while controlling the maximum cost encountered along a trajectory. This setting is motivated by safety-critical applications in which a single large violation can be catastrophic and therefore cannot be adequately captured by the standard CMDP framework based on expected cumulative cost. Existing reachability-constrained RL methods adopt Lagrangian-based approaches, yet the underlying duality properties of peak-cost constrained MDPs remain unclear. We show that, unlike standard CMDPs, peak-cost constrained MDPs may not admit zero duality gap. We further consider a robust formulation to address simulator-to-real-world mismatch in the transition dynamics. To solve this problem, we develop a surrogate optimization framework and a robust value estimation method based on integral probability metrics. We prove that, with appropriate hyperparameter choices, the surrogate solution attains the same robust reward value as the original problem while violating the constraint by at most epsilon. Experiments show that the proposed method effectively enforces safety under dynamics perturbations while retaining strong reward performance.

</details>


### [121] [ADS-C: Antidistillation Sampling for Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15467)
*Khawaja Abaid Ullah,Mohammad Javad Khojasteh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ADS-C，一种针对分类任务的抗蒸馏防御方法，通过闭合形式的逐输入置信度边际预算添加扰动，在保证教师模型准确率完全不变（零效用成本）的前提下，有效阻止攻击者通过知识蒸馏复制分类器，在CIFAR-10/100和Tiny-ImageNet上使学生模型准确率分别下降29.6、17.4和13.3个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 知识蒸馏使攻击者能通过查询API获取概率向量来训练替代模型，复制专有分类器。现有针对LLM的抗蒸馏采样方法尚未在分类任务中研究，且直接迁移会因教师模型过度自信而存在惰性窗口，无法有效防御。需设计一种对分类任务有效且不损失教师准确率的抗蒸馏防御。

Method: 提出ADS-C方法，在闭合形式的逐输入置信度边际预算下构造扰动，保证每个输出的top-1预测不变（教师准确率完全保留），同时通过梯度引导的扰动破坏概率向量中的可蒸馏信息，使攻击者训练的学生模型准确率大幅下降。

Result: ADS-C在教师准确率零损失下，使学生模型在CIFAR-100下降17.4个百分点，CIFAR-10下降29.6个百分点，Tiny-ImageNet下降13.3个百分点；而未经修改的防御要达到同等学生退化需要牺牲教师27.5、32.9、22.2个百分点的准确率。硬标签攻击者无任何收获，软标签攻击者训练的学生比硬标签基线还低29.7个百分点。

Conclusion: ADS-C是首个对分类任务效用成本精确为零的抗蒸馏防御方法，不仅消除了蒸馏动机，还逆转了激励——攻击者反而会因选择蒸馏而获得更差的学生模型。

Abstract: Knowledge distillation enables an adversary to replicate a proprietary classifier by querying its prediction interface and training a surrogate on the returned probability vectors. Antidistillation sampling, proposed for large language models, counters this threat with an input-dependent, gradient-directed perturbation of the served distribution; its transfer to classification has not been studied. Adapting the defense to classification, we show its behavior is governed by the distribution of the teacher's per-input confidence margins. Because well-trained classifiers are severely overconfident, the direct transfer exhibits an inert window: below a closed-form-predictable threshold, it affects neither attacker nor defender; beyond it, the defense undergoes a phase transition and degrades the teacher faster than the attacker's student. Temperature softening rescales the transition in closed form, and every temperature configuration lies on the same unfavorable trade-off curve. Our method, ADS-C, composes the perturbation under a closed-form, per-input margin budget that provably preserves every served top-1 prediction, so the defended teacher's accuracy equals the undefended teacher's identically. Under this guarantee the distilled student still loses 17.4 percentage points on CIFAR-100, 29.6 on CIFAR-10, and 13.3 on Tiny-ImageNet; matching this degradation with the unmodified defense costs 27.5, 32.9, and 22.2 points of teacher accuracy. Because served labels are unchanged, a hard-label attacker gains nothing, while the defended soft output trains a student up to 29.7 points below that floor: the incentive to distill served probabilities is not merely removed but reversed. To our knowledge, ADS-C is the first antidistillation defense for classification whose utility cost is exactly zero.

</details>


### [122] [Deep Learning Approaches for Sleep Apnea Classification from Polysomnographic EEG Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15477)
*Shashank Manjunath,Mukesh Cheemakurthi,Aarti Sathyanarayana*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究在2410名儿童受试者上系统比较了多种深度学习架构（Vision Transformers、Graph Attention Networks）和特征表示（原始时域信号、STFT频谱图、相干性图、拓扑数据分析特征）用于多通道EEG自动睡眠呼吸暂停检测，最佳模型（基于TDA特征的ViT）在575名测试集上达到0.750 AUC，并揭示了不同人口统计学和睡眠分期下的性能差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统多导睡眠图诊断睡眠呼吸暂停需要大量人工分析，耗时耗力；尽管已有研究表明EEG可检测睡眠呼吸暂停的中枢神经效应，但现有工作多采用单一特征类型和不同分类算法，缺乏对多种深度学习架构和特征表示的系统性比较。本文旨在填补这一空白，为基于EEG的自动化OSA筛查提供全面的基准评估。

Method: 在2410名儿童受试者（测试集575名）上，采用年龄和性别匹配的训练/测试集划分，系统比较Vision Transformers和Graph Attention Networks两大类深度学习架构，覆盖五种信号表示：原始时域信号、短时傅里叶变换频谱图、基于相干性的图结构、两种拓扑数据分析衍生特征。评估指标为AUC，并进行按年龄、性别、AHI严重程度和睡眠分期的分层分析。

Result: 最佳测试AUC为0.750，由基于TDA特征训练的Vision Transformer模型取得。分层分析显示模型性能在不同人口统计学特征（年龄、性别、AHI严重程度）和睡眠阶段（N1、N2、N3、REM）之间存在显著差异。

Conclusion: 基于EEG的自动OSA筛查具有可行性，但临床部署仍需解决性能变异等关键挑战。本研究为后续大规模、多中心EEG睡眠呼吸暂停检测研究提供了重要的基准参考。

Abstract: Sleep apnea diagnosis via polysomnography remains resource intensive and relies on time consuming manual data analysis and scoring. Recent work has demonstrated that central nervous system effects of sleep apnea events can be detected through electroencephalogram (EEG) signals. However, most work uses a single feature type on various datasets combined with different classification algorithms. In this work, we present a comprehensive comparison of deep learning architectures and feature representations for automated sleep apnea detection from multichannel EEG on a single dataset of pediatric subjects. We evaluate Vision Transformers and Graph Attention Networks across distinct signal representations: raw temporal signals, short-time Fourier transform spectrograms, coherence based graphs, and two topological data analysis (TDA) derived features. Using age and sex matching of our train and test sets, we train on 2410 pediatric subjects and test on 575 pediatric subjects. We achieve a best test AUC of 0.750 using a vision transformer based model trained on TDA features. Stratified analysis across patient demographics (age, sex, AHI severity) and sleep stages (N1, N2, N3, REM) reveals significant performance variation. Our results demonstrate the feasibility of EEG based automated OSA screening while highlighting essential challenges for clinical deployment.

</details>


### [123] [Inpainting Insights: Elevating Visual XAI with Photorealistic Perturbations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15482)
*Josef Lindl,Mariana Chaves,Damien Garreau*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对可解释人工智能（XAI）中扰动方法的不足，提出用生成式图像修复（generative inpainting）替代传统像素替换技术来改进LIME方法，生成更逼真、符合数据分布的扰动样本，从而提升图像解释质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扰动解释方法（如LIME）在图像上使用像素替换（如固定颜色填充）会生成不真实的、脱离训练数据分布的样本（out-of-distribution），并留有可见伪影，误导模型并降低解释质量。需要更真实的扰动方式来提升解释的可靠性。

Method: 对广泛使用的扰动方法LIME进行改进：用生成式图像修复（generative inpainting）技术取代传统的像素替换（如预定义颜色填充）或确定性扰动技术，从而生成与原始数据分布更一致、更具照片真实感的扰动样本。

Result: 生成的扰动样本具有更高的照片真实感，与原始数据分布对齐更好，最终提升了解释质量（explanation quality）。

Conclusion: 生成式修复能有效克服传统扰动方法生成虚假样本的缺陷，增强基于扰动的XAI方法在图像领域的可靠性与解释效果。

Abstract: The increasing complexity of state-of-the-art machine learning models has made their behavior progressively harder to interpret, spurring rapid advancements in the field of eXplainable Artificial Intelligence (XAI). Among many methods proposed, perturbation-based approaches play a major role. By systematically altering (perturbing) input features, these approaches measure the impact on the model's predictions. For image data, traditional perturbation techniques, often involve replacing pixel values e.g., with a pre-defined color. However, such approaches, but also more refined deterministic techniques, generate unrealistic out-of-distribution samples and often leave visible artifacts, which can mislead the model and compromise explanation quality. In this work, we adjust LIME, a widely used perturbation-based method, to demonstrate how generative inpainting can improve perturbation-based explanations for images. We achieve photorealistic perturbed samples that align better with the original data distribution and enhance explanation quality.

</details>


### [124] [Diffusion models recover accurate mixture weights despite score function insensitivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15485)
*Andrew Dennehy,Ramchandran Muthukumar,Rebecca Willett,Nisha Chandramoorthy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了基于分数的生成模型的一个悖论：模型虽然能覆盖多模态分布的所有模态，却可能无法正确学习模态权重（混合权重）。作者通过将扩散分数匹配（DSM）损失与从生成样本中估计混合权重的误差联系起来，解释了这一现象。他们定义了扩散分数敏感性指数（DSSI）来衡量DSM损失相对于参数变化的变异性，并证明了DSSI决定了生成样本恢复参数（如混合权重）的精度。在高斯混合模型下，他们证明了混合权重估计误差与DSM损失同阶。实验表明，在典型噪声调度下，敏感性在加噪过程中出现，并预测了模型恢复混合权重的能力；同时，噪声调度的选择可以降低敏感性，从而抑制模态放大。该敏感性框架可推广到目标分布的任何定性参数恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决分数生成模型的一个矛盾现象：模型似乎能覆盖所有模态，但模态的相对幅度（混合权重）可能学习不正确。作者希望从理论上解释这一悖论，并量化模型恢复混合权重等参数的能力。

Method: 1. 将扩散分数匹配（DSM）损失与混合权重估计误差建立理论联系。2. 定义扩散分数敏感性指数（DSSI）为DSM损失相对于参数变化的变异性。3. 在高斯混合模型下严格证明混合权重估计误差与DSM损失同阶。4. 通过实验验证敏感性在噪声过程中的出现及其对权重恢复的预测能力。5. 分析噪声调度对敏感性的影响。

Result: 1. 证明了即使目标分数对混合权重不敏感，中间噪声水平的分数仍能携带权重信息，从而生成样本可准确恢复权重。2. 定义了DSSI，并证明它控制着从生成样本中估计参数的精度。3. 在高斯混合模型中，严格证明混合权重估计误差与DSM损失同阶。4. 实验显示基准数据分布在典型噪声调度下会出现敏感性，且敏感性值可预测模型恢复混合权重的效果。5. 噪声调度的选择能改变扩散敏感性，进而影响模态放大现象。

Conclusion: 该工作不仅解决了分数生成模型中模态权重学习的悖论，还提出了一个统一的敏感性框架，用于理解生成模型如何恢复目标分布的各种定性参数（如混合权重）。这一框架有助于指导噪声调度设计，以改善生成质量。

Abstract: Score-based generative models exhibit a puzzling behavior: they often appear to cover all modes of a target multimodal distribution and yet may fail to learn the correct relative mode amplitudes, which can be interpreted as mixture weights. We resolve this apparent paradox by relating the diffusion score matching (DSM) loss to the error in estimating mixture weights from generated samples. We show that, even when the target score is insensitive to mixture weights, generated samples can recover the weights accurately if the scores at intermediate noise levels are informative about the weights. Accordingly, we define the diffusion score sensitivity index (DSSI) as the variation in the DSM loss relative to changes in a parameter. We then show that the DSSI governs the accuracy with which the parameter of the target distribution can be estimated from generated samples. For Gaussian mixtures in arbitrary dimensions, we prove that the mixture weight estimation errors are on the same order as the DSM loss under mild conditions. Empirically, we show the emergence of sensitivity during the noising process of benchmark data distributions under typical noise schedules, and that these sensitivity values predict how well a well-trained model recovers mixture weights. Furthermore, we show that the choice of noise schedule can reduce diffusion sensitivity, leading to mode amplification. Although we focus on mixture weights, the proposed sensitivity framework governs the recovery of any qualitative parameter of the target distribution.

</details>


### [125] [An Auto-Scaling Approach for Serverless Environments Based on a Multi-Expert Consensus Mechanism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15511)
*Mobina Kashaniyan,Mehrdad Ashtiani,Amirhossein Ghassemi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种面向无服务器计算的依赖感知自动缩放框架，结合有向依赖图瓶颈识别、多模型集成预测（MLP/LSTM/CNN + 贝叶斯模型平均）、冷启动感知与成本比较控制策略，在真实负载下实现99.88%预测精度并降低基础设施成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无服务器计算虽提供自动资源管理和按需付费，但由于动态负载、冷启动延迟和函数间依赖关系，有效的自动缩放仍然面临挑战，现有方法难以同时兼顾依赖分析、精准预测与成本优化。

Method: 1) 将无服务器应用表示为有向依赖图，采用加权度中心性识别结构重要函数；2) 使用MLP、LSTM、CNN三个轻量模型预测资源需求，并通过基于性能加权的概率集成（类似贝叶斯模型平均）融合输出；3) 控制器结合冷启动感知和成本比较，选择扩容、缩容或保持动作。

Result: 实验表明：监督预测显著优于无监督聚类；集成模型达到99.88%预测精度，误差低于代表性混合预测方法；在多种云定价模型下均实现基础设施成本降低，同时维持性能目标。

Conclusion: 将依赖分析、多专家预测与成本感知控制相结合，为无服务器自动缩放提供了一种稳健且实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Serverless computing provides automatic resource management and pay-per-use execution, but effective autoscaling remains challenging because of dynamic workloads, cold-start latency, and dependencies among functions. We present a dependency-aware autoscaling framework that integrates graph-based bottleneck identification, short-term workload forecasting, multi-model consensus, and cost-aware scaling control. Serverless applications are represented as directed dependency graphs, and structurally important functions are identified using weighted degree centrality. Resource demand is predicted using lightweight MLP, LSTM, and CNN models. Their outputs are combined through a performance-weighted probabilistic ensemble inspired by Bayesian model averaging. The controller further incorporates cold-start awareness and cost comparison to select among scale-up, scale-down, and hold actions. Experiments using real workload traces show that supervised forecasting substantially outperforms unsupervised clustering for autoscaling decision generation. The proposed ensemble achieves 99.88 percent prediction accuracy and reduces prediction error compared with representative hybrid forecasting methods. Evaluations across multiple cloud pricing models also demonstrate consistent infrastructure cost reductions while maintaining performance targets. The results show that combining dependency analysis, multi-expert forecasting, and cost-aware control provides a robust and practical solution for serverless autoscaling.

</details>


### [126] [Cache-Aware Prompt Compression:A Two-Tier Cost Model for LLM API Caching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15516)
*Yan Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出缓存感知的提示压缩（CAPC），结合查询无关压缩与显式缓存控制及层级保持比率约束，在真实缓存命中率（~0.83）下比查询感知压缩更经济，在LongBench-v2上平均节省49%成本，并在三个生产工作负载中验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有提示压缩文献假设缓存命中率ρ=1.0的理想情况，但实际生产部署中提示缓存和压缩存在根本性矛盾：查询感知压缩会为每个查询生成不同的压缩前缀，机械地使前缀缓存失效。需要弥合压缩研究与实际缓存架构之间的差距。

Method: 1) 在Sonnet 4.6 API上实证刻画缓存架构，发现两层缓存架构（热层/冷层），在~3500 token处存在锐利阈值，ρ~0.83；2) 建立成本模型预测查询感知压缩在压缩比r≥6时优于朴素缓存；3) 提出CAPC：查询无关压缩 + 显式cache_control + 层级保持比率约束（防止过压缩导致缓存前缀落入热层）。

Result: LongBench-v2上16/16配置最优，比纯缓存节省49%，比查询感知压缩节省64%，比基线节省90%，质量差异<0.05。三个生产工作负载：企业工具助手（94k token schema，51.7%成本降低，r=3）；知识图谱RAG（FastAPI上9.3x，httpx上2.4x）；tau-bench零售基准（成本最优，奖励与基线相同36/50，p=1.00），查询感知压缩最贵（+40.1%）。

Conclusion: CAPC是首个在实际缓存命中率下被验证优于查询感知压缩的策略，在公共基准上首次实验确认了交叉模型的负ROI预测，证明查询感知压缩在生产环境中可能不仅不省钱反而更贵。

Abstract: Production LLM deployments combine two cost-reduction primitives: prompt caching (a discounted rate for re-used token prefixes) and prompt compression (fewer tokens sent). The compression literature has standardized on query-aware methods that produce a different compressed prefix per query, mechanically invalidating the prefix-strict cache on every call. We characterize this cost empirically on Anthropic's Sonnet 4.6 API and find caching is far from the rho=1.0 ideal the literature assumes: Sonnet's cache has a two-tier architecture with a sharp threshold near 3,500 tokens, below which the hit rate plateaus at rho~0.83 across 30-call sessions. Our cost model predicts, and experiments confirm, that under realistic rho, query-aware compression beats naive caching at high compression ratios (r>=6). We propose Cache-Aware Prompt Compression (CAPC), pairing query-agnostic compression with explicit cache_control plus a tier-preserving ratio bound that prevents over-compression from pushing the cached prefix into the hot tier. CAPC is the cheapest strategy in 16/16 configurations on LongBench-v2, with mean savings of 49% over cache-only, 64% over query-aware compression, and 90% over vanilla, at quality within 0.05 of the uncompressed baseline. We validate CAPC on three production workloads: an enterprise tool-using assistant with a 94k-token schema prefix (51.7% cost reduction at r=3); a graphify knowledge-graph RAG pipeline across two codebases (9.3x vs cache-all on FastAPI, 2.4x on httpx); and the public tau-bench retail benchmark (50 tasks), where CAPC is the cheapest of four strategies with reward exactly equal to vanilla (both 36/50, p=1.00) while query-aware compression is the most expensive at +40.1% over vanilla -- the first production confirmation of the crossover model's negative-ROI prediction on a public benchmark.

</details>


### [127] [Recursive Harness Self-Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15524)
*Hyunin Lee,Jinglue Xu,Jeffrey Seely,Donghyun Lee,Matei Zaharia,Yujin Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出递归式框架自改进（RHI），通过迭代优化用户构建的框架（以提示形式定义智能体循环），利用配对反馈提升执行轨迹质量，在30个合成机器学习研究任务中，低推理成本智能体的性能超越高推理成本设置，同时降低最多60%推理成本，其收益主要源于改进的任务特定上下文管理而非更长推理链。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在模型-框架共同演化范式下，框架不仅是推理时的支架，更是数据生成组件，其执行轨迹影响未来基础模型训练。然而持续更新提供商构建的框架成本高昂，因此需要探索轻量级、仅需少量迭代的任务特定框架优化方法，以提升轨迹质量。

Method: 提出递归式框架自改进（RHI），将框架表示为智能体循环的提示级规范，通过在其自身修订历史上的成对反馈进行迭代优化，无需大规模参数更新。

Result: 在30个合成机器学习研究任务（涵盖量化金融、机器人、药学）中，仅需少量RHI迭代即可显著提升低推理努力智能体的性能上限，超过对应最大推理努力设置，同时降低最高60%推理成本。收益主要源自改进的任务特定上下文管理（通过更有效的智能体间信息流动），而非更长的推理轨迹。

Conclusion: 将RHI的行为形式化为信息论假说，揭示其隐式优化目标；RHI可视为模型-框架共同演化范式下持续学习的实用算法。

Abstract: Under model--harness co-evolution, harnesses are not merely inference-time scaffolds but data-generating components whose execution traces can shape future foundation models. This motivates harness-in-the-loop learning: optimizing harnesses for both immediate agent performance and the quality of traces used for future model training. However, continually updating provider-built scaffolds is costly and labor-intensive. We therefore investigate whether optimizing user-constructed harnesses in a task-specific manner can improve execution-trace quality while remaining computationally lightweight and requiring only a few update iterations. To this end, we introduce Recursive Harness Self-Improvement (RHI), which represents the harness as a prompt-level specification of the agent loop and iteratively refines it using pairwise feedback over its own revision history. Across 30 synthetic machine-learning research tasks spanning quantitative finance, robotics, and pharmacy, a few RHI iterations suffice to substantially raise the performance ceiling of low-reasoning-effort agents, exceeding the corresponding maximum-reasoning-effort setting while reducing inference cost by up to 60%. We show that these gains arise primarily from improved task-specific context management through more effective inter-agent information flow rather than longer reasoning traces. Finally, we formalize this behavior as an information-theoretic hypothesis for RHI's implicit optimization objective, suggesting RHI as a practical algorithm for continual learning within the paradigm of model--harness co-evolution.

</details>


### [128] [Kolmogorov--Arnold Networks for Small Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15525)
*Felippe Alves,Renato Vicente*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: KANs换用边缘函数替代固定激活，虽然在小规模上可解释性良好，但作为Transformer前馈网络的替代方案，在多个基准测试中未表现出优于强MLP基线的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证KAN的两个核心宣称：（1）作为可解释性接口的能力；（2）作为Transformer前馈网络替代方案的性能。

Method: 使用六层10M参数的B样条KAN重构884,736条边，通过NLS和活性分析评估可解释性；在BabyLM上比较MLP、SwiGLU、分组Chebyshev、GR-KAN等架构；进行多种子BLiMP评估、EWoK测试、WikiText-103及286M参数大规模对比。

Result: 87.8%边缘超过非线性阈值，0.4%不活跃；剪枝20-25%低活性边损失变化可忽略。小基网格(gird-2)下接近完全的fPCA压缩和闭式拟合覆盖率，但非普遍性质。KAN族在验证损失上改善GELU MLP，但标准化基准中BLiMP准确率仅62.4-63.1%，EWoK随机水平，GR-KAN效果反转。大模型下MLPEdge弱于MLP，286M GR-KAN不及SwiGLU ClimbMix基线。

Conclusion: 小基KAN提供了实用的、可跨语料迁移的标量变换审计接口，但测试的替代方案在基准、质量或延迟上均未表现出对强MLP基线的一致优势。

Abstract: Kolmogorov--Arnold Networks (KANs) replace fixed node activations with learned one-dimensional edge functions, offering an explicit interface for interpretation and a possible alternative to transformer feed-forward networks. We test these claims separately. In a six-layer, 10M-parameter B-spline KAN, we reconstruct all 884,736 feed-forward edges: 87.8\% exceed (NLS>0.1) and 0.4\% are inactive. Pruning the lowest-activity 20--25\% causes negligible loss increase, although structured MLP neuron pruning tolerates comparable sparsity. The audit replicates on BabyLM, but grid-size sweeps show that near-total fPCA compression and high closed-form-fit coverage are properties of the low-capacity grid-2 basis, not universal KAN behavior. For replacement, we evaluate MLP, SwiGLU, grouped Chebyshev, and rational GR-KAN networks on BabyLM. The KAN-family and gated variants improve validation loss over the GELU MLP, but this ordering does not transfer to standardized benchmarks: across ten seeds and 59,875 BLiMP pairs, accuracies span 62.4--63.1\%, EWoK remains at chance, and a (+0.7)-point GR-KAN effect on BLiMP reverses on the supplement. Larger tests are also cautionary: parameter-matched MLPEdge underperforms the MLP on Wikitext-103, and 286M-parameter GR-KAN remains below a SwiGLU ClimbMix baseline after stabilization. Thus, small-basis KANs provide a practical, corpus-transferable interface for auditing learned scalar transformations, but the tested replacements show no consistent benchmark, quality, or latency advantage over strong MLP baselines.

</details>


### [129] [Publicly-Verifiable Certificates for Statistical Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15528)
*Michael Ngo,Michael P. Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文定义了非交互式学习证明的新概念：公开可验证的统计有效性证书（pvCSV），允许学习者发布假设和证书，任何用户可根据自身分布高效验证假设的有效性。针对自适应统计查询（SQ）算法，构造了样本复杂度为O(log k)的pvCSV，而最佳学习算法的样本复杂度为Ω̃(√k)。该工作展示了SQ模型中证明系统的优势与局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 继Goldwasser等人提出交互式学习证明框架后，本文旨在探索非交互式学习证明的可能性，以实现更公开、分布鲁棒的验证机制。

Method: 定义了公开可验证的统计有效性证书（pvCSV），并针对自适应统计查询（SQ）算法构造了具体的pvCSV方案，其证书样本复杂度为O(log k)，远低于学习算法本身的Ω̃(√k)。

Result: 成功构造了样本复杂度仅对数增长的pvCSV，证明了非交互式学习证明的可行性，并揭示了SQ模型中证明系统的能力与界限。

Conclusion: 本文首次系统研究了非交互式学习证明，为SQ模型下的学习验证提供了高效工具，同时指出了该方向的未来挑战。

Abstract: Following Goldwasser, Rothblum, Shafer, and Yehudayoff, who defined a framework for interactive proofs of learning [ITCS'21], we initiate the study of non-interactive proofs of learning. We define and study a new notion: Publicly-Verifiable Certificates of Statistical Validity (pvCSVs), which allow for public, distributionally-robust certification that the result of a learning algorithm is valid. In a pvCSV, a learner publishes a hypothesis $h$ and corresponding certificate $π$; then, any user, who holds a user-specific distribution, can read the pair $(h,π)$ and determine efficiently whether the hypothesis is valid according to the user-specific distribution.
  We construct pvCSVs in the context of Adaptive Statistical Query (SQ) Algorithms. To certify SQ algorithms that makes $k$ adaptive queries, we construct pvCSVs where the sample complexity scales with $O(\log k)$, whereas the sample complexity of the best learning algorithms scale with $\tilde{O}(\sqrt{k})$. More generally, we study proof systems for learning in the SQ model, demonstrating the model's strengths as well as its limitations.

</details>


### [130] [From Feasibility to Desirability: Plan, Learn, Adapt (PLA) Framework for Personalized On-Device Itinerary Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15552)
*Himel Dev,Tanmoy Sen,Madhusudan Basak,Bashima Islam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 PLA（Plan, Learn, Adapt）三阶段框架，用于在移动设备上生成个性化旅行行程。该框架通过异构轻量级规划器集合生成可行候选方案，利用配对比较学习 Bradley-Terry 奖励模型捕捉行程级偏好，并在设备感知计算预算内进行保持可行性的局部优化。在人机比较中达到 67.8% 胜率、100% 可行性，在生产部署中行程完成率提升 91%，平均延迟 109.9 ms。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个性化旅行行程生成面临硬组合可行性与软潜在合意性之间的张力。经典优化方法能保证可行性但无法捕捉主观偏好，学习方法能建模偏好但无法保证可行性，且移动端部署对两者施加额外资源约束。

Method: PLA 三阶段框架：Plan 阶段构建异构轻量级规划器集成，生成结构多样的可行候选行程；Learn 阶段通过配对行程比较拟合紧凑的 Bradley-Terry 奖励模型，捕获步调、地理连贯性、日间平衡等涌现属性；Adapt 阶段在设备感知计算预算内进行保持可行性的局部精炼，确保中间状态始终可行。

Result: 在超过 100 个美国城市的 2,519 对人机比较中，奖励引导集成达到 67.8% 胜率，比最佳单一规划器高 11.2 个百分点，可行性 100%；GPT-5、Claude Opus 4.5、Gemini 3 Pro 等前沿 LLM 在相同约束下可行性为 0%；奖励模型在留一城市交叉验证中平均准确率 67.6%；在 FlyEnJoy 生产部署中，行程完成率提升 91%，平均设备端延迟 109.9 ms。

Conclusion: PLA 框架通过将可行性约束与学习型偏好建模分离并有机结合，有效解决了个性化行程生成中可行性与合意性的矛盾，实现了高可行性、高偏好匹配和低延迟的移动端部署，具有良好的泛化能力和实际应用价值。

Abstract: Generating personalized trip itineraries is a complex planning task and involves a tension between hard combinatorial feasibility and soft latent desirability. Classical optimization enforces constraints but fails to capture subjective traveler preferences. While learning-based approaches model preferences, they cannot guarantee feasibility. Mobile deployment imposes additional resource constraints on both. To address this, we propose Plan, Learn, Adapt (PLA), a three-stage framework for personalized on-device itinerary generation. The Plan stage builds a heterogeneous ensemble of lightweight planners that produces structurally diverse feasible candidates. From pairwise itinerary comparisons, Learn fits a compact Bradley-Terry reward model that captures emergent schedule properties such as pacing, geographic coherence, and day balance, which per-POI signals miss. Finally, Adapt applies feasibility-preserving local refinement within a device-aware compute budget; every intermediate state is feasible by construction. On 2,519 pairwise human comparisons across more than 100 U.S. cities, the reward-guided ensemble achieves a 67.8% win rate, 11.2 percentage points above the best single planner, with 100% feasibility. Three frontier LLMs, GPT-5, Claude Opus 4.5, and Gemini 3 Pro, achieve 0% feasibility under the same constraints. The reward model generalizes across held-out cities, with a 67.6% mean leave-one-city-out accuracy. In production deployment within FlyEnJoy, PLA increased itinerary completion rates by 91%, with 109.9 ms average on-device latency.

</details>


### [131] [Hard Rules, Soft Preferences: Bridging Reasoning, Learning, and Optimization for Personalized Packing Checklist Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15562)
*Himel Dev,Madhusudan Basak,Tanmoy Sen,Paromita Shome,Bashima Islam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种推理引导的学习框架，用于航空旅行行李打包的个性化清单生成。该框架包括符号引擎、两阶段偏好学习器和CP-SAT优化器，在604个标注行程场景中取得了99.7%召回率、0.96规则有效性、0.943 AUC-ROC、0.923 NDCG@5和100%约束满足，并在实际iOS旅行应用中使清单完成率翻倍、编辑和完成时间减少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的行李打包助手要么是模板驱动且通用的，要么是推荐驱动但无约束的，用户需要手动修补违反规定和容量限制的问题。航空旅行打包需要个性化、上下文感知的清单，同时必须满足安全规则、物品依赖和行李限额，这是一个错误率高且重复性的任务。

Method: 提出了一个三阶段推理引导学习框架：(1) 符号引擎生成具有显式依赖结构的规则感知种子清单；(2) 两阶段偏好学习器从用户的添加/删除操作中估计包含效用和优先效用，同时缓解生存偏差；(3) CP-SAT优化器选择紧凑且合规的子集。该架构实现了约束个性化的一般模式，适用于硬可行性约束与稀疏偏好信号共存场景。

Result: 在604个标注行程场景（包含29K包含标签和343K成对比较）上，符号引擎达到99.7%召回率和0.96规则有效性（前沿LLM为0.78–0.81）。梯度提升树和LambdaMART达到0.943 AUC-ROC和0.923 NDCG@5。CP-SAT达到100%约束满足（贪心选择28%，随机选择10%）。在FlyEnJoy生产级iOS旅行应用中部署后，清单完成率翻倍，编辑和完成时间减少。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了如何通过符号推理与偏好学习相结合，在严格的硬约束下实现有效的个性化。框架在航空旅行打包场景中取得了显著改进，并具有适用于其他约束个性化问题的通用性。

Abstract: Packing for air travel is recurring and error-prone: the checklist must be personal and context-aware, yet feasible under safety rules, item dependencies, and luggage limits. Existing packing assistants are template-driven and generic, or recommendation-driven but unconstrained, leaving users to manually patch regulatory and capacity violations. We propose a reasoning-guided learning framework with three stages: (1) a symbolic engine that generates a regulation-aware seed checklist with explicit dependency structure, (2) a two-stage preference learner that estimates inclusion and priority utilities from user add and remove actions while mitigating survivorship bias, and (3) a CP-SAT optimizer that selects a compact, compliant subset. The architecture instantiates a general pattern for constrained personalization, applicable wherever hard feasibility coexists with sparse preference signals. On 604 labeled trip scenarios, comprising 29K inclusion labels and 343K pairwise comparisons, the symbolic engine attains 99.7% recall and 0.96 rubric validity, compared with 0.78 to 0.81 for frontier LLMs. Gradient-boosted trees and LambdaMART reach an AUC-ROC of 0.943 and an NDCG@5 of 0.923. CP-SAT attains 100% constraint satisfaction, compared with 28% for greedy selection and 10% for random selection. Deployment in FlyEnJoy, a production iOS travel app, doubled checklist completions and reduced editing and completion time.

</details>


### [132] [Information-Directed Sampling for Causal Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15577)
*Muhammad Qasim Elahi,Murat Kocaoglu,Mahsa Ghasemi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究包含不可操控变量的上下文因果臂博弈问题，提出两种贝叶斯算法（汤普森采样和信息导向采样），证明了亚线性遗憾界，并在实验中优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在许多实际应用中，部分变量虽影响奖励且提供因果信息，但无法直接操控。现有因果臂博弈方法通常假设所有变量均可干预，忽略了这一重要场景。

Method: 采用已知因果图（无隐混淆）的贝叶斯框架，将观测分布的CPT作为未知参数；提出因果汤普森采样和因果信息导向采样两种算法，其中IDS算法给出了蒙特卡洛近似的误差上界。

Result: 为汤普森采样建立了熵依赖的亚线性贝叶斯遗憾界；为IDS推导了熵依赖的遗憾界，在精确计算时恢复标准亚线性IDS速率，并给出了蒙特卡洛估计的高概率置信界。

Conclusion: 在多个合成因果臂博弈任务中，所提方法通过更有效地利用跨干预共享的信息，优于因果和非因果基线方法。

Abstract: Causal bandits exploit structural relationships among variables to share information across interventions and accelerate the identification of high-reward decisions. In many applications, however, some variables cannot be directly manipulated, even though they influence the reward and provide useful information about the underlying causal system. We study contextual causal bandits with non-manipulable variables, where context variables are observed before action selection and additional variables are observed after each intervention. Assuming a known causal graph without latent confounding, we adopt a Bayesian formulation in which the conditional probability tables of the observational distribution constitute the unknown parameter. This representation allows observations collected under one intervention to update reward estimates for other interventions through their shared causal mechanisms. We develop causal variants of Thompson Sampling and Information-Directed Sampling (IDS) for this setting. For Thompson Sampling, we establish an entropy-dependent sublinear Bayesian regret bound. For IDS, we derive an entropy-dependent regret bound that explicitly quantifies the additional error introduced by Monte Carlo approximation of the expected regret and information gain; when these quantities are available exactly, the bound recovers the standard sublinear IDS rate. We further provide high-probability confidence bounds for the Monte Carlo estimates used by the algorithm. Experiments on several synthetic causal bandit tasks show that the proposed methods outperform causal and non-causal baselines by more effectively exploiting information shared across interventions.

</details>


### [133] [Rethinking Transfer in Continual Learning: A Replay-Based Realisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15587)
*Yang Meng,Zhenya Liu,Zhuokai Zhao,Yuxin Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出转移选择性回放（TSR）方法，通过三个可测条件判断何时存在前向迁移，并选择有益的回放数据而非无差别回放，在低预算持续学习场景下显著提升前向迁移同时保持稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有持续学习方法几乎只关注防止灾难性遗忘，而前向迁移（过去帮助未来）主要通过参数重用实现，没有明确解释何时应该期望迁移。本文在设计迁移机制之前先问：迁移何时存在？

Method: 提出三个可测量条件：目标任务必须有超越自身有限监督的改进空间、可迁移信息必须在持续优化中幸存、回放必须来自兼容的先前任务。据此实现TSR，通过零训练任务签名选择对当前任务有益的回放数据，并用蒸馏保持先前任务的稳定性。

Result: 在标准持续学习协议的低预算场景下，TSR一致改善前向迁移同时保持稳定性，在异构和同构任务流中均优于现有回放基线。

Conclusion: 前向迁移应作为持续学习的一阶目标，在工程设计之前应先理解迁移何时存在。

Abstract: Continual learning studies how deployed language models can continually acquire new tasks without expensive retraining from scratch. Existing methods, whether rehearsal-based (replaying stored past data) or rehearsal-free (regularising or isolating parameters), overwhelmingly target one objective: preventing catastrophic forgetting. Forward transfer, the past helping the future, has meanwhile been pursued almost exclusively through parameter reuse, with no explicit account of when transfer should be expected at all. We begin one step earlier: before designing a transfer mechanism, we ask when transfer should exist at all.
