We answer with a framework of three measurable conditions: the target task must leave room for improvement beyond its own limited supervision, transferable information must survive continued optimisation, and replay must come from compatible previous tasks. We instantiate this view as Transfer-Selective Replay (TSR), which selects replay data predicted to benefit the incoming task rather than replaying past examples indiscriminately. Selection is guided by a zero-training task signature, while distillation preserves stability on previous tasks. Under the standard continual learning protocol in the low-budget regime, TSR consistently improves forward transfer while maintaining stability, outperforming existing replay baselines across heterogeneous and homogeneous task streams. More broadly, the results argue for treating transfer as a first-class objective of continual learning, to be understood before it is engineered.

</details>


### [134] [Field-Aware RankMixer with Dual-Stream Bilinear Fusion for the Tencent UNI-REC Challenge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15590)
*Yufeng Zhang,Zhengqi Xu,Jiajun Cui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对KDD Cup 2026腾讯UNIREC挑战赛，提出了一种面向域感知的RankMixer（FA-RankMixer）模型，采用双流双线性融合架构，结合目标感知DIN模块、RankMixer跨token交互和分组双线性融合，在多个行为域上实现用户兴趣建模与目标广告pCVR预测，最终获得官方排行榜第九名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KDD Cup 2026腾讯UNIREC挑战赛要求对多域用户行为序列和非序列多字段特征进行联合建模，实现目标广告的转化率（pCVR）预测。现有方法难以有效融合异构的多域行为信息与结构化特征。

Method: 提出FA-RankMixer模型：1）使用目标感知DIN模块从多个行为域提取用户兴趣，并对最长行为序列分别建模近期和早期兴趣；2）基于特征字段和行为域构建语义token，通过RankMixer块进行跨token交互；3）设计浅层MLP流与深层RankMixer流构成双流架构，通过分组双线性模块融合两流表征。

Result: 在KDD Cup 2026腾讯UNIREC挑战赛官方排行榜上排名第九。

Conclusion: FA-RankMixer通过双流双线性融合架构有效解决了多域行为序列与非序列多字段特征的联合建模问题，验证了RankMixer在跨域用户行为建模中的有效性。

Abstract: This paper presents our solution to the KDD Cup 2026 Tencent UNIREC Challenge. The task requires joint modeling of multi-domain user behavior sequences and non-sequential multi-field features for target-ad pCVR prediction. We develop a Field-Aware RankMixer (FA-RankMixer) with dual-stream bilinear fusion. The model first applies target-aware DIN modules to extract user interests from multiple behavior domains. It also models recent and earlier interests separately for the longest behavior sequence. The model then forms semantic tokens based on feature fields and behavior domains and uses RankMixer blocks for cross-token interaction. A shallow MLP stream complements the deep RankMixer stream, and a group-wise bilinear module fuses their representations. Our final solution ranks ninth on the official leaderboard. Our code is available at https://github.com/PixelCookie-zyf/TAAC-2026-SeRankMixer.

</details>


### [135] [Do Generative Models Keep Time? A Time-Aware Evaluation of Synthetic Sequential Tabular Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15606)
*Kiwan Kwon,Kangmin Kim,Hojin Lee,Yeseong Jung,Hyeongwoo Kong,Vamsi K. Potluru,Saerom Park,Yongjae Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对合成时序表格数据，现有评估方法仅关注静态分布而忽略时间维度上的错误（如时间倒流、重复、异常轨迹）。本文提出基于数据属性分类的时序保真度评估协议，通过四个数据属性决定评估维度，测量时间戳有效性、跨截面结构、实体动态和时变关系，并将效用与隐私评估从单行扩展到轨迹。在13个数据集8个模型上的实验表明，传统评估与时间评估结果差异显著，且错误具有架构一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有合成时序表格数据评估仅基于静态分布，无法检测时间倒流、重复、实体从未经历的异常路径等时间维度错误，缺乏衡量时序保真度的系统性方法。

Method: 提出基于分类学的评估协议：先按四个属性（时间表示方式、采样是否规则、轨迹是否相互依赖、模式如何链接实体与历史）对数据集分类，由属性决定可用的评估维度。协议测量：1) 时间戳有效性；2) 对齐时间点的截面结构；3) 实体内部动态；4) 时变关系结构。将效用和隐私评估从隔离行扩展到轨迹层面。

Result: 在13个数据集、8种生成模型上的实验显示：传统评估排名与时间评估排名存在显著不一致；时间维度上的错误并非随机，而是与模型架构相关（具有架构一致性）。

Conclusion: 时序保真度必须在时间轴上直接测量，而不能从聚合的记录分布中推断。传统静态评估不足以捕捉合成时序表格数据的时间维度缺陷。

Abstract: Synthetic sequential tabular data are increasingly used for privacy-preserving data sharing, yet a generator can reproduce every marginal and every foreign-key relationship while emitting timestamps that run backwards or repeat, and while sending entities along paths that no real entity followed. Conventional tabular evaluation, which pools records into static distributions, is blind to such failures. We present a taxonomy-guided evaluation protocol for temporal fidelity, in which the applicable measurements are determined by the data rather than fixed in advance. Each dataset is first characterized along four properties: how time is represented, whether observations are regularly sampled, whether trajectories are mutually dependent, and how the schema links entities to their histories. These properties determine which evaluation dimensions are meaningful. The protocol then measures timestamp validity, cross-sectional structure at aligned time points, within-entity dynamics, and time-varying relational structure, and recasts utility and privacy evaluation over trajectories rather than isolated rows. We apply the protocol to eight generative models across thirteen datasets spanning six domains. Rankings under conventional evaluation disagree substantially with those obtained under temporal evaluation, and the resulting failures are architecture-coherent rather than random. Temporal fidelity must therefore be measured on the time axis itself, rather than inferred from pooled record distributions.

</details>


### [136] [ASK-NN: An Asymmetric Nearest-Neighbor Test that detects Distribution Drifts in Natural Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15607)
*Sergey Zakharov,Rodion Oblovatny,Alexey Zaytsev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于有向k近邻图的不对称两样本检验方法ASK-NN，用于检测LLM生成文本中的幻觉和人工痕迹，通过统计参考样本中最近邻仍为参考点的数量来判断分布偏移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM生成文本中的幻觉和人工痕迹表现为提示（参考样本）与响应（查询样本）隐状态分布之间的偏移，且两者长度不对称，传统对称检验方法不适用，需要一种非对称的检验统计量。

Method: 提出ASK-NN方法：基于有向k近邻图构建统计量，统计参考样本中其最近邻（在合并样本中）仍是参考样本的点数。在置换零假设下推导了精确有限样本条件均值和方差，并建立了渐近正态性和固定备择假设下的一致性。

Result: 在合成基准、人工文本检测以及基于token级隐状态的LLM幻觉检测任务上，ASK-NN与基于核和图的方法相比具有竞争力。

Conclusion: ASK-NN是一种计算高效、易于实现的非对称两样本检验方法，在检测LLM生成文本偏差和幻觉方面表现优异。

Abstract: Hallucinations and artificial text in LLM-generated outputs often appear as distributional deviations between prompt and response hidden-state distributions. Since prompts or retrieved contexts typically serve as reference samples and responses as query samples, with major differences in length, these asymmetries motivate the use of change test statistics that treat the two samples differently. We consider an asymmetric two-sample test ASK-NN based on the directed k-nearest-neighbor graph. Our statistic counts reference points whose nearest neighbor in the pooled sample is also a reference point. Under the permutation null, it admits an exact finite-sample conditional mean and variance; we further establish asymptotic normality and consistency under fixed alternatives. ASK-NN is computationally effective and easy to implement. Empirically, it is competitive with kernel and graph-based baselines on synthetic benchmarks, artificial-text detection, and LLM hallucination detection from token-level hidden states.

</details>


### [137] [Neural Non-Equilibrium Hamiltonian Monte Carlo for Corrected Boltzmann Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15682)
*Moxian Qian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Neural Non-Equilibrium Hamiltonian Monte Carlo (NHMC) is a train-then-correct learned sampler that uses stochastic Hamiltonian-style paths to sample from unnormalized Boltzmann densities, with statistical correction via non-equilibrium work, enabling importance sampling, free-energy estimation, and Metropolis-Hastings correction on path space and configuration space.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Sampling from unnormalized Boltzmann densities (e.g., in statistical physics, molecular dynamics) requires proposals that globally move probability mass while retaining path-probability information for correction. Traditional methods like HMC can be inefficient; learned samplers can improve mixing but need to maintain correct statistics. The paper aims to develop a flexible, trainable sampler that provides statistically correct samples.

Method: NHMC learns stochastic Hamiltonian-style paths from a tractable base distribution to the target via neural networks. After training, the proposal generates paths and endpoints; the recorded non-equilibrium work (probability ratio of forward and reverse paths) is used for statistical correction: minimizing its mean during training reduces path-space KL divergence; during evaluation, it defines weights for self-normalized importance sampling (path-SNIS), estimates normalizing constants/free-energy differences, and gives acceptance ratio for path-space independent Metropolis-Hastings (path-IMH). A shared-bridge round-trip Metropolis kernel acting directly on configurations is also defined.

Result: On double-well and finite-volume lattice φ⁴ targets, NHMC gives corrected estimates when path overlap is sufficient; poor overlap leads to weight degeneracy, low acceptance, and long autocorrelation. A molecular internal-coordinate feasibility study using an MD prior and learned-force path proposal is additionally reported.

Conclusion: NHMC provides a principled framework for learned Hamiltonian sampling with statistical guarantees, but its success depends on sufficient path overlap between forward and reverse proposals; failure modes like weight degeneracy and low acceptance indicate proposal mismatch. The approach opens possibilities for leveraging neural networks to accelerate sampling in complex systems.

Abstract: Sampling from an unnormalized Boltzmann density requires proposals that move probability mass globally while retaining enough path-probability information for statistical correction. We introduce Neural Non-Equilibrium Hamiltonian Monte Carlo (NHMC), a train-then-correct learned Hamiltonian sampler. Starting from a tractable base distribution, NHMC learns stochastic Hamiltonian-style paths toward the target. Once training is complete, the learned proposal parameters are fixed; the proposal then generates complete paths and endpoint configurations, which are statistically corrected using the recorded non-equilibrium work. This dimensionless generalized work is determined by the probability ratio between the forward proposal path and a reverse reference path. During training, minimizing its mean reduces a path-space KL divergence and controls an upper bound on endpoint mismatch. During evaluation, the same quantity defines weights for self-normalized importance sampling on paths (path-SNIS), estimates normalizing constants or free-energy differences, and gives the acceptance ratio for path-space independent Metropolis-Hastings (path-IMH). The same forward-reverse laws also define a shared-bridge round-trip Metropolis kernel that acts directly on configurations and preserves the Boltzmann target. On double-well and finite-volume lattice $φ^4$ targets, the NHMC construction gives corrected estimates when path overlap is sufficient; when overlap is poor, weight degeneracy, low acceptance, and long autocorrelation expose proposal failure. We additionally report a molecular internal-coordinate feasibility study using an MD prior and learned-force path proposal.

</details>


### [138] [Understanding Reasoning from Pretraining to Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16097)
*Jingyan Shen,Ang Li,Salman Rahman,Yifan Sun,Micah Goldblum,Matus Telgarsky,Pavel Izmailov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过国际象棋这一受控测试环境，系统研究了预训练（模型大小、数据）如何影响后续强化学习（RL）的收益，以及RL对模型的实际作用。研究发现，RL后的性能可由预训练损失准确预测，且RL奖励曲线的斜率与预训练token数近似线性增长。此外，RL并非简单锐化SFT策略：在简单谜题上它放大SFT已偏好的正确动作，在困难谜题上则浮现SFT几乎不存在的正确动作。这些规律在数学领域也得到验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准LLM设置中，预训练语料庞大且不可控，难以区分预训练与RL的贡献，且跨两阶段的系统计算扫描成本过高。因此，需要可控测试环境来研究预训练到RL的完整流程。

Method: 使用国际象棋作为受控测试床，遵循标准LLM训练流程：在人类棋谱上预训练语言模型（5M到1B参数），在合成推理轨迹上进行监督微调（SFT），然后在可验证奖励的象棋谜题上运行RL。

Result: （1）给定RL计算量下，RL后性能可由预训练损失良好预测；（2）RL奖励曲线的斜率随预训练token数近似线性提升；（3）RL并非简单锐化SFT策略：简单谜题放大SFT已偏好的正确动作，困难谜题浮现SFT几乎不存在的正确动作；（4）在数学领域1B模型上验证了相同预测模式。

Conclusion: 提供了预训练到RL接口的定量描述，并建立了一个跨完整预训练到后训练管道的推理科学受控测试床。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has become central to improving large language models (LLMs) on complex reasoning tasks, yet RL post-training is largely studied in isolation from the pretraining that precedes it. As a result, two basic questions remain open: (1) how do pretraining choices (model size, data) shape the returns to RL compute, and (2) what does RL actually do to the model? These questions are difficult to study in the standard LLM setting: pretraining corpora are vast and uncontrolled, making it hard to attribute behaviors to pretraining versus RL, and systematic compute sweeps across both stages are prohibitively expensive. To address these challenges, we use chess as a controlled testbed for studying reasoning across the full pretraining-to-post-training pipeline. We follow the standard LLM training pipeline by pretraining language models from 5M to 1B parameters on human chess games, supervised fine-tuning on synthetic reasoning traces, and running RL on chess puzzles with verifiable rewards. Using this framework, we find that the post-RL performance at given RL compute level is well-predicted from the pretraining loss, and slope of the RL reward curves improves approximately linearly with the pretraining tokens. Beyond scaling, we find that RL does not simply sharpen the SFT policy: on easy puzzles it amplifies correct moves the SFT policy already preferred, while on hard puzzles it surfaces correct moves that were nearly absent under SFT. We further test whether our findings transfer beyond chess by training a 1B language model on math-domain text, where the same predictive pattern emerges: longer-pretrained checkpoints reach higher post-RL performance and improve faster under RL. In sum, we provide a quantitative account of the pretraining-to-RL interface and a controlled testbed for studying the science of reasoning across the full pretraining-to-post-training pipeline.

</details>


### [139] [Toward Federated Multimodal Graph Foundation Models: A Topology-Aware Multimodal Alignment Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15687)
*Xunkai Li,Guohao Fu,Yuming Ai,Zhengyu Wu,Hongchao Qin,Rong-Hua Li,Guoren Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了 FedGAMMA，一个面向联邦多模态属性图的基础学习框架，将联邦多模态图学习视为两阶段语义-结构对齐问题（联邦预训练 + 提示微调），在 12 个数据集上超过多种基线，最多提升 12.96%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法仅覆盖多模态图学习或联邦学习的单一方面，无法同时处理隐私约束下的多模态图碎片化场景，缺乏一种能在不暴露原始数据的前提下进行跨客户端协作训练且具备强迁移能力的底层模型。

Method: 将联邦多模态图基础学习建模为两阶段语义-结构对齐问题：(1) 预训练阶段：共享-私有语义增强器通过最优传输解耦跨模态共性与模态特定信息；拓扑感知图融合模块通过语义残差图和双位置编码解耦语义与结构视图；双通道亲和感知聚合机制通过特征和图质心估计客户端相似性而不暴露原始数据。(2) 微调阶段：轻量级图感知提示、共享提示池与受控探索、通道级提示同步来适配预训练编码器。

Result: 在 12 个多模态图数据集上，FedGAMMA 在多项下游任务中持续超越广泛基线，最高提升 12.96%；在少样本学习场景下，跨多领域数据集的多项任务中最高提升 5.71%。

Conclusion: FedGAMMA 有效解决了联邦多模态图学习中的语义-结构对齐与数据隐私问题，通过两阶段框架实现了跨客户端可迁移的多模态图基础模型，实验证明了其在全监督和少样本场景下的优越性。

Abstract: Multimodal-attributed graphs (MAGs), whose nodes carry modalities such as images and text alongside topological structure, now pervade applications including social platforms, e-commerce, and biomedical networks, offering richer semantic signals than single-modality graphs. In practice, such graphs are fragmented across privacy-restricted silos owned by different platforms and institutions, so learning a broadly transferable model over them demands collaborative training that never exposes raw data. This places the task at the intersection of multimodal graph learning and federated learning, yet existing methods cover only one side of it. To address the challenges from these two perspectives, we propose FedGAMMA, casting federated multimodal graph foundation learning as a two-stage semantic-structural alignment problem of federated pre-training and prompt-based fine-tuning. During pre-training, a shared-private semantic enhancer disentangles cross-modal commonality from modality-specific information, aligning it through optimal transport, a topology-aware graph fusion module decouples semantic and structural views via semantic residual graphs and dual positional encodings, and a dual-channel affinity-aware aggregation mechanism estimates client similarity from feature and graph centroids without exposing raw data. During fine-tuning, FedGAMMA adapts the pretrained encoder through lightweight graph-aware prompts, a shared prompt pool with controlled exploration, and channel-wise prompt synchronization. Experiments on twelve multimodal graph datasets show FedGAMMA consistently surpassing a broad range of baselines across downstream tasks, with gains of up to 12.96%. FedGAMMA further outperforms competitive baselines accross multi-domain datasets on multiple tasks with up to 5.71% under few-shot learning scenario.

</details>


### [140] [A Benchmark for Electrical Load Forecasting Across Grid Levels: Time-Series Transformers Outperform Established Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15705)
*Matthias Hertel,Sebastian Pütz,Jonathan Kolar,Benjamin Schäfer,Ralf Mikut,Veit Hagenmeyer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个涵盖多级电网（输电系统运营商控制区域、低压电网馈线和单个终端消费者）的负荷预测基准，评估了十种短期负荷预测方法。结果表明，基于Transformer的方法始终优于传统方法，预测误差降低6.6-10.7%。作者开发了YAformer（一种灵活的Transformer架构）进行消融研究，但发现标准Transformer表现更优，说明架构修改并非必要。此外，Transformer基础模型Chronos-2在零样本场景下表现有竞争力，但在处理特殊事件时失败。研究强调了长输入上下文、协变量和持续重训练的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确的跨多级电网负荷预测对未来智能电网至关重要，但缺乏统一的基准来比较不同方法在不同网格层级上的表现。现有研究往往忽略长输入上下文、协变量和持续重训练等关键因素。本文旨在提供一个全面的基准，系统评估多种预测方法，并分析架构设计的影响。

Method: 构建了三个代表不同电网层级的数据集（TSO控制区域、低压电网馈线、单个终端消费者），评估了十种短期负荷预测方法。引入YAformer（一种灵活的Transformer架构，整合了先前工作中的多种改进），并通过超参数优化进行对比。同时评估了Transformer基础模型Chronos-2的零样本性能，并进行了详细的消融研究。

Result: Transformer方法一致优于传统方法，误差降低6.6-10.7%。标准Transformer表现超过YAformer，表明额外的架构修改不必要。Chronos-2在零样本下表现有竞争力，但未能准确捕捉TSO数据中的特殊事件。消融实验证实了长输入上下文、协变量和持续重训练的重要性。

Conclusion: 基于Transformer的方法在负荷预测中表现最优，但标准Transformer已足够，无需复杂架构修改。长输入上下文、协变量和持续重训练是提升预测性能的关键因素，常被文献忽视。Chronos-2展示了零样本预测的潜力，但在特定领域事件处理上存在局限。

Abstract: Accurate load forecasting at multiple grid levels is essential for future smart grids, ranging from aggregated control area forecasts for balancing supply and demand to forecasts of individual end-consumer loads for demand-side management and energy management systems. We present a comprehensive benchmark for load forecasting across grid levels, comprising three datasets that represent a transmission system operator control area, low-voltage grid feeders, and individual end consumers. We evaluate ten methods for short-term load forecasting and find that Transformer-based approaches consistently outperform established methods, reducing forecast error by 6.6-10.7 %. To analyze the impact of architectural design, we introduce YAformer, a flexible Transformer architecture that integrates modifications from prior work and is optimized via hyperparameter optimization. However, the standard Transformer achieves superior performance, suggesting that these architectural modifications are not required for accurate load forecasting. We further evaluate the Transformer-based time-series foundation model Chronos-2, which demonstrates competitive zero-shot performance on two datasets but fails to accurately capture special events in the TSO data. Detailed analyses reveal model-specific strengths and weaknesses, and ablation studies highlight the importance of long input contexts, covariates and continuous retraining - aspects that are often overlooked in the time-series forecasting literature.

</details>


### [141] [CardioMeta: Calibrated Multi-Task Prediction of Diabetes, Hypertension, and Cardiovascular Disease Across Population and EHR Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15721)
*S M Asif Hossain,Ruksat Khan Shayoni,M. F. Mridha,Jungpil Shin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CardioMeta是一个多任务校准框架，用于联合预测糖尿病、高血压和心血管疾病，在NHANES和MIMIC-IV数据集上验证，重点解决标签泄露、校准可靠性、时间稳健性、外部可迁移性和子组可靠性问题。在去泄露的时间验证中达到Macro-AUROC 0.839，校准误差0.024，外部迁移时性能下降但微调可部分恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心血管代谢疾病（糖尿病、高血压、心血管疾病）常共同发生且共享代谢、血管、人口学和行为决定因素，是主要可预防发病原因。现有机器学习研究常侧重单一数据集的区分度，忽视标签泄露、概率校准、时间稳健性、外部可迁移性和子组可靠性等问题。

Method: 提出CardioMeta框架：一个共享的心血管代谢编码器+疾病特异性的门控头（gated heads）进行多任务联合预测，后接事后概率校准（post-hoc probability calibration）。为减少循环标签重构，主要分析中排除疾病定义变量，仅在全临床特征敏感性分析中保留。使用NHANES进行群体层面的模型开发和时序验证，MIMIC-IV进行EHR领域评估（存在显著分布偏移）。

Result: 在去泄露的时序验证中：Macro-AUROC 0.839，Macro-AUPRC 0.536，Macro-F1 0.614，期望校准误差（ECE）0.024，较梯度提升和神经表格基线有适度但一致的提升。在MIMIC-IV的外部评估中性能明显下降，但有限微调可部分恢复。

Conclusion: 多任务心血管代谢建模的核心价值不在于提高准确率，而在于可重复的泄露控制、校准概率输出以及跨异构医疗数据源的透明可靠性报告。

Abstract: Cardiometabolic diseases remain among the most persistent drivers of preventable morbidity because diabetes, hypertension, and cardiovascular disease frequently co-occur and share metabolic, vascular, demographic, and behavioral determinants. Existing machine learning studies for chronic disease prediction often emphasize discrimination on a single dataset, while underreporting label leakage, calibration, temporal robustness, external transportability, and subgroup reliability. This paper presents CardioMeta, a calibrated multi-task framework for joint prediction of diabetes, hypertension, and cardiovascular disease across population survey and electronic health record (EHR) data. The study uses NHANES for population-level model development and temporal validation, and MIMIC-IV for EHR-domain evaluation under substantial distribution shift. To reduce circular label reconstruction, the primary analysis excludes disease-defining variables from the corresponding prediction heads, while a full-clinical feature setting is retained only as sensitivity analysis. CardioMeta combines a shared cardiometabolic encoder with disease-specific gated heads and post-hoc probability calibration. In the leakage-reduced temporal validation setting, the model achieved a macro-AUROC of 0.839, macro-AUPRC of 0.536, macro-F1 of 0.614, and expected calibration error of 0.024, with modest but consistent improvements over strong gradient-boosting and neural tabular baselines. External evaluation on MIMIC-IV showed clear degradation under domain shift, while limited fine-tuning partially recovered performance. The findings indicate that the principal value of multi-task cardiometabolic modeling lies not in inflated accuracy, but in reproducible leakage control, calibrated probabilities, and transparent reliability reporting across heterogeneous healthcare data sources.

</details>


### [142] [Learning Faster without Deeper Networks: A*-Inspired Batch Selection for Efficient CNN Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15745)
*Anxhelo Shehu,Enes Stastoli,Arben Cela*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种受A*搜索启发的批次选择策略（A*-BS），通过将小批量调度视为启发式搜索问题，利用损失难度和重用惩罚来排序批次，从而在无需修改网络结构或优化器的情况下提升训练效率和模型性能。在MedMNIST-v2基准上，使用约22.5万参数的轻量级CNN配合A*-BS，在半数任务上超越了ResNet-18和ResNet-50，相对提升高达15%，且训练速度更快。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统CNN训练使用随机打乱的小批量，存在收敛慢和学习信号衰减的问题（大量样本很快被分类为简单样本）。

Method: 提出A*-Inspired Batch Selection (A*-BS)，将小批量调度视为启发式搜索问题，每个批次作为搜索空间中的节点，使用类似A*的分数（结合基于损失的难度度量与重用惩罚）进行排序，鼓励信息丰富的梯度更新和批次多样性，无需修改网络架构或优化算法。

Result: 在MedMNIST-v2的12个2D分类任务上，使用约22.5万参数的轻量级CNN配合A*-BS，在半数任务上达到比ResNet-18和ResNet-50更高的准确率和AUC，相对提升高达15%。消融实验显示在所有12个任务上超越随机批次打乱。壁钟测量表明轻量级CNN配合A*-BS训练速度显著快于ResNet-18和ResNet-50。

Conclusion: 智能批次排序可以部分补偿架构复杂性的降低，为深度模型提供计算高效的替代方案，即使在面对更深、更复杂的架构时也能保持可靠性。

Abstract: Common practice when training Convolutional Neural Networks (CNNs) is to use randomly shuffled mini-batches. This creates two limitations: slower convergence, and a diminishing learning signal, since many samples are quickly classified as easy during training. We address these inefficiencies with A*-Inspired Batch Selection (A*-BS), a lightweight, model-agnostic strategy that formulates mini-batch scheduling as a heuristic search problem. Each batch is treated as a node in a search space and ranked using an A*-like score combining a loss-based difficulty measure with a reuse penalty. This encourages informative gradient updates and batch diversity throughout training, without modifying network architectures or optimization algorithms, so it integrates seamlessly into existing pipelines. We evaluate A*-BS on the twelve 2D classification tasks of the MedMNIST-v2 benchmark, using a deliberately simple architecture of approximately 2.25x10^5 parameters, compared against the ResNet-18 and ResNet-50 baselines reported by the benchmark. On half of these tasks, the lightweight model with A*-BS reaches higher accuracy and AUC than both ResNet baselines, with relative gains of up to 15%. An ablation under identical architecture and hyperparameters shows A*-BS outperforms random batch shuffling on all twelve tasks. Wall-clock measurements further show the lightweight CNN with A*-BS trains substantially faster than ResNet-18 and ResNet-50 on identical hardware. These results indicate that intelligent batch ordering can partially compensate for reduced architectural complexity, offering a computationally efficient alternative to deeper models, with reliability reinforced by strong performance even against deeper, more sophisticated architectures.

</details>


### [143] [Trainable Spline Representations for Physics-Informed Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15751)
*Giovanni Canali,Nicola Demo,Gianluigi Rozza*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出物理信息样条（PI-Splines），用张量积B样条展开替代神经网络参数化微分方程解，在保持残差训练范式的同时提供紧支撑、显式光滑控制、解析导数和参数几何解释，在多个基准问题上表现出竞争力和稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有物理信息神经网络（PINNs）使用神经网络作为解的表达形式，存在训练不稳定、缺乏可解释性、边界条件难以精确施加等问题，需要一种结构化的替代方案。

Method: 采用张量积B样条展开参数化未知场，将样条控制系数作为可训练参数，保留残差损失训练范式；通过固定边界控制系数强加边界条件；在所有对比实验中保持控制方程、配置点、损失项和优化过程一致，仅改变近似架构。

Result: 在多个难度递增的基准问题上，PI-Splines与标准物理信息框架相比表现出竞争性和稳定性，尤其在需要结构化表示、局部性和参数效率的场景中优势明显。

Conclusion: PI-Splines是神经网络物理信息架构的有效替代方案，提供紧支撑、显式光滑控制、解析导数和参数可解释性，边界条件可通过固定控制系数强加。

Abstract: This work introduces Physics-Informed Splines (PI-Splines), a structured spline-based architecture for physics-informed learning. Instead of representing the solution of a differential equation with a neural network, PI-Splines directly parametrize the unknown field through a tensor-product B-spline expansion with trainable control coefficients. This formulation preserves the residual-based training paradigm of Physics-Informed Neural Networks while providing compact support, explicit smoothness control, analytical derivatives, and a direct geometric interpretation of the trainable parameters. When compatible with the spline representation, boundary conditions can be imposed strongly by fixing suitable boundary control coefficients. The proposed method is evaluated on several benchmark problems of increasing difficulty and compared with standard physics-informed frameworks under matched governing equations, collocation sets, loss terms, and optimization procedures, so as to isolate the effect of the approximation architecture. Numerical experiments show that PI-Splines provide a competitive and stable alternative to neural physics-informed architectures, particularly in settings where structured representations, locality, and parameter efficiency are desirable.

</details>


### [144] [CoG-Guided Weight Correction for Fault-Tolerant Deep Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15753)
*Bahram Parchekani,Samira Nazari,Ali Azarpeyvand,Mohammad Hasan Ahmadilivani,Tara Ghasempouri,Jaan Raik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于重心（CoG）引导的权重校正方法，利用层内权重的空间特征检测和修复硬件故障导致的权重错误，无需重训练或架构修改。在LSTM和CNN网络上的实验表明，该方法在10^{-3} BER下实现了高达230倍（StageNet）和49.55倍（ResNet-18）的容错提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全关键应用中使用的深度神经网络易受硬件和内存故障影响，导致权重损坏和可靠性下降，现有方法通常需要重训练或架构修改，效率受限。

Method: 提出重心（CoG）引导的权重校正方法，基于每层内权重的空间特征，利用距离感知的校正规则检测和修复故障权重，无需重训练或架构修改。

Result: 在LSTM网络（StageNet、MTFNet）上，BER=10^{-3}时容错提升分别达230x和6.41x；在CNN网络（ResNet-18、VGG-16）上，容错提升分别达49.55x和20.79x，且精度损失可忽略。

Conclusion: 首次将重心（CoG）概念应用于神经网络权重张量以增强模型可靠性，验证了该方法在多种网络架构上的有效性和通用性。

Abstract: Deep Neural Networks (DNNs) used in safety-critical applications are vulnerable to hardware and memory faults that corrupt network weights and degrade reliability. In this paper, we propose a Center of Gravity (CoG) guided weight correction method that restores faulty weights based on their spatial characteristics within each layer. The proposed approach detects and corrects weight faults using distance-aware correction rules, eliminating the need for retraining or architectural modification. The effectiveness of the proposed method in terms of the capability of tolerating hardware faults has been evaluated through performing fault injection at different Bit Error Rates (BERs).
  Experiments on safety-critical LSTM-based Networks, including StageNet for disease progression tracking and MTFNet for cardiac anomaly detection, demonstrate fault tolerance improvements of up to 230x and 6.41x, respectively, at a BER of 10^{-3}, with negligible accuracy loss. When extended to Convolutional Neural Networks (CNNs), the method achieves up to 49.55x and 20.79x improvements under comparable fault conditions on ResNet-18 and VGG-16, respectively. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first work to apply the CoG concept to neural network weight tensors for enhancing model reliability.

</details>


### [145] [From Diffusion to Reaction-Diffusion: A Dynamical-Systems View of Oversmoothing in Hypergraph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15773)
*Zhiheng Zhou,Mengyao Zhou,Yancheng Chen,Dengyi Zhao,Xingqin Qi,Guiying Yan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从动力系统视角研究超图神经网络中的过平滑问题，提出一种反应-扩散框架（HNRD），通过引入反应机制补偿扩散导致的能量耗散，确保深层传播中表示不坍塌，并在多个数据集上取得优于基线模型的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高阶耦合增强了超图神经网络的表达能力，但也因强多路特征混合导致深层传播中的表示坍塌（过平滑）。现有方法缺乏对超图过平滑的系统性分析，本文旨在从动力系统角度理解该现象并设计深度鲁棒的超图学习框架。

Method: 定义超图梯度与散度算子，将消息传递建模为关联层面的扩散过程；分析纯扩散的连续半流会导致零模无关分量指数收缩、Dirichlet能量归零，从而揭示过平滑本质；在此基础上提出超图神经反应-扩散（HNRD），在横向分量上引入反应机制以补偿扩散耗散，保持判别性变化；证明动力学的全局适定性和零模无关Dirichlet能量有正下界；采用前向欧拉离散化得到实用HNRD层并给出稳定性条件。

Result: 在基准和合成异配超图上的实验表明，HNRD一致优于代表性超图基线；深度、鲁棒性和效率分析显示HNRD在深层传播和扰动下能保持稳定性能和非零Dirichlet能量。

Conclusion: 本文提供了一个 principled 的动力框架，用于设计既能保持高阶表达能力又避免表示坍塌的深度超图架构。

Abstract: Higher-order couplings enhance the expressive power of hypergraph neural networks (HGNNs), but they also intensify representation collapse in deep propagation due to strong multi-way feature mixing. This work investigates hypergraph oversmoothing from a dynamical-systems perspective and develops a reaction--diffusion framework for depth-resistant hypergraph learning. By defining hypergraph gradient and divergence operators, we interpret message passing as an incidence-level diffusion process. The analysis of pure diffusion shows that its continuous semiflow exponentially contracts the null-mode-free component of node representations and drives the Dirichlet energy to zero, revealing hypergraph oversmoothing as an intrinsic transverse-energy dissipation phenomenon. Motivated by this analysis, we propose Hypergraph Neural Reaction--Diffusion (HNRD), which introduces a reaction mechanism acting on the transverse component to compensate diffusion-induced dissipation and stabilize discriminative variations. We establish global well-posedness of the proposed dynamics and prove that the null-mode-free Dirichlet energy remains bounded away from zero. A forward-Euler discretization provides a practical HNRD layer with a stability condition for deep propagation. Experiments on benchmark and synthetic heterophilic hypergraphs demonstrate that HNRD consistently improves over representative hypergraph baselines. Depth, robustness, and efficiency analyses further show that HNRD preserves stable performance and nonzero Dirichlet energy under deep propagation and perturbations. These results provide a principled dynamical framework for designing deep hypergraph architectures that maintain higher-order expressiveness without representation collapse.

</details>


### [146] [Scaling Time Series Classification via XAI-Driven Data Reduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15774)
*Davide Italo Serramazza,Thach Le Nguyen,Georgiana Ifrim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 drXAI，一种利用可解释人工智能（XAI）归因方法进行时间序列分类数据缩减的新方法。通过快速 GPU 加速分类器 Hydra 生成局部归因，聚合得到全局特征重要性，并采用自动肘部截断启发式选择最显著特征，实现 80%-90% 的数据缩减同时保持分类精度，使资源密集型模型（如 ConvTran）能够扩展到先前因内存限制而无法处理的数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列分类（TSC）模型（如 Transformer）在序列长度和通道数上具有二次或线性复杂度，导致大规模数据集计算成本高昂；同时，可解释人工智能（XAI）在提升下游任务性能方面的潜力尚未被充分挖掘。本文旨在弥合这一差距，将 XAI 方法用于数据缩减，以提升 TSC 的可扩展性。

Method: 提出 drXAI 方法：使用快速 GPU 加速分类器 Hydra 生成局部归因（解释），将局部归因聚合为全局特征重要性分数，然后通过自动肘部截断启发式（elbow-cut heuristic）自动选择最显著的特征，无需手动设定阈值。该方法可将原始数据缩减至 10%-20%，同时保留关键特征。

Result: 在合成数据上，drXAI 成功恢复真实特征，而传统基线失败；在真实数据集上，实现 80%-90% 的数据缩减，同时分类精度与使用完整数据的模型相当；最重要的是，drXAI 使资源密集型模型（如 ConvTran）能够扩展到之前由于内存限制而无法处理的数据集。

Conclusion: XAI 不仅可用于可解释性，还可作为时间序列分析中特征选择和可扩展性的强大工具。drXAI 通过数据缩减有效提升了 TSC 的可扩展性，且所有代码和数据已公开。

Abstract: Explainable AI (XAI) for time series has seen significant algorithmic growth, but its utility in providing measurable performance gains for downstream tasks remains under-explored. This paper bridges this gap by introducing drXAI, a novel methodology that repurposes XAI attribution methods for effective data reduction in Time Series Classification (TSC). The core challenge in modern TSC is scalability; state-of-the-art models, such as Transformers, exhibit quadratic complexity relative to sequence length and linear complexity relative to the number of channels. This renders them computationally prohibitive for massive datasets. drXAI addresses this by using a fast, GPU-accelerated classifier (Hydra) to generate local attributions. We aggregate these into global feature importance scores and employ an automated elbow-cut heuristic to select the most salient features without requiring manual thresholds.
  We evaluate our approach on both synthetic and real-world univariate and multivariate datasets. On synthetic benchmarks, drXAI successfully recovers ground-truth features where traditional baselines fail. On real-world data, drXAI achieves between 80% and 90% data reduction while maintaining classification accuracy comparable to models trained on the full dataset. Most importantly, we show that drXAI allows resource-intensive models like ConvTran to scale to datasets that were previously inaccessible due to memory constraints. Our results show the benefits of using XAI not just for interpretability, but as a robust tool for feature selection and scalability in time series analysis. All our code and data are openly available.

</details>


### [147] [AquaAugmentor: A Novel Feature Augmentation Algorithm for Water Potability Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15775)
*Muntasir Tabasum,Al Zadid Sultan Bin Habib,Tanpia Tasnim,Md. Ekramul Islam,Md Younus Ahamed,Md Asif Bin Syed*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为AquaAugmentor的特征增强算法，用于提升机器学习和深度学习模型在低维水质数据集上预测饮用水可饮用性的性能，并通过实验验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确分类水质对于健康、经济发展和可持续性至关重要，但由于水质数据的复杂性和变异性，传统方法面临挑战，亟需更有效的预测方法。

Method: 提出AquaAugmentor特征增强算法，将其应用于多种机器学习和深度学习模型，在包含pH、硬度、固体、氯胺、硫酸盐等化学属性的水质数据集上，对水是否可饮用进行分类。

Result: 通过测试准确率和AUC分数评估模型在使用和不使用AquaAugmentor时的性能，展示了该算法在提升低维数据集预测性能方面的优势和局限性。

Conclusion: 该研究为利用机器学习进行环境质量评估提供了框架，有助于研究人员、政策制定者和公共卫生官员基于可靠的预测做出知情决策，为保障安全饮用水获取做出了贡献。

Abstract: Access to potable water is crucial for health, economic development, and sustainability. However, accurately classifying water quality remains a significant challenge due to the complexity and variability of water source data. This paper addresses the challenge of predicting water potability through machine learning and deep learning algorithms. It introduces a novel feature augmentation algorithm, AquaAugmentor, to enhance the predictive performance of these models for low-dimensional datasets. Utilizing a dataset that includes chemical attributes of water, such as pH, hardness, solids, chloramines, sulfate, and others. This study evaluates the performance of the models with and without AquaAugmentor. Each model applied to classify water as potable or non-potable and its performance is then evaluated and compared based on test accuracy and AUC score. The results highlight the strengths and limitations of our proposed algorithm, providing insights into the most effective techniques for improving the predictive performance of water quality classification. This study contributes to the broader efforts of ensuring safe water access and serves as a framework for employing machine learning in environmental quality assessments. The findings aim to assist researchers, policymakers, and public health officials in making informed decisions based on reliable machine learning predictions.

</details>


### [148] [Knowledge-Assisted Multi-Graph Dependency Learning for Multivariate Time Series Anomaly Detection in Multi-Stage Industrial Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15799)
*Jaeyeong Lee,Taeseong Yoon,Wonmo Koo,Heeyoung Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种知识辅助的多图框架，用于多阶段工业过程中的多变量时间序列异常检测。该方法通过构建一个纯数据驱动图和两个集成过程知识的结构约束图，并利用多图注意力网络进行依赖建模，从而提升异常检测性能。在真实工业数据集上的实验表明，融入过程知识能够显著增强检测效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于图神经网络的多元时间序列异常检测方法往往忽略关键的过程知识，即便考虑了过程知识，也难以无缝地将其融入现有模型，导致性能次优。

Method: 提出知识辅助的多图框架，构建三个互补图：一个纯数据驱动图，两个通过过程知识的结构约束精炼的图；并采用多图注意力网络来有效利用这些图进行异常检测，从而更准确、鲁棒地表示复杂依赖关系。

Result: 在两个真实的多阶段工业数据集上的综合实验表明，融入过程知识显著增强了异常检测性能。

Conclusion: 显式地将过程知识融入图学习能够增强依赖建模并提升异常检测性能，所提出的多图框架为解决工业过程中复杂依赖关系提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Industrial processes often generate complex, interdependent time-series data from multiple sensors across multiple stages, forming complex dependencies among variables and process stages. Effective monitoring and timely anomaly detection of these time series through multivariate time series anomaly detection (MTAD) is crucial for preventing failures and ensuring the reliability of automated systems. Graph neural networks (GNNs) have advanced MTAD by leveraging data-driven graphs to model complex dependencies among variables, effectively capturing relational structures within multivariate time series to enhance anomaly detection performance. However, existing GNN-based approaches often overlook critical process knowledge, and even when this knowledge is considered, seamlessly incorporating it into existing models remains inherently challenging, leading to suboptimal performance. To address this limitation, we propose a knowledge-assisted multi-graph framework for modeling sensor dependencies in multi-stage industrial processes for MTAD, which explicitly incorporates process knowledge into graph learning to enhance dependency modeling and improve anomaly detection performance. Our method constructs three complementary graphs: one purely data-driven and two refined by integrating structural constraints derived from process knowledge. To effectively leverage these graphs for anomaly detection, we employ a multi-graph attention network, enabling a more accurate and robust representation of complex dependencies. Comprehensive experiments on two real-world, multi-stage industrial datasets demonstrate that incorporating process knowledge substantially enhances anomaly detection performance.

</details>


### [149] [QUADS: Stabilizing NVFP4 Reinforcement Learning for MoE via QUantization-error Alignment across Dual Sides](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15810)
*Zhengyang Zhuge,Hao Yu,Xin Wang,Zheng Li,Yizhong Cao,Dayiheng Liu,Jianwei Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对MoE大模型RL中 rollout生成这一瓶颈，提出用NVFP4低精度格式加速。但直接应用NVFP4会导致训练崩溃（约150步后），原因是激活值量化误差而非权重误差。为此，提出QUADS方法：训练端引入非对称量化感知训练（仅量化权重），推理端用残差激活补偿纠正高误差通道。实验表明QUADS达到BF16精度，pass@1比朴素NVFP4 RL提升21.49点，吞吐量比FP8高约16%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE大模型RL中的rollout生成是主要瓶颈，亟需低精度加速（如FP8）。NVFP4格式结合细粒度缩放和原生W4A4 GEMM，吞吐量高于FP8，但直接用于MoE RL rollout会导致训练不稳定（约150步后崩溃），因此需要解决NVFP4在RL场景下的稳定性问题。

Method: 提出QUADS（Quantization-error Alignment across Dual Sides）：(1) 训练端：非对称量化感知训练（Asymmetric QAT），仅对权重进行fake-quantization，保持激活未量化以更好对齐；(2) 推理/rollout端：残差激活补偿（Residual Activation Compensation），在保持原生W4A4 GEMM的同时纠正高误差激活通道。

Result: 在多个MoE RL基准上，QUADS达到BF16级精度，朴素NVFP4 RL的pass@1平均提升21.49点，rollout吞吐量比FP8高约16%。

Conclusion: 通过识别FP4 RL不稳定的根源（激活误差而非权重误差），并提出QUADS双端对齐策略，成功将NVFP4低精度格式应用于MoE RL rollout加速，兼顾精度与吞吐量。

Abstract: Rollout generation is a major bottleneck in Reinforcement Learning (RL) for Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) Large Language Models, motivating low-precision rollout acceleration such as FP8. As an emerging low-precision format, NVFP4 combines fine-grained scaling for accuracy preservation with native W4A4 FP4 GEMMs for higher throughput than FP8. However, we find that directly applying NVFP4 to MoE RL rollout is impractical. NVFP4 rollout with BF16 training collapses after roughly 150 steps, accompanied by rapidly growing rollout-trainer log-probability gaps. Through training-inference error analysis and controlled ablations, we identify activation error, rather than weight error, as the dominant source of FP4 RL instability: weights can be synchronized and aligned by a shared quantization-dequantization path, whereas activations are recomputed online and error is amplified by the coarse E2M1 grid. Therefore, to stabilize NVFP4 RL for MoE, we propose QUantization-error Alignment across Dual Sides (QUADS). On the trainer side, we introduce Asymmetric Quantization-Aware Training fake-quantizing weights while keeping activations unquantized for better alignment. On the rollout side, Residual Activation Compensation corrects high-error activation channels while preserving native W4A4 GEMMs. In our MoE RL experiments on several benchmarks, QUADS achieves BF16-level accuracy, improves average pass@1 by 21.49 points over naive NVFP4 RL, and delivers ~16% higher rollout throughput than FP8.

</details>


### [150] [Graph Coloring Approach to Solving Sudoku with Oscillatory Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15814)
*Filip Sabo,Aida Todri-Sanial*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于振荡神经网络（ONN）的数独求解器，通过将数独问题转化为图着色问题，并修改现有图着色求解器使其计算更高效，同时引入额外项确保数独约束，从而在准确率上显著优于现有的霍普菲尔德神经网络（HNN）和ONN求解器。在4×4数独上达到近乎完美的准确率，在9×9数独上也获得了较高的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 振荡神经网络（ONN）是一种基于物理的计算范式，但现有ONN求解器在约束组合优化问题（如数独）上的性能有限。本文旨在通过改进ONN的图着色求解器，提高数独求解的准确率。

Method: 将数独问题建模为图着色问题，修改已有的图着色ONN求解器，使其计算成本更低（ computationally cheaper），并引入一个额外的约束项以确保数独规则（行、列、宫格内数字唯一）的满足。

Result: 在4×4数独上实现了近乎完美的准确率，在9×9数独上针对不同未知数字数量均获得了较高的准确率，显著优于现有的HNN和ONN求解器。

Conclusion: 所提出的ONN求解器在约束组合优化问题（如数独）上具有更高的准确性和潜力，验证了ONN作为物理计算范式的有效性。

Abstract: Oscillatory Neural Networks (ONNs) present an attractive physics-based computing paradigm rooted in the dynamics of a network of typically fully coupled oscillators aiming to minimize an underlying energy function. In this paper, we propose an ONN-based solver for one well-known constrained combinatorial optimization problem, namely a Sudoku, by formulating the problem as a Graph Coloring problem. By modifying the already existing Graph Coloring solver to a computationally cheaper version and introducing an additional term ensuring the fulfillment of the Sudoku constraints, our solver is shown to significantly outperform the existing HNN- and ONN solvers in terms of accuracy. In particular, we are able to achieve nearly flawless accuracies on $4 \times 4$ as well as rather high accuracies on $9 \times 9$ Sudoku puzzles for different numbers of unknown digits.

</details>


### [151] [In-context learning of closed form solution to simple linear regression task using transformer with linear self-attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15819)
*Katsuyuki Hagiwara*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个具有线性自注意力的Transformer，通过层归一化近似获得最小二乘估计的闭式解，而非梯度下降算法，从而在上下文中学习线性回归任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究显示Transformer能实现回归求解器（多为梯度下降），但尚不清楚这些实现是否真正通过训练获得。本文旨在澄清Transformer是否能在训练后实际获得解析解的实现。

Method: 设计线性自注意力Transformer，利用层归一化（Layer Normalization）近似逼近最小二乘估计的闭式解，而非依赖梯度下降；使用L1正则化训练，目标输出为最小二乘估计。

Result: 实验表明，所提出的线性自注意力Transformer在简单回归任务中成功实现上下文学习，其内部机制通过层归一化近似闭式解，且主要在使用L1正则化训练时有效。

Conclusion: Transformer可以通过层归一化而非梯度下降来获得最小二乘估计的近似闭式解，为理解上下文学习的内部实现机制提供了新视角。

Abstract: In-context learning is a remarkable property of transformers and has recently received a lot of interest. In many studies of in-context learning, it has been shown that transformers are capable of implementing solver for linear and non-linear regression problems, in which the most of them implement gradient descent algorithm. However, it is still unclear whether those implementations have actually been acquired through training. In this paper, we construct a transformer with linear self-attention, which in-context learns the least squares estimate in a simple regression task. The point here is that the closed form (analytical) solution is approximately obtained by using layer normalization rather than an approximate solution based on gradient descent algorithm. Then, we show an experimental example, in which our implementation is mainly used in the transformer trained with l1 regularization when the target output is the least squares estimate.

</details>


### [152] [Data-Native Global Optimization for Big Data K-means Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15835)
*Ravil Mussabayev,Rustam Mussabayev,Zukhra Yerdaliyeva,Kuldeyev Nursultan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Big-means++算法，通过样本诱导的替代景观和流动 incumbent 策略，在任意高维大数据上实现 K-means 聚类的高质量全局优化，显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大数据聚类面临挑战：MSSC问题是NP难的，现有方法要么陷入局部最优，要么需要昂贵的元启发式混合。目标是在固定特征空间中处理任意多（甚至无限）的观测数据，算法只能访问有限随机样本。

Method: Big-means++通过三个关键机制实现：1) 流动 incumbent 策略——在样本间传递质心状态而非回滚到最佳解，增强移动性；2) 几何变尺度抖动机制——按几何级数变化样本大小，拓宽探索的替代景观范围；3) 异步多智能体系统——多个智能体独立探索样本景观，将多样随机轨迹转化为集体搜索智能。自动收敛检测在达到高质量解后及时停止。

Result: 在22个数据集上与11种竞争算法对比，实验证明了Big-means++的有效性、效率和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: Big-means++成功解决了大数据K-means聚类中可扩展性与全局搜索质量的矛盾，通过样本诱导替代景观的巧妙设计实现了高效全局优化。

Abstract: Big data clustering remains challenging: the Minimum Sum-of-Squares Clustering (MSSC) problem underlying K-means is NP-hard, and existing methods either reach poor local minima or require prohibitive metaheuristic hybrids. We target arbitrarily tall data: a fixed feature space may contain arbitrarily many, possibly infinitely many, observations, while the algorithm accesses only finite random samples. We propose Big-means++, an algorithm achieving scalability and global-search quality by curating inputs to MSSC optimization on big data. It orchestrates local K-means refinements into a data-native global search for big data clustering. Rather than optimizing the full-data MSSC objective, Big-means++ traverses sample-induced surrogate landscapes. Each sample defines a distinct empirical MSSC approximation with a perturbed local-optimum structure, turning sample-to-sample variation into a global-search mechanism. Unlike Big-means, a flowing-incumbent strategy propagates centroid state across empirical landscapes through K-means refinements on fresh samples without rollback to a best-so-far solution. This increases mobility and favors stable, high-quality configurations across approximations of the full-data structure. A new shaking mechanism varies sample size geometrically, broadening the surrogate landscapes explored across resolution scales, accounting for cluster imbalance, and improving solution quality. A competitive multi-agent system asynchronously explores independent sampled landscapes, transforming diverse stochastic trajectories into collective search intelligence. Automatic convergence detection stops each agent after attaining a high-quality solution but before further search risks degrading it, while providing a universal speed-quality control. Experiments on 22 datasets against 11 competing algorithms demonstrate the effectiveness, efficiency, and robustness of Big-means++.

</details>


### [153] [ContinuityBench: A Benchmark and Systems Study of Stateful Failover in Multi-Provider LLM Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15899)
*Vishal Pandey,Gopal Singh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对大规模语言模型生产部署中API高可用性不等于对话连续性的问题，提出了两种新指标：连续性保留率（CPR）和连续性延迟开销（CLO）。作者设计了一种基于历史转发策略的状态ful多提供商代理架构，能够在故障转移时无缝重建跨异构LLM端点的对话状态。同时发布了开源评估工具continuity-bench。在750次故障转移事件的实证评估中，该状态ful代理实现了99.20%的CPR，而标准无状态架构几乎为0%。此外，论文还表征了故障转移延迟分布，指出了异步指数退避加抖动的关键必要性，以防止级联重试风暴。结果为实现鲁棒、状态保留的多模型推理系统提供了原则性基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的高可用性API保证并不能保证对话连续性。当主要提供商出现故障或严格限速时，无状态故障转移机制虽然能维持正常运行时间，但会静默丢弃对话历史，严重破坏用户体验。需要量化并解决这一问题。

Method: 提出了两种新指标（CPR和CLO）来衡量对话连续性；设计了一种状态ful多提供商代理架构，采用历史转发策略在故障转移时跨异构LLM端点无缝重建对话状态；开发了开源评估框架continuity-bench用于在高并发提供商故障条件下测试上下文保存能力；在故障转移延迟分布分析中确定了异步指数退避加抖动的必要性。

Result: 在750次故障转移事件的评估中，状态ful代理实现了99.20%的CPR（95% CI: 98.27%, 99.63%），而标准无状态架构的保留率接近0%。

Conclusion: 这项工作为实现鲁棒、状态保留的多模型推理系统提供了原则性基础，证明了状态ful历史转发机制能有效解决故障转移时的对话历史丢失问题，同时指出了防止级联重试风暴的关键设计要素。

Abstract: In production large language model (LLM) deployments, high API availability guarantees do not equate to conversational continuity. When a primary provider experiences an outage or strict rate-limiting, naive stateless failover mechanisms successfully maintain uptime but silently discard conversation history, severely disrupting the user experience. To rigorously quantify and resolve this failure mode, we introduce two novel metrics: Continuity Preservation Rate (CPR) and Continuity Latency Overhead (CLO). We propose a stateful, multi-provider proxy architecture utilizing a History-Forwarding strategy to seamlessly reconstruct conversational state across heterogeneous LLM endpoints during failover events. Furthermore, we release continuity-bench, https://github.com/Vishal-sys-code/continuity-bench, an open evaluation harness designed to stress-test context preservation under high-concurrency provider failure conditions. Our empirical evaluation ($N=750$ failover events) demonstrates that our stateful proxy achieves a 99.20\% CPR [95\% CI: 98.27\%, 99.63\%], cleanly transferring deep conversational context to fallback providers, compared to a near-0\% preservation rate for standard stateless architectures. Finally, we characterize failover latency distributions, identifying the critical necessity of asynchronous exponential backoff with jitter to prevent cascading retry storms against strict-limit fallback APIs. Our results provide a principled foundation for building robust, state-preserving multi-model inference systems.

</details>


### [154] [A Semiparametric Framework for Stochastic Fundamental Diagram Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15907)
*Pengnan Chi,Xiaoliang Ma,Magnus Jansson,Magnus Nordenvaad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种半参数随机基本图（SFD）建模框架，通过设计特殊函数形式内在满足物理约束（条件流量分布矩约束），同时利用神经网络捕捉复杂非线性模式。推导了矩匹配方程组将物理约束转化为条件分布参数化，证明了位置-尺度分布族解的唯一性，并拓展至非位置-尺度分布。在真实数据上优于基线模型，尤其在拥堵区域提供更优的概率预测和不确定性量化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有随机基本图模型难以同时满足严格的物理约束（如密度-流量关系上下界、单调性等）和保持足够的灵活性以捕捉复杂的非线性模式，亟需一种既能理论保证物理合理性又能灵活拟合数据的方法。

Method: 提出半参数SFD建模框架：1) 设计特殊函数形式，内在满足对条件流量分布矩的物理约束；2) 结合神经网络结构捕获复杂经验模式；3) 推导矩匹配方程组，将物理约束转化为条件分布的参数化；4) 证明位置-尺度分布族解的唯一性，保证模型适定性；5) 将框架扩展至非位置-尺度分布（含边界约束）。

Result: 在真实数据集上的实证评估表明，该方法一致优于代表性基线模型，提供更优的概率预测精度和稳健的不确定性量化，尤其在拥堵交通状态下优势明显。

Conclusion: 该框架为随机交通流建模提供了理论严谨且灵活的基础，兼具物理可解释性与数据驱动灵活性，有望推广至更广泛的交通不确定性建模任务。

Abstract: The stochastic fundamental diagram (SFD) provides a probabilistic description of the relationship between traffic density and flow or speed, enabling uncertainty-aware traffic modeling. However, existing stochastic models frequently struggle to accommodate rigorous physical constraints while retaining sufficient flexibility to capture complex nonlinear patterns. To address this, we propose a novel semiparametric SFD modeling framework by leveraging specially designed functional forms. These functions intrinsically satisfy physical constraints defined on the moments of the conditional flow distribution given traffic density while incorporating neural-network-based structures to capture complex empirical patterns. We derive a system of moment-matching equations to convert physical constraints into the parameterization of the conditional distribution, proving that a unique solution exists for the location-scale family of distributions, thereby guaranteeing model well-posedness. Furthermore, we demonstrate that the framework can be extended to non-location-scale distributions, including those requiring additional boundary constraints. Empirical evaluations on a real-world dataset reveal that our approach consistently outperforms representative baselines, delivering superior probabilistic accuracy and robust uncertainty quantification, particularly in congested regimes. Overall, the proposed framework provides a theoretically grounded and flexible foundation for stochastic traffic flow modeling.

</details>


### [155] [(MPO)$^2$: Multivariate Polynomial Optimization based on Matrix Product Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15916)
*Niccolò Ciolli,Anders Vestergaard Nørskov,Michael Kastoryano,Petr Taborsky,Morten Mørup*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了基于矩阵乘积算子（MPO）的多项式优化框架 (MPO)^2，通过学习 MPO 特征嵌入和紧凑多项式权重张量，实现了特征顺序无关的多项式表示，并能在回归和分类任务中优于现有张量分解多项式模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多元多项式模型能有效表达特征间的乘法交互，但其系数张量随多项式次数呈指数增长。现有张量化模型（如 CP 分解、张量列）存在秩表达受限或特征顺序依赖等问题，亟需一种更高效、灵活且特征顺序无关的多项式函数逼近方法。

Method: (MPO)^2 框架结合了可学习的 MPO 特征嵌入与紧凑多项式权重张量，形成特征顺序无关的多项式表示；同时可融入投影、卷积、掩码等结构化算子以施加权重张量对称性。

Result: 在回归与分类基准测试中，(MPO)^2 优于基于张量分解的现有多项式模型，提供了高效且灵活的多项式函数逼近替代方案。

Conclusion: (MPO)^2 是一种有效的多项式函数逼近框架，克服了传统张量分解模型的局限性，具备特征顺序无关性和结构化算子集成能力，为机器学习中的多项式建模提供了新途径。

Abstract: Central to machine learning and signal processing is the ability to perform universal function approximation and learn complex input-output relationships from limited numbers of observations. Multivariate polynomial models offer a natural way to express such relationships through multiplicative feature interactions, but their coefficient tensors grow exponentially in size with the polynomial degree. Existing tensorized polynomial models reduce this cost, yet canonical polyadic decompositions have rank-limited expressivity, and tensor train formulations are feature order dependent. We introduce Multivariate Polynomial Optimization based on Matrix Product Operators (MPO)$^2$, a framework that combines learned MPO feature embeddings with compact polynomial weight tensors. This yields feature order independent polynomial representations that can incorporate structured operators such as projections, convolutions, and masks for weight tensor symmetries. Across regression and classification benchmarks, (MPO)$^2$ improves over existing tensor decomposition based polynomial models and provides a flexible alternative for efficient polynomial function approximation.

</details>


### [156] [On the Failure of Boundary-Seeking Distillation in Bottlenecked Generative Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15919)
*Mohamed Amine Kina*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了数据无关知识蒸馏在自编码器（autoencoder）中的应用，发现原本用于分类器的边界搜索（boundary-seeking）目标函数在瓶颈式生成架构（bottlenecked generative architectures）中存在根本性问题。通过将连续重建任务重新表述为密集的逐特征分类任务，作者证明CAKE（对比溯因知识提取）方法直接应用于解码器时，独立采样对比目标会违反潜在流形几何结构并产生梯度冲突。而流形感知合成（manifold-aware synthesis）能够避免这些冲突，为数据无关生成式蒸馏建立了有效基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究数据无关知识蒸馏中用于分类器的边界搜索原则（如CAKE方法）能否扩展到自编码器蒸馏，从而在无原始训练数据情况下实现生成式模型的蒸馏。

Method: 将自编码器的连续重建任务重新公式化为密集的逐特征分类任务（解码器输出分类logits），并尝试将CAKE的对比目标采样直接应用于解码器输出；同时提出流形感知合成方法作为替代方案。

Result: 边界搜索目标在瓶颈式生成架构中本质不适定：独立为耦合的解码器输出采样对比目标会违反潜在流形几何结构，导致严重梯度冲突，无法产生有效的边界样本；流形感知合成则能绕过这些冲突。

Conclusion: 边界搜索原则不能直接扩展到自编码器蒸馏，流形感知合成是数据无关生成式蒸馏的有效基线方法。

Abstract: Data-free knowledge distillation transfers the knowledge encoded in a teacher model to a student model without access to the original training data. Prior work such as Contrastive Abductive Knowledge Extraction (CAKE) achieves this for classifiers by synthesizing samples near the teacher's decision boundary. In this work, we investigate whether this boundary-seeking principle extends to autoencoder distillation through experiments on the MNIST dataset . To enable a direct comparison, we reformulate continuous reconstruction as a dense, per-feature classification task, allowing the decoder to output categorical logits. We show that boundary-seeking objectives are fundamentally ill-posed in bottlenecked generative architectures. CAKE operates on a single, instance-level objective, but a decoder acts as an array of tightly coupled, feature-level classifiers constrained by a shared low-dimensional bottleneck. Independently sampling contrastive targets for these coupled outputs violates the geometry of the learned latent manifold and produces severe gradient conflicts instead of informative boundary samples. Manifold-aware synthesis bypasses these conflicts entirely and establishes an effective baseline for data-free generative distillation.

</details>


### [157] [Knowledge-Guided Cross-Modal Fusion for Adult-to-Pediatric ECG Transfer via Label-Conditioned Contrastive Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15928)
*Xinran Liu,Yuwen Li,Hongxiang Gao,Heyang Xu,Jianqing Li,Zongmin Wang,Chengyu Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PEACE是一个知识引导的多模态ECG-文本对齐框架，通过将诊断按节律、形态和ST-T轴分解，并利用标签查询网络与标签集感知双向对比学习，在成人数据预训练后有效迁移至儿科ECG解释，在零样本和少样本场景下显著优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 成人与儿童ECG判读依赖年龄敏感性标准，现有主要在成人ECG上预训练的模型在儿科标签稀缺时迁移效果差；已有的多模态ECG-文本方法在全局样本级对齐，混淆了共现诊断的证据，限制了跨年龄段迁移能力。

Method: PEACE框架：1）将每个诊断按节律、形态、ST-T三个轴描述，仅组合阳性标签描述为三个轴令牌和融合嵌入；2）标签查询网络（LQN）以诊断标签为查询，交叉关注ECG令牌和轴令牌；3）标签集感知双向对比学习（LSBC）对齐共享诊断的ECG特征与融合嵌入；4）课程自适应融合（CAF）根据平滑分类损失和训练进度调控对齐强度；知识分支仅用于训练，推理仅需ECG信号。

Result: 在ZZU-pECG上，零样本、50样本和全微调下宏平均AUC分别达59.39%、81.74%和91.56%，在有限监督下提升最显著；零样本大幅优于领域自适应初始化，50样本AUC与DANN相当；在PTB-XL微调后九标签宏平均AUC达96.90%。消融实验证实标签条件知识对齐而非全局文本融合是儿科迁移提升的关键。

Conclusion: PEACE通过细粒度的诊断轴分解和标签条件对齐，有效弥合了成人-儿科ECG间的领域差异，在儿科标签稀缺场景下实现了显著性能提升，验证了知识引导的跨模态对齐策略在医学领域迁移学习中的有效性。

Abstract: Adult and pediatric electrocardiogram (ECG) interpretation relies on age-sensitive criteria, and models pretrained mainly on adult ECGs often transfer poorly to pediatric populations when pediatric labels are scarce. Existing multimodal ECG--text methods typically align waveforms and text at the global sample level, entangling evidence from co-occurring diagnoses and limiting transfer under this gap. We propose Pediatric-Adult ECG Alignment via Cross-modal Enhancement (PEACE), a knowledge-guided framework pretrained on the largely adult MIMIC-IV ECG corpus. PEACE describes each diagnosis along rhythm, morphology, and ST--T axes and, per recording, composes only positive-label descriptors into three axis tokens and a fused embedding. A label query network (LQN) uses diagnostic labels as queries to cross-attend over ECG tokens and axis tokens, while label set aware bidirectional contrastive learning (LSBC) aligns pooled ECG features with the fused embedding when recordings share diagnoses. Curriculum adaptive fusion (CAF) gates alignment strength according to smoothed classification loss and training progress, limiting disruption during early optimization. The knowledge branch is used only for training supervision; inference uses ECG signals alone. On ZZU-pECG, PEACE reaches macro average AUCs of 59.39%, 81.74%, and 91.56% under zero-shot, 50-shot, and full fine-tuning, with the clearest gains over foundation and knowledge-pretraining baselines under limited supervision; versus domain adaptation initializations, zero-shot improves substantially while 50-shot AUC is comparable to DANN. After fine-tuning on PTB-XL, PEACE reaches 96.90% macro average AUC over nine harmonized labels. Ablations confirm that label-conditioned knowledge alignment, rather than global text fusion, is the key driver of pediatric transfer gains.

</details>


### [158] [An Exploratory Study of Single Channel Surface Electromyography for Hand Gesture Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15972)
*Daanish Hindustani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究探索使用单通道表面肌电信号（sEMG）结合轻量级机器学习架构进行十种手势分类的可行性，通过特征工程和降维，在有限时空信息下实现高达90%的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统高精度手势识别依赖多通道传感器阵列和计算密集型模型，限制了其在低功耗和嵌入式系统中的实际部署，因此需要探索更经济、低功耗的替代方案。

Method: 将单通道原始sEMG信号转换为包含时域、频域、过零点高阶特征和相对强度特征的综合性特征表示；通过皮尔逊相关滤波去除高相关特征进行冗余削减，并选择性应用LDA和PCA进行降维；系统评估了前馈神经网络（NN）、K近邻（KNN）和支持向量机（SVM）三种分类器，设计了四组实验。

Result: 结合时域与频域特征、皮尔逊滤波以及紧凑型神经网络，在有限的时空信息下可达到高达90%的分类准确率。

Conclusion: 单通道sEMG系统在成本效益和低功耗手势识别应用中具有潜力，为嵌入式部署提供了可能。

Abstract: Accurate hand gesture recognition using surface electromyography (sEMG) typically relies on multichannel sensor arrays and computationally intensive models. This limits practical deployment in low-power and embedded systems. This study investigates the feasibility of classifying ten hand gestures using a single sEMG channel combined with lightweight machine learning architectures. Raw sEMG signals were transformed into a comprehensive feature-based representation, including time-domain, frequency-domain, higher-order-crossing, and relative-intensity features. Feature redundancy was reduced using Pearson correlation filtering and the removal of highly correlated features, while dimensionality-reduction techniques (LDA and PCA) were applied selectively. Three classifiers, a feed-forward neural network (NN), k-nearest neighbors (KNN), and a support vector machine (SVM), were systematically evaluated across four experiments. Results demonstrate that combining time and frequency features with Pearson filtering and a compact NN can achieve up to 90 percent accuracy, even with limited temporal and spatial information. These findings highlight the potential of single-channel sEMG systems for cost-effective, low-power gesture-recognition applications.

</details>


### [159] [DebrisTracer: Reliable Tracking in Hypervelocity Impact Fast Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15986)
*Théophane Loloum,Fabien Vivodtzev,David Hébert,Baptiste Reynier,Michel Arrigoni,Julien Tierny*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DebrisTracer是一个用于超高速撞击成像中可靠碎片追踪的框架，基于拓扑追踪框架扩展，融合领域知识与物理假设，实现自动、精确、可解释的碎片追踪分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在航空航天应用中，可靠估计超高速撞击后碎片的质量与速度分布至关重要。然而，这类快速成像数据集噪声大、高度特异，现有工具难以提供准确可靠的追踪结果。

Method: 基于现成的拓扑追踪框架（基于临界点提取与匹配），通过融入领域知识和物理假设进行扩展，形成自动化的碎片追踪流程，支持可解释的视觉分析。

Result: 该方法在物理验证方面显著优于领域专家使用的既有工具，能准确预测实验 ejected mass 和 crater depth 剖面；统计摘要可视觉识别碎片群体中的不同 regime，印证并细化专家预期。

Conclusion: DebrisTracer在多种撞击角度和物理条件的使用案例中均有效，提供了 C++ 实现与数据库开源（GitHub），为超高速撞击碎片分析提供了可靠且可解释的解决方案。

Abstract: This application paper presents DebrisTracer, a framework for the reliable tracking of debris in hypervelocity impact fast imaging. These noisy and highly specific datasets capture the ejection of a large number of debris fragments after the impact of a projectile launched at hypervelocity into a target material. The reliable estimation of debris mass and speed distributions is of major importance in aerospace applications. We document how to extend an off-the-shelf topology tracking framework based on critical point extraction and matching, in order to incorporate domain knowledge and physical assumptions. Our approach automatically produces an accurate and reliable debris tracking, enabling an interpretable visual analysis of this complex space-time phenomenon. Extensive experiments demonstrate the accuracy improvements provided by our approach over established tools used by domain experts in terms of physical validation, specifically via the prediction of the experimental ejected mass and crater depth profiles. We illustrate the utility of our approach across several use cases (with varying impact angles and physics). We show that our statistical summaries enable the visual identification of distinct regimes within the debris population, corroborating and refining prior expectations of domain experts. Our database and our C++ implementation are available at this address: https://github.com/tloloum/DebrisTracer.

</details>


### [160] [Presentation, Not Mechanism: A Render Confound in Deprecation-Aware Memory Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16019)
*Zhaoyang Jiang,Zhizhong Fu,Zicheng Li,Yunsoo Kim,Jiacong Mi,Xuanqi Peng,Fei Teng,Honghan Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究AI系统从自修订记录（如议题线程、百科历史、政策日志、长对话）中检索证据时的状态判断问题。通过控制呈现方式（render-matched）这一关键混杂变量，发现所谓的“细粒度证据修订记录”（RevisionLedger）相比扁平基线的性能提升几乎全部来自更易读的呈现方式，而非细粒度机制本身；在控制呈现后，粗粒度无效化机制反而优于细粒度方案。结论：记忆评估应固定呈现方式，弃用感知系统应部署覆盖查询所需的最粗保留状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI系统越来越多地从会自我修订的记录中检索信息，但现有评估常同时改变检索机制和提示呈现方式（混淆变量），导致无法判断性能提升的真实来源。需要厘清究竟是细粒度的结构化记忆机制本身有效，还是呈现方式更友好带来的增益。

Method: 定义“证据状态修订”（Evidence-State Revision）任务，在2,907个高一致性问题上比较三种方法：扁平检索、粗粒度边无效化、细粒度RevisionLedger。关键设计是引入呈现匹配对照（相同布局但禁用弃用功能），以隔离呈现方式对性能的贡献。数据来源包括GitHub议题、多仓库问题历史、Wikipedia和DyKnow风格的时间流。

Result: 呈现匹配对照揭示了核心混杂：当值被修改后又恢复时，RevisionLedger看似比扁平基线高+0.182，但几乎全部增益来自更易读的呈现方式；细粒度机制的残余效果在统计上不可区分于零（+0.021至+0.025）。控制呈现后，粗粒度无效化是唯一对当前状态查询有正向贡献的机制，超出细粒度方案0.084。

Conclusion: 记忆评估应固定呈现方式（render fixed）以消除混杂；弃用感知系统应部署覆盖查询所需的最粗保留状态（coarsest retained state），因为证据充分性原理表明主要需要保留无效化证据而非更丰富的类型标记。

Abstract: AI systems increasingly retrieve from records that revise themselves: issue threads, encyclopedic histories, policy logs, and long conversations. The challenge is not only finding relevant evidence, but deciding which claims remain in force, which were superseded, and when to abstain. Structured memories promise to solve this with typed edges, temporal updates, and conflict status, yet evaluations often change mechanism and prompt presentation together. We study this as Evidence-State Revision, comparing flat retrieval, coarse edge invalidation, and fine-grained RevisionLedger on 2,907 high-agreement questions from GitHub, multi-repo issue histories, Wikipedia, and DyKnow-style temporal streams. A render-matched control (same layout, deprecation disabled) reveals the central confound: when a value is changed and later restored, RevisionLedger appears to beat a flat baseline by +0.182, but almost all the gain comes from easier presentation; the fine-grained mechanism residual is indistinguishable from zero (+0.021 to +0.025 across two judge families). After presentation is controlled, coarse invalidation is the only mechanism that pays for current-state queries, beating the fine ledger by 0.084; the same query-sufficiency principle says provenance mainly needs retained invalidated evidence, not richer typing. Memory evaluations should hold render fixed, and deprecation-aware systems should deploy the coarsest retained state that covers their queries.

</details>


### [161] [Constrained Hebbian Learning Supports Efficient Representational Allocation under Structural Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16027)
*Patrick Inoue,Florian Röhrbein,Andreas Knoblauch*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究生物神经系统在解剖和代谢约束下，兴奋性竞争性Hebbian学习规则能否支持突触资源分配，并在成本-性能权衡中优于其他学习规则。通过信息瓶颈度量表征成本，在三个视听基准上比较Hebbian学习、DDTP和反向传播，发现Hebbian学习以较低的任务信息成本实现了可比的功能性能，支持将其视为突触资源分配机制而非单纯最大化分类准确率的策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物神经系统面临高昂的突触维护成本和有限的连接性，这倾向于选择低冗余的神经编码。作者希望检验兴奋性竞争性Hebbian规则能否在这种约束下有效地分配突触资源，并探索其是否比参考学习规则（如反向传播和DDTP）占据更优的成本-性能权衡区间。

Method: 使用基于互信息度量的变分信息瓶颈（Variational Information Bottleneck）来量化表征成本。实验采用三个视听基准数据集（AVE、Kinetics-Sounds、VGGSound100）的固定视听嵌入，以隔离下游联想可塑性。在匹配稀疏性和架构约束的条件下，将Hebbian学习与密集差分目标传播（DDTP）和反向传播（BP）进行比较。

Result: 在主要压缩比较中，Hebbian学习实现了比稀疏BP和DDTP更低的任务信息成本（CTI），同时达到与非负权重浅层BP相当的CTI值。Hebbian学习并未普遍提高分类性能，而是改变了任务相关信息与表征成本之间的权衡，在若干设置中以较低CTI实现了可比的功能性能。

Conclusion: 结果支持成本-性能权衡而非统一的准确率提升。对于给定的任务相关信息水平，Hebbian表征保留了更少的输入信息同时保持了功能性能，尽管在某些数据集上准确率略有下降。这些发现支持将Hebbian学习解释为一种突触资源分配机制，而非最大化视听分类准确率的通用策略。

Abstract: Introduction: Biological systems face anatomical and metabolic constraints, including costly synaptic maintenance and limited connectivity. These constraints favor neural codes that compress behaviorally relevant information into low-redundancy patterns. We test whether an excitatory competitive Hebbian rule can support synaptic resource allocation under such constraints and whether the resulting representations occupy a more favorable cost-performance regime than reference learning rules.
  Methods: Representational cost is quantified using mutual-information-based measures derived from the Variational Information Bottleneck. Experiments use fixed audiovisual embeddings from three audiovisual benchmarks (AVE, Kinetics-Sounds, VGGSound100) to isolate downstream associative plasticity. Hebbian learning is compared with Dense Difference Target Propagation (DDTP) and backpropagation (BP) under matched sparsity and architectural constraints.
  Results: Hebbian learning achieves lower task-information cost (CTI) than sparse BP and DDTP in the main compressed comparisons, while reaching CTI values comparable to shallow BP with nonnegative weights. Rather than uniformly improving classification performance, Hebbian learning shifts the trade-off between task-relevant information and representational cost, yielding lower CTI at comparable functional performance in several settings.
  Discussion: The results indicate a cost-performance trade-off rather than uniform accuracy gains. For a given level of task-relevant information, Hebbian representations retain less input information while preserving functional performance, although accuracy is slightly reduced on some datasets. These findings support interpreting Hebbian learning as a mechanism for synaptic resource allocation rather than as a general strategy for maximizing audiovisual classification accuracy.

</details>


### [162] [CLaC@FinMMEval 2026 Task 3: Sentiment-Augmented Deep Reinforcement Learning for Active Trading -- An Alpha-Reward Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16028)
*Andrei Neagu,Eeham Khan,Leila Kosseim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于深度强化学习（DRL）的金融交易系统，参与CLEF 2026 FinMMEval Lab的Task 3（比特币和特斯拉的日频交易决策）。系统将交易建模为离散动作MDP，比较了PG、PPO、DQL和DDPG四种算法，使用技术指标、日历编码和LLaMA新闻情感分数作为状态特征，引入alpha奖励和随机化episode起始日期以减少过拟合。DDPG在测试集上整体表现最佳，DQL因验证集Sharpe比率最高被选为线上端点。TSLA上DDPG和DQL分别获得54.96%和52.62%的累计收益（基准16.45%），BTC上DDPG正收益1.58%（基准-34.27%）。但验证集到测试集存在显著泛化差距，反映了牛市训练策略迁移到熊市环境的困难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用深度强化学习在金融交易中超越传统的买入并持有策略，通过整合新闻情感和市场价格数据，实现自动化日频交易决策，并解决DRL在金融应用中的过拟合和策略泛化问题。

Method: 将交易问题建模为离散动作MDP，使用四种DRL算法（PG、PPO、DQL、DDPG）；状态空间包括技术指标、周期性日历编码和LLaMA 3.2 1B生成的日频新闻情感分数；设计alpha奖励（超额市场回报）和随机化episode起始日期来正则化训练；使用Ray Tune进行超参数优化（每算法-资产对180次试验），基于验证集Sharpe比率进行早停和模型选择。

Result: DDPG在测试集上整体表现最强。TSLA上DDPG和DQL分别实现54.96%和52.62%的累计收益（买入并持有16.45%）；BTC上DDPG实现正收益1.58%（买入并持有-34.27%）。存在显著验证集到测试集的泛化差距，牛市训练的模型在熊市环境下表现大幅下降。

Conclusion: 深度强化学习能够显著超越买入并持有策略，但存在严重的过拟合和跨市场周期泛化问题；alpha奖励和随机化起始日期等正则化技术有助于缓解但无法完全消除这一问题；DQL因验证集稳定性被选为线上部署模型，体现了离线评估与在线部署之间的权衡。

Abstract: This paper presents our system for Task 3 of the CLEF 2026 FinMMEval Lab, which requires daily long, flat, or short trading decisions for Bitcoin (BTC) and Tesla (TSLA) using news and historical market data. We formulate the problem as a discrete-action Markov Decision Process and compare four deep reinforcement learning algorithms: Policy Gradient (PG), Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO), Deep Q-Learning (DQL), and Deep Deterministic Policy Gradient (DDPG). The agents use technical indicators, cyclical calendar encodings, and daily news sentiment scores produced by LLaMA 3.2 1B. To reduce overfitting and align training with the objective of outperforming buy-and-hold, we introduce an alpha reward based on excess market return and randomize episode start dates. Hyperparameters are optimized with Ray Tune over 180 trials per algorithm-asset pair, with early stopping and model selection based on validation Sharpe ratio. On the CLEF Task 3 test set, DDPG achieves the strongest overall performance. DQL was selected a priori for the live endpoint because it obtained the highest validation Sharpe ratio, with selection performed without access to the test period. For TSLA, DDPG and DQL achieve cumulative returns of 54.96% and 52.62%, respectively, compared with 16.45% for buy-and-hold. For BTC, DDPG achieves a positive return of 1.58% while buy-and-hold declines by -34.27%. The results also reveal a substantial validation-to-test generalization gap, highlighting the difficulty of transferring policies selected in bull-market conditions to a bear-market regime.

</details>


### [163] [Revisiting data-driven dynamic security assessment with a tabular foundation model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16031)
*Olayiwola Arowolo,Maosheng Yang,Jochen Cremer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出使用表格基础模型（TFM）通过上下文学习进行数据驱动的预故障动态安全评估（DSA），无需重新训练或超参数优化。单个TFM可同时评估多种故障，仅需约120个标注样本即可达到约90%的Macro F1分数（比常规需求低两个数量级）。对于未见故障，仅需10个标注样本并结合电气距离坐标（EDC）编码即可匹配最佳迁移学习性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据驱动的预故障动态安全评估方法存在两个主要局限：一是需要大量标注数据库进行训练，且需为每个故障单独训练、调优和维护模型；二是训练的模型对未见故障泛化能力差。本文旨在解决这些局限，降低数据需求并提升泛化能力。

Method: 采用表格基础模型（TFM）进行上下文学习，无需重新训练或超参数优化。使用电气距离坐标（EDC）作为连续特征来增强泛化能力。单个TFM可同时评估多种故障，消除了每个故障需单独训练分类器的需求。通过少量标注样本（如10个）结合EDC编码来改善对未见故障的泛化能力。

Result: 在IEEE 68节点系统上的案例研究表明：单个TFM在每故障仅需120个标注样本的情况下，平均Macro F1分数达到约90%，比传统假设的样本需求低两个数量级。对于未见故障，仅使用10个该故障的标注样本结合EDC编码，即可匹配需要完整标注数据且实际中不可部署的最佳迁移学习oracle模型。

Conclusion: 这项初步研究为开发和部署用于电力系统运行的 foundation models 铺平了道路，其应用可扩展到多个运行任务。

Abstract: Data-driven pre-fault dynamic security assessment (DSA) rapidly evaluates the dynamic risk of credible contingencies on a power system using machine learning. Existing approaches face two limitations. First, they require a large labelled database for training, with a separate model trained, tuned, and maintained for each contingency in a potentially long list of credible contingencies. Second, the trained models generalize poorly to unseen contingencies. This work addresses the limitations by using a tabular foundation model (TFM) that assesses stability through in-context learning, requiring no retraining or hyperparameter optimization. A single TFM can assess many contingencies at once, removing the need for one model per classifier. We also characterize when the use of electrical distance coordinates (EDC) as continuous features enables generalization of TFM to unseen contingencies and when they do not, demonstrating how a few labelled samples can reliably improve generalization. Through comprehensive case studies on the IEEE 68-bus system, we show that a single TFM attains an average Macro F1 score of about 90% with only 120 labelled samples per contingency, roughly two orders of magnitude fewer than conventionally assumed, without any model retraining or hyperparameter tuning. For new/unseen contingencies, we show that using just 10 labelled samples of the new contingency with EDC encoding matches the best achievable transfer learning oracle model, which requires fully labelled data and is not deployable in practice. Overall, this initial study paves the way towards developing and deploying foundation models for power system operations, with possible applications across multiple operational tasks.

</details>


### [164] [DELUGE: Towards Continental-Scale Daily Pluvial Flood Damage Prediction via Interpretable Conditioning on Foundation Model Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16050)
*Yuya Kawakami,Daniel Cayan,Dongyu Liu,Kwan-Liu Ma,Tom Corringham*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DELUGE 是一个多模态深度学习框架，用于美国本土每日约1公里分辨率的暴雨洪涝灾害损失预测。它利用NFIP索赔数据（2017-2022），通过创新的Value Modulator和Temporal Modulator参数化模块，在空间块留出验证下，相比随机森林、XGBoost和LightGBM基线，在美元加权PR-AUC指标上提升9%到30%，并具备架构级可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 暴雨洪涝占美国NFIP索赔的45%，但比河流和沿海洪水更难预测。现有方法要么分辨率粗糙，要么局限于区域范围，要么计算开销大，不适合日常大陆尺度使用。因此需要一种高分辨率、可扩展且高效的全国性每日预测方法。

Method: 方法包括：(1) 以约1公里分辨率、全国尺度建模，聚焦索赔最高的前100个75km网格单元（覆盖约81%索赔）；(2) 使用经过时空校正的NFIP索赔数据（2017-2022）训练；(3) 多模态架构围绕灾害、暴露度和脆弱性三个风险维度组织；(4) 水文气象分支中的核心创新：Value Modulator和Temporal Modulator，以地形描述符和AlphaEarth基础模型嵌入为条件，输出可直接检查的水文响应参数，实现架构级可解释性。

Result: 在空间块留出验证下，DELUGE在美元加权PR-AUC指标上，比调优的随机森林、XGBoost和LightGBM基线高出9%到30%。该指标强调对实际运营最重要的罕见高成本索赔事件。

Conclusion: DELUGE展示了深度学习在大尺度暴雨洪涝预测中的有效性，其可解释的条件调制方案可推广到其他地理空间预测任务中集成基础模型嵌入。

Abstract: Pluvial (rainfall-driven) flooding accounts for 45% of National Flood Insurance Program (NFIP) claims in the United States and is harder to predict than its riverine and coastal counterparts, with existing approaches limited to coarse resolution, regional domains, or computationally intensive process-based models unsuitable for daily continental-scale use. We present DELUGE, a multimodal deep learning framework for daily pluvial flood damage prediction at ~1 km resolution and national scale, trained on spatially and temporally corrected NFIP claims (2017-2022) and structured around the hazard, exposure, and vulnerability components of disaster risk. Rather than blanket coverage of the Conterminous United States (CONUS), we model the top 100 highest-claim 75 km cells, distributed nationwide and accounting for ~81% of total pluvial flood claims. Our architectural novelty is a pair of parametric modules in the hydrometeorology branch, a Value Modulator and a Temporal Modulator, conditioned on terrain descriptors and AlphaEarth foundation-model embeddings, that expose directly inspectable hydrological response parameters and provide architecture-level interpretability-by-design. Under a spatial block holdout, DELUGE outperforms tuned Random Forest, XGBoost, and LightGBM baselines by 9% to 30% on a dollar-weighted area under the precision-recall curve (PR-AUC), a metric that emphasizes the rare, high-cost claims of greatest operational interest. Beyond DELUGE, we argue this interpretable conditioning scheme is a transferable pattern for integrating foundation-model embeddings into other geospatial prediction tasks.

</details>


### [165] [When Model Merging Rivals Joint Multi-Task Reinforcement Learning: A Task-Vector Geometry Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16062)
*S. Aaron McClendon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在强化学习场景下，测试模型合并（TIES、RAM+）是否能替代联合多任务训练。在AppWorld基准上使用Qwen3-8B专家，合并独立训练的专家并与联合训练模型对比，发现合并效果与联合RL相当，且不同合并方法无显著差异。原因在于任务向量接近正交（余弦0.06-0.10），导致基于支持和符号的合并退化为近均匀平均。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型合并被宣传为联合多任务训练的替代方案，但在强化学习场景中，这种替代从未被真正测试过——合并独立发布的智能体正是因为联合模型不可用。本文填补了这一缺失的比较。

Method: 在AppWorld智能体基准上，使用LOOP训练难度-1和难度-2的Qwen3-8B专家，然后通过TIES和RAM+方法合并这些专家，并与在相同数据上联合训练的模型进行对比。

Result: 在任务目标完成率上，合并模型与联合RL模型表现相当，且所有合并变体之间在统计上无显著差异。进一步分析发现，任务向量接近正交（余弦相似度0.06-0.10），尽管支持重叠约65%，但方向和支撑解耦，导致基于支持和符号的合并（RAM、TIES）退化为近均匀平均。

Conclusion: 模型合并可以作为联合多任务训练的有效替代方案，但其有效性取决于任务向量的几何特性。作者公开了所有代码和统计数据以供复现。

Abstract: Model merging is promoted as a substitute for joint multi-task training, yet in the reinforcement-learning setting this substitution is essentially never tested against the baseline it claims to replace: methods merge independently released agents precisely because a joint model is unavailable. We build the missing comparison. Training difficulty-1 and difficulty-2 Qwen3-8B specialists on the AppWorld agent benchmark with LOOP, we merge them (TIES, RAM+) and pit the result against a jointly trained model on the same data. On task-goal completion, merging matches joint RL -- and every merge variant is statistically indistinguishable. To explain why merge method does not matter here, we measure the geometry of the specialists' task vectors, which carries no task-sampling noise: they are near-orthogonal (cosine 0.06 - 0.10) despite ~65% support overlap, a small, shared direction that grows over training and that we calibrate against a random-init floor and a same-run ceiling to confirm it reflects learning, not the low-rank parameterization. Because direction and support are decoupled, support and sign-based merging (RAM, TIES) collapse to near-uniform averaging. We release all code and statistics.

</details>


### [166] [Physics-Based Deep Spatiotemporal Hyperlocal Radar Nowcasting with a Multi-Variable U-Net for High-Resolution Precipitation Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16080)
*Akshay Sunil,Muhammed Rashid,Raja Sekhar Sivaraju,Sushma Nair,Subimal Ghosh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种紧凑的仅雷达的临近预报框架，结合多仰角反射率、多普勒径向速度和径向速度梯度代理特征，使用编码器-解码器U-Net预测未来90分钟内12个合成反射率场（7.5分钟间隔）。模型在2023年5-8月孟买多普勒雷达观测数据上训练，在90分钟预报时效上对≥10、≥20、≥30 dBZ阈值的临界成功指数分别达到0.437、0.332、0.193，优于持续性预报。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市洪水管理和实时决策需要10-90分钟的降水临近预报，但传统高分辨率数值天气预报因频繁资料同化、模式初始化和spin-up而存在计算延迟。机器学习可从高频观测中直接学习风暴演变并快速生成预报，尤其适用于孟买这种季风对流、海陆相互作用和局地强降雨使短期预报困难的地区。
