Method: 开发了一个紧凑的仅雷达临近预报框架，使用编码器-解码器U-Net结构，输入包括多仰角反射率、多普勒径向速度和径向速度梯度代理特征（速度大小、散度类似场、方向切变类似场和涡度类似场），无需完整反演风场。设计了高反射率注意力模块增强对对流核的敏感性，并通过物理引导归因分析检验学习敏感性是否具有气象意义。模型使用2023年5-8月孟买多普勒雷达观测训练。

Result: 在90分钟预报时效，对≥10、≥20、≥30 dBZ阈值的临界成功指数分别为0.437、0.332、0.193。与持续性预报相比，模型在较长预报时效上具有更低的均方根误差和更高的空间相关性。训练后可在标准计算机上运行，数秒内生成临近预报。

Conclusion: 提出的仅雷达U-Net临近预报框架能够有效利用多普勒雷达多仰角观测和径向速度梯度代理特征，在孟买复杂季风对流环境下实现10-90分钟的快速准确降水预报，具有实时应用潜力。

Abstract: Precipitation nowcasting over the immediate 10-90 min period is important for flood management and real-time decision-making in urban regions. Conventional short-range forecasting with high-resolution numerical weather prediction requires frequent data assimilation, model initialization, and spin-up, introducing computational latency. Machine learning provides an alternative by learning storm evolution directly from high-frequency observations and producing forecasts quickly after training. This is particularly relevant for Mumbai, India, where monsoon convection, land-sea interactions, and localized intense rainfall make short-term prediction difficult. Here, we develop a compact radar-only nowcasting framework that combines multi-elevation reflectivity, Doppler radial velocity, and radial-velocity-gradient proxy features within an encoder-decoder U-Net. Using the most recent radar volume scan, the model predicts 12 future composite reflectivity fields at 7.5-min intervals up to 90 min lead time. The derived velocity magnitude, divergence-like, directional-shear, and vorticity-like channels represent kinematic signatures associated with convergence and boundary interactions without requiring full wind-field retrieval. A high-reflectivity attention module improves sensitivity to convective cores, and physics-guided attribution examines whether the learned sensitivities are meteorologically meaningful. The model is trained using Mumbai Doppler radar observations from May to August 2023 and evaluated on temporally independent events. At 90 min lead time, Critical Success Index values are 0.437, 0.332, and 0.193 for $\geq$10, $\geq$20, and $\geq$30 dBZ thresholds, respectively. Compared with persistence, the model gives lower RMSE and higher spatial correlation at longer lead times. Once trained, it runs on a standard computer, generating nowcasts within seconds for real-time use.

</details>


### [167] [Neural spectroscopy of AlphaFold2 reveals encoded protein conformational landscapes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16087)
*Kaustav Mehta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AlphaFold2's weights, beyond predicting static structures, encode a learned conformational organization that can be probed via neural spectroscopy (Scaled Gaussian Convolution). Perturbing weights reveals physically structured landscapes: ubiquitin contacts break in known order, KaiB alternative fold not recovered, alpha-synuclein shows coherent landscapes where training signal is present. The method reveals emergent conformational knowledge not explicitly trained.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AlphaFold2's 93M parameters are conventionally treated as machinery for sequence-to-structure conversion. The authors propose they can be analyzed directly as a learned encoding of protein conformational organization, extending beyond what unperturbed inference reveals.

Method: The method, termed neural spectroscopy, uses Scaled Gaussian Convolution: smoothing the Evoformer's weight tensors with Gaussian convolution and scaling the result to produce conformational landscapes under perturbation.

Result: Under perturbation, ubiquitin's native contacts break in order established by folding experiments. For KaiB, five independently trained models agree that the alternative fold is not recovered. For alpha-synuclein, five models produce five different but coherent landscapes, mapping where training signal determines representation. Matched-power noise controls confirm random corruption produces debris.

Conclusion: AlphaFold2's weights encode structural constraints shaped by evolutionary and structural training data that extend beyond static structure prediction. The conformational organization visible under perturbation emerged as a byproduct of the training objective, not an explicit target. Neural spectroscopy enables reading these learned constraints.

Abstract: AlphaFold2's 93 million parameters, shaped by the evolutionary record of protein structure encoded in the Protein Data Bank and in sequence alignments, are conventionally treated only as machinery for converting sequence to structure. We propose they are also a scientific object that can be analyzed directly: a learned encoding of protein conformational organization that can be probed and characterized. By smoothing the Evoformer's weight tensors with a Gaussian convolution and scaling the result, we show that the trained model produces physically structured conformational landscapes. Under perturbation, ubiquitin's native contacts break in the order established by decades of folding experiments. For KaiB, five independently trained models agree that the alternative fold is not recovered under perturbation. For alpha-synuclein, five models produce five different but coherent landscapes, mapping where the training signal has determined the representation and where it has not. Matched-power noise controls confirm that random corruption of equal magnitude produces debris, not conformations. The model learned to predict static structures; the conformational organization visible under perturbation was not an explicit training target, suggesting it emerged as a byproduct of that objective. AlphaFold2's weights appear to encode structural constraints, shaped by evolutionary and structural training data, that extend beyond what unperturbed inference reveals. We call the approach of reading them neural spectroscopy, and Scaled Gaussian Convolution one such protocol.

</details>


### [168] [DADiff: Diffusion-Driven Cross-Domain Policy Adaptation for Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16090)
*Hanyang Chen,Anirudh Satheesh,Longchao Da,Hua Wei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DADiff，一种基于扩散模型的在线动力学适应框架，通过生成轨迹差异估计源域和目标域之间的动力学不匹配，并利用奖励修改和数据选择两种变体实现策略迁移，理论证明性能差异受生成轨迹偏差上界约束，实验表明该方法优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习中跨域策略迁移面临动力学不匹配的挑战，现有方法通过域分类器、价值引导数据过滤或表征学习来解决，但仍有改进空间。本文从生成建模视角出发，利用扩散模型捕捉源域与目标域生成轨迹的差异来估计动力学不匹配。

Method: 提出DADiff框架，使用扩散模型在生成下一状态的过程中计算源域和目标域生成轨迹之间的偏差，以此估计动力学不匹配。基于此开发了两种变体：奖励修改（Reward Modification）和数据选择（Data Selection），分别通过调整奖励函数和筛选训练数据来适应目标域。同时提供了理论分析，证明策略在两域的性能差异受生成轨迹偏差的上界约束。

Result: 在多种环境偏移设置下进行大量实验，结果表明DADiff在解决动力学不匹配问题上优于现有方法，提供了充分的性能提升验证。

Conclusion: DADiff从生成建模角度有效解决了跨域动力学适应问题，两种变体各有适用场景，理论分析与实验共同验证了方法的有效性，代码已开源。

Abstract: Transferring policies across domains poses a vital challenge in reinforcement learning, due to the dynamics mismatch between the source and target domains. In this paper, we consider the setting of online dynamics adaptation, where policies are trained in the source domain with sufficient data, while only limited interactions with the target domain are allowed. There are a few existing works that address the dynamics mismatch by employing domain classifiers, value-guided data filtering, or representation learning. Instead, we study the domain adaptation problem from a generative modeling perspective. Specifically, we introduce DADiff, a diffusion-based framework that leverages the discrepancy between source and target domain generative trajectories in the generation process of the next state to estimate the dynamics mismatch. Both reward modification and data selection variants are developed to adapt the policy to the target domain. We also provide a theoretical analysis to show that the performance difference of a given policy between the two domains is bounded by the generative trajectory deviation. More discussions on the applicability of the variants and the connection between our theoretical analysis and the prior work are further provided. We conduct extensive experiments in environments with various shifts to validate the effectiveness of our method. The results demonstrate that our method provides superior performance compared to existing approaches, effectively addressing the dynamics mismatch. We provide the code of our method at https://github.com/hanyang-chen/DADiff-release

</details>


### [169] [The Honest Quorum Problem: Epistemic Byzantine Fault Tolerance for Agentic Infrastructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16109)
*Jun He,Deying Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对智能体验证者（如AI代理）的新型容错模型——Epistemic Byzantine Fault Tolerance (EBFT)。传统的拜占庭容错（BFT）假设故障节点会任意偏离协议，而智能体验证者可能在遵守协议的情况下仍因推理错误而支持语义无效的状态转换，这种故障模式称为“认知故障”（epistemic fault）。EBFT在传统拜占庭故障界限之外，引入了两个置信度索引的参数：e_δ（拜占庭集外的连贯无效认可上限）和 u_ε（不可用验证者支持上限），分别量化语义安全风险与活性退化。论文推导了语义有效性、共识一致性、活性及可行阈值选择的仲裁阈值条件，并给出了校准方法。研究表明，增加名义上不同的智能体只有在能有效降低无效认可或不可用支持的上尾集中度时才能提升容错性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统SMR和BFT共识协议假设故障节点会明确违反协议，但智能体验证者（如大语言模型）可能因推理错误而在协议遵守的情况下支持语义无效的状态转换，且由于共享模型权重、训练数据等，容易产生相关认知故障。现有容错模型无法捕捉此类因“诚实但错误”导致的语义无效风险。

Method: 定义了一种新的容错模型EBFT，在拜占庭故障界限f之外引入两个参数：e_δ（拜占庭集外连贯无效认可的数量上限）和 u_ε（不可用验证者支持的上限）。推导了保证语义有效性、共识一致性和活性的仲裁阈值条件，并提出了基于置信度索引的校准方法，用于估计这些预算。

Result: 得到了语义有效性、一致性、活性及阈值选择的条件。表明增加名义上不同的智能体仅在能显著降低无效认可或不可用支持的上尾集中度时才能改善容错性。

Conclusion: EBFT为智能体基础设施和后确定性分布式系统提供了一种更实际的容错模型，指出传统BFT在智能体验证者场景下不足以保证执行安全性，必须考虑认知故障。未来需要关注如何校准e_δ和u_ε参数，以及设计减轻相关认知故障的机制。

Abstract: State machine replication (SMR) and Byzantine fault-tolerant (BFT) consensus guarantee agreement despite a bounded number of arbitrary, colluding faulty participants. However, these guarantees rely on participants outside this set correctly executing the protocol's transition semantics. Agentic validators expose a weaker boundary: an authenticated, responsive, non-equivocating, and protocol-compliant reasoning participant may still endorse a semantically invalid transition due to reasoning errors.
  We call this failure mode an epistemic fault, and the collective phenomenon the Honest Quorum Problem (where "honest" means protocol-compliant, not semantically correct). Such a quorum can satisfy ordinary checks while forming a certificate for an invalid transition. Thus, agreement alone does not guarantee semantic validity or execution safety. Furthermore, because agentic validators often share model weights, training distributions, prompts, or toolchains, they are highly susceptible to correlated epistemic faults.
  We define Epistemic Byzantine Fault Tolerance (EBFT), a fault-tolerance model for agentic infrastructure and post-deterministic distributed systems. EBFT augments the conventional Byzantine fault bound with two separate, confidence-indexed quantities: $e_δ$ bounds coherent invalid endorsements outside the Byzantine set, and $u_ε$ bounds unusable validator support that degrades liveness. These quantities characterize semantic safety risk and liveness degradation independently. We derive quorum-threshold conditions for semantic validity, consensus agreement, liveness, and feasible threshold selection, and outline a calibration methodology for estimating these budgets. We show that adding nominally distinct agents improves fault tolerance only when it measurably reduces the upper-tail concentration of invalid endorsements or unusable support.

</details>


### [170] [When Do Multi-Agent Systems Help? An Information Bottleneck Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16133)
*Wendi Yu,Lianhao Zhou,Xiangjue Dong,Sai Sudarshan Barath,Declan Staunton,Byung-Jun Yoon,Xiaoning Qian,James Caverlee,Shuiwang Ji*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从信息瓶颈视角解释多智能体系统（MAS）相对于单智能体系统（SAS）的优势条件。核心观测：SAS将所有推理轨迹积累在共享上下文中，而MAS通过有限中继消息连接隔离的局部上下文。在有限带宽中继下，压缩上下文可提升效率但可能丢失任务相关信息，形成信息瓶颈权衡。通过18个受控实验验证，MAS在中继接近充足时（尤其是弱模型）持续获益，但当信息丢失严重时优势减弱或逆转（尤其强模型）。研究表明多智能体设计本质上是信息瓶颈优化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM驱动的多智能体系统（MAS）虽有潜力，但其相对于单智能体系统（SAS）的优势尚不明确，性能在不同设置下变化不一致。需要阐明二者差异的根本原因。

Method: 提出信息瓶颈视角：SAS使用共享上下文累积完整推理轨迹，MAS使用隔离局部上下文通过有限中继消息连接。证明无限带宽下任何SAS可被MAS模拟，而有限中继下压缩引入效率与信息损失的权衡，形式化为参数β控制的信息瓶颈。开展18个受控实验，覆盖5个基准和3个模型规模验证理论。

Result: MAS在中继接近充足时持续有益，尤其对弱模型帮助更大；当信息损失严重时MAS优势缩小甚至逆转，尤其强模型已能从冗余上下文提取有用信息，从压缩中获益小。

Conclusion: 多智能体设计本质上是信息瓶颈优化问题，该视角解释了有限中继通信何时帮助或损害性能。

Abstract: LLM powered multi-agent systems (MAS) have emerged as a promising paradigm for complex tasks. However, their advantages over single-agent systems (SAS) remain unclear, with performance varying inconsistently across settings. Here, we provide an information bottleneck perspective on elucidating the differences between MAS and SAS. Specifically, our key observation is that a SAS accumulates its full reasoning trace in one shared context, while a MAS uses isolated local contexts connected by bounded relay messages. We show that, under infinite relay bandwidth, any SAS can be simulated by a MAS that transmits the full upstream context. Thus, the nontrivial advantage of MAS arises under bounded relays, where compression introduces a fundamental trade-off: reducing redundant context can improve efficiency, but may also incur loss of task-relevant information. We formalize this trade-off as an information bottleneck controlled by an effective parameter $β$, which captures how the balance shifts with model capability, and shows that MAS gains arise when context reduction outweighs relay information loss. We conduct 18 controlled experiments across five benchmarks and three model scales to validate our theoretical studies. We observe that MAS consistently helps when relays are near-sufficient, especially for weaker models. In contrast, MAS gains shrink or reverse when relays incur information loss, especially for stronger models that can already extract useful information from redundant context and thus gain little from compression. Our study shows that multi-agent design is fundamentally an information-bottleneck optimization problem. This perspective explains when bounded inter-agent communication helps or hurts.

</details>


### [171] [Improving Improved Kernel PLS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16138)
*Ole-Christian Galbo Engstrøm*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文改进了快速 PLS 校准算法 IKPLS 1 和 2，通过加速 X 旋转矩阵 R 和 Y 载荷矩阵 Q 的计算，在保持输出完全一致的前提下，实现了 CPU 上约 2 倍、GPU 上约 6 倍的总体加速，并在开源 Python 包 ikpls 中实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IKPLS 算法是目前最快的 PLS 校准算法之一，但其中 R 和 Q 的计算步骤仍有优化空间，尤其是在现代并行硬件上。作者旨在通过更好的并行化策略和计算等价性来进一步加速这些步骤。

Method: 针对 R 的计算，将逐项累加替换为直接求值策略，在保持乘法次数不变的情况下提升并行性；针对 Q 的计算，首次发现每个 Y 载荷可由同一迭代中更早计算出的量（加上显式推导的常数）得到，从而将复杂度从 Θ(KM) 降至 Θ(M)（当 M=1 或 2≤M<K 时）。两种改进均保证与原始算法输出完全一致。

Result: 在 NumPy（CPU）和 JAX（GPU）上的基准测试显示，单独步骤的加速可达两个数量级；整体拟合的加速约为 CPU 2 倍、GPU 6 倍。所有改进已在开源 Python 包 ikpls 中实现。

Conclusion: 改进后的 IKPLS 算法在输出不变的前提下显著提升了计算效率，可作为原始算法的即插即用替代方案，尤其适合现代并行硬件环境。

Abstract: Improved Kernel Partial Least Squares (IKPLS) algorithms 1 and 2 are among the fastest PLS calibration algorithms. This article focuses on two shared steps, the computation of the $\mathbf{X}$ rotations, $\mathbf{R}$, and the $\mathbf{Y}$ loadings, $\mathbf{Q}$, and accelerates both. For $\mathbf{R}$, term-by-term accumulation is replaced by a direct evaluation strategy that requires the same number of multiplications but parallelizes better on modern hardware. For $\mathbf{Q}$, I identify - to the best of my knowledge, for the first time - equivalences showing that each $\mathbf{Y}$ loading is obtainable, up to explicitly derived constants, from quantities already computed earlier in the same iteration, and I exploit them in IKPLS to reduce the cost of each loading from $Θ\left(KM\right)$ to $Θ\left(M\right)$ operations whenever $M = 1$ or $2 \leq M < K$, with $K$ predictor variables (number of columns in $\mathbf{X}$) and $M$ response variables (number of columns in $\mathbf{Y}$). Both improvements provably yield exactly the same $\mathbf{W}$, $\mathbf{P}$, $\mathbf{Q}$, $\mathbf{R}$, and $\mathbf{T}$ as the original algorithms. Benchmarks with NumPy (CPU) and JAX (GPU) show speedups of up to two orders of magnitude for the isolated steps and of approximately $2\times$ (CPU) and $6\times$ (GPU) for entire fits. Both improvements are implemented in the free, open-source Python package \texttt{ikpls}.

</details>


### [172] [PRISA: Proactive Infrastructure LiDAR Framework for Intersection Safety Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16156)
*Tam Bang,Hussam Abubakr,Emiliano de la Garza Villarreal,Truc Phuong Nguyen,Austin Harris,Toru Hirano,Mina Sartipi,Yunfei Xu,Hoang H. Nguyen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PRISA是一个模块化基础设施LiDAR框架，利用隐私保护、低光鲁棒的路侧传感器进行长期交通观测和边缘实时风险检测。它包含感知层和即插即用风险评估模块，后者能自动从累积感知输出中策展站点特定的训练数据，无需人工标注即可训练轨迹预测模型，并部署用于连续运动预测和双重代理安全评估（TTC用于纵向冲突，PPET用于交叉和弱势道路使用者交互）。在R-LiViT数据集和Chattanooga实际信号化路口部署测试，PPET评估端到端延迟194ms（2.4秒预测视界），满足实时性要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市交叉口是道路网络中最危险的位置之一，对车辆和弱势道路使用者（VRUs）构成重大风险。多智能体交互的复杂性需要持续、实时的监控系统，能够在冲突升级为碰撞之前预测冲突。现有方法在隐私保护、低光环境鲁棒性和实时边缘部署方面存在不足。

Method: PRISA框架包含两个核心组件：1) 感知与感知层（利用LiDAR传感器）；2) 即插即用风险评估模块。该模块自动从累积感知输出中策展站点特定的训练数据，无需人工标注即可训练轨迹预测模型，然后部署用于连续运动预测和双重代理安全评估（TTC和PPET）。

Result: 在公共R-LiViT数据集和Chattanooga实际信号化路口部署测试，PPET评估端到端延迟194ms（2.4秒预测视界），TTC检测和感知满足实时约束，验证了多智能体交叉口安全监控的实用可行性。

Conclusion: PRISA框架证明了利用基础设施LiDAR进行隐私保护、实时多智能体交叉口安全监控的实用可行性，其自动数据策展和即插即用设计降低了部署成本，PPET指标特别适合评估VRU参与的交叉冲突。

Abstract: Urban intersections are among the most hazardous locations in road networks, posing significant risks to vehicles and vulnerable road users (VRUs) such as pedestrians and cyclists. The complexity of multi-agent interactions demands continuous, real-time monitoring systems capable of anticipating conflicts before they escalate into crashes. We present PRISA, a modular infrastructure LiDAR framework leveraging privacy-preserving, low-light-robust roadside sensors for long-term traffic observation and real-time risk detection at the edge. The framework comprises two core components: a sensing and perception layer and a plug-and-play risk assessment module. The latter automatically curates site-specific training data from accumulated perception outputs to train a trajectory prediction model without manual annotation. It then deploys the trained model for continuous motion forecasting and dual surrogate safety evaluation, using Time-to-Collision (TTC) for longitudinal conflicts and Predicted Post-Encroachment Time (PPET) for crossing and VRU-involved interactions. PRISA is evaluated on the public R-LiViT dataset and deployed on an NVIDIA Jetson AGX Thor at a live signalized intersection in Chattanooga, Tennessee. PPET-based assessment operates at 194~ms end-to-end latency over a 2.4-second predictive horizon, with TTC-based detection and perception remaining within real-time constraints, demonstrating practical feasibility for proactive multi-agent intersection safety monitoring.

</details>


### [173] [Behaviour-Conditioned Neural Processes for Adaptive Residential Short-Term Load Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16168)
*Ramin Soleimani,Andrea Visentin,Dirk Pesch*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于行为条件化的注意力神经过程（Behaviour-conditioned Attentive Neural Process, BANP）模型，用于住宅短期负荷概率预测。该模型通过离散潜变量表征用户行为结构，并利用聚类信息进行弱监督训练，在不依赖真实行为标签的条件下实现上下文条件下的概率预测。在SGSC数据集上的实验表明，该方法在MAE和CRPS指标上优于标准ANP和固定窗口确定性基线，尤其在有限上下文场景下提升显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 住宅短期负荷预测面临住户用电需求异质性强、时间变化大、受多样化行为模式影响等挑战。现有方法通常将行为结构作为外部分组信号使用，而非将其嵌入预测机制内部。本文旨在探索能否将推断出的行为结构直接嵌入神经过程（Neural Process）的概率预测框架中，实现单模型、不确定性感知的跨用户、跨上下文、跨预测时域的负荷预测。

Method: 提出行为条件化的注意力神经过程（BANP）框架，将每个负荷曲线视为一个预测任务。通过离散潜变量表示行为结构（从可用上下文中推断），用于条件化解码器；连续潜变量捕捉异质曲线间的共享功能不确定性。训练时利用聚类结果作为弱监督信号（无需真实行为标签），测试时仅依赖上下文推断的类别分布进行条件化。在SGSC数据集上采用用户分离的划分方式，对比了不同上下文长度和多步预测时域下的性能。

Result: 所提变体在MAE和CRPS上全面优于标签无关的ANP基线，尤其在有限上下文下增益最大。最优变体相对ANP平均降低MAE 7.9%、CRPS 6.9%。与固定窗口确定性基线相比，该变体在所有预测时域上RMSE更低，同时MAE保持竞争力，表明在异质用电模式下大偏差预测更少。

Conclusion: 将推断的行为结构嵌入神经过程预测机制是有效的，单模型即可实现跨异质用户、不同上下文和多种预测时域的不确定性感知预测。行为条件化通过弱监督聚类信息即可实现，无需真实行为标签，具有良好的实用价值。

Abstract: Residential short-term load forecasting (STLF) is challenging because household demand is heterogeneous, temporally variable, and shaped by diverse behavioural routines. This work investigates whether inferred behavioural structure can be embedded within the forecasting mechanism of a Neural Process-based probabilistic model, rather than used only as an external grouping signal, for context-conditioned residential STLF. We propose a behaviour-conditioned Attentive Neural Process framework that treats each load profile as a forecasting task. Behavioural structure is represented by a discrete latent variable inferred from the available context and used for behaviour-conditioned decoder conditioning, while a continuous latent variable captures shared functional uncertainty across heterogeneous profiles. To enable conditioning without ground-truth behavioural labels, clustering-derived information provides weak supervision during training, whereas test-time conditioning relies only on context-inferred class distributions. Experiments on the Smart Grid, Smart City (SGSC) dataset use user-disjoint train/validation/test splits, variable context lengths, and multi-step forecast horizons, with comparisons against a label-agnostic ANP baseline and fixed-window deterministic STLF baselines. The proposed variants improve MAE and CRPS over ANP across horizons and context settings, with the largest gains under limited context. The best-performing variant achieves average reductions of 7.9% in MAE and 6.9% in CRPS relative to ANP. Compared with fixed-window baselines, this variant achieves lower RMSE across all evaluated horizons while maintaining competitive MAE, suggesting fewer large prediction deviations under heterogeneous consumption patterns. These results support single-model, uncertainty-aware forecasting across heterogeneous households, contexts, and horizons.

</details>


### [174] [When Does Muon Help Agentic Reinforcement Learning?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16169)
*Kai Ruan,Jinghao Lin,Zihe Huang,Ziqi Zhou,Qianshan Wei,Xuan Wang,Hao Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Muon优化器在稀疏奖励的智能体强化学习中显著优于AdamW，在ALFWorld任务上使用GiGPO将验证成功率从0.290提升至0.546（+88%），且效果依赖于优势估计器和学习率；在GRPO和GraphGPO中也有提升，表明Muon可受益于智能体RL，但多种子和跨任务验证仍有待研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索Muon优化器（在大规模预训练中与AdamW竞争）在强化学习后训练阶段的有效性，尤其是在稀疏奖励的智能体强化学习场景中，填补Muon在RL post-training中的空白。

Method: 在ALFWorld任务上使用Qwen2.5-0.5B-Instruct模型，通过匹配的单种子比较，在Group-in-Group Policy Optimization (GiGPO)框架下将Muon仅应用于隐藏权重矩阵，并与AdamW对比；同时考察不同优势估计器（GRPO、GraphGPO）和学习率的影响。

Result: GiGPO下Muon使最终窗口验证成功率从0.290提升至0.546（+88%），高学习率AdamW对照组无后更新成功；在3e-5学习率下Muon将GRPO成功率从0.161提升至0.268，GraphGPO晚期窗口差距缩小；在1e-5学习率下GraphGPO Muon达到0.901成功率，归一化验证AUC从0.399提升至0.556，且达到0.5和0.7成功率分别提前30和60次更新。

Conclusion: Muon优化器能够有效提升智能体强化学习性能，建议联合研究策略优化器、优势估计器和学习率；多种子和跨任务验证仍需进一步探索。

Abstract: Muon is competitive with AdamW in large-scale pre-training, but its value for reinforcement-learning (RL) post-training remains unclear. We study vanilla Muon in sparse-reward agentic RL through matched single-seed comparisons with AdamW on ALFWorld using Qwen2.5-0.5B-Instruct. Under Group-in-Group Policy Optimization (GiGPO), applying Muon only to hidden weight matrices raises final-window validation success from 0.290 to 0.546 (+88%); high-rate AdamW controls retain no post-update success. The effect depends on the advantage estimator and learning rate. At 3e-5, Muon improves GRPO from 0.161 to 0.268, whereas GraphGPO's late-window gap narrows near saturation. At 1e-5, GraphGPO Muon reaches 0.901, raises normalized validation AUC from 0.399 to 0.556, and reaches 0.5 and 0.75 success 30 and 60 updates earlier, respectively. These exploratory results show that Muon can benefit agentic RL and motivate studying the policy optimizer, advantage estimator, and learning rate jointly. Multi-seed and cross-task validation remain open.

</details>


### [175] [Physics-enhanced reinforcement learning for real-time optimal control of dynamical systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16177)
*Matteo Tomasetto,Nicolò Botteghi,Gabriele Bruni,Andrea Manzoni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PEARL bridges reinforcement learning and optimal control for high-dimensional parametric dynamical systems by combining short-horizon automatic differentiation gradients with adjoint-based sensitivity analysis for long-term returns, achieving sample efficiency and scalability.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RL algorithms are sample inefficient and require many environment interactions, especially for high-dimensional systems. This limits RL to sparse sensors and actuators due to the curse of dimensionality in exploration-exploitation.

Method: PEARL employs an actor-adjoint algorithm that uses automatic differentiation to compute policy gradients over short horizons and adjoint-based sensitivities of future returns (approximated via neural networks) to reduce environment interactions and mitigate long-term gradient instabilities.

Result: On two challenging parametric navigation problems in unsteady flows, PEARL outperforms state-of-the-art RL algorithms, demonstrates sample efficiency, generalizes across multiple scenarios, and scales to high-dimensional state/action spaces without requiring low-dimensional representations or multi-agent strategies.

Conclusion: Physics-guided policy learning enables efficient RL for high-dimensional parametric dynamical systems, bridging RL and traditional optimal control effectively.

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has recently emerged as a promising feedback control strategy for nonlinear and complex dynamical systems. However, RL algorithms are sample inefficient and require a large number of interaction with the environment to synthesize optimal control strategies. Consequently, applications of RL are typically limited to sparse sensors and actuators due to the curse of dimensionality entailed by the exploration-exploitation dilemma in high-dimensional spaces. In this work, we bridge RL and traditional optimal control for dynamical system with a novel Physics-EnhAnced Reinforcement Learning (PEARL) paradigm tailored to the control of high-dimensional and parametric dynamical systems, exploiting the differentibility of their dynamics. Specifically, PEARL employs an actor-adjoint algorithm that leverages automatic differentiation to compute policy gradients over short horizons and adjoint-based sensitivities of future returns approximated via neural networks, significantly reducing the number of environment interactions, while mitigating long-term gradient instabilities. Through two challenging parametric navigation problems in unsteady flows, we show that PEARL (i) effectively exploits differentiable environments to outperform state-of-the-art RL algorithms, (ii) is sample efficient, thanks to the physics-guided policy learning, (iii) generalizes across multiple scenarios, which is crucial when dealing with parametric systems, and (iv) enables scaling RL to high-dimensional state and action spaces, without requiring low-dimensional state representations or multi-agent strategies.

</details>


### [176] [A Blueprint for Equilibrium-Based Differentiable Continuous-Variable Thermodynamic Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16183)
*Owen Lockwood,Jérémy Béjanin,Joost Bus,Christopher Chamberland,Patrick Huembeli,Frank Schäfer,Guillaume Verdon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用热力学计算（基于随机模拟过程的能量基模型）的硬件蓝图，旨在高效、快速地处理机器学习工作负载。通过实现可调能量势的物理硬件（如超导电路），结合概率图模型框架，作者展示了如何构建和训练多种机器学习模型，并从理论和数值上分析了能耗与延迟。初步实验采用热噪声驱动的随机模拟超导电路，为节能概率机器学习硬件铺平了道路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决机器学习工作负载日益增长的能源和延迟需求，寻求一种能效高且速度快的计算范式。

Method: 提出热力学计算堆栈蓝图，利用物理硬件中的随机模拟过程（朗之万动力学）实现可调能量势，从而生成和采样参数化能量基模型；借助概率图模型框架构建和训练主流机器学习模型；通过理论分析和数值研究评估运行时与能耗；并采用热噪声驱动的随机模拟超导电路作为初步硬件实现。

Result: 展示了如何基于硬件原生能量基模型构建和训练多种机器学习模型；给出了不同模型在热力学范式下的运行时与能耗分析结果；初步实验实现了热噪声驱动的超导电路。

Conclusion: 这些结果为面向概率机器学习的节能热力学硬件指明了发展路径。

Abstract: To address the escalating energy and latency demands of machine-learning workloads, we introduce a blueprint for an energy-efficient and fast thermodynamic computing stack that leverages stochastic analog processes in physical hardware. In this work, we focus on energy-based thermodynamic computing where the stochastic process is well described by Langevin dynamics with tunable energy potentials. The implementation of such potentials in physical hardware enables us to generate and sample from basic parameterized energy-based models. We demonstrate how to construct and train popular classes of machine learning models based on these hardware-native energy-based models, using the framework of probabilistic graphical models. We analyze the runtime and energy consumption of different models in this thermodynamic paradigm based on theoretical considerations and numerical studies. As a preliminary experimental realization of such hardware, we present our stochastic analog superconducting circuits driven by thermal noise. Together, these results outline a path toward energy-efficient thermodynamic hardware for probabilistic machine learning.

</details>


### [177] [PagedWeight: Efficient MoE LLM Serving with Dynamic Quality-Aware Weight Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16184)
*Yuchen Yang,Yifan Zhao,Anisha Dasgupta,Sasa Misailovic*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PagedWeight 是一种用于 MoE 大语言模型（LLM）推理服务的新型权重管理方法，通过运行时动态量化专家权重，在 KV 缓存与模型精度之间取得平衡，显著提升 GPU 内存效率和吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在 KV 缓存密集型的 MoE 模型服务场景中，模型权重与不断增长的 KV 缓存之间存在 GPU 内存竞争，导致精度、内存和吞吐量之间的复杂权衡。

Method: 提出 PagedWeight，一种运行时动态量化 MoE 模型权重的方法，能够根据当前 KV 缓存大小动态调整专家权重的精度，从而在模型任务精度、内存消耗和吞吐/延迟之间进行有效导航。

Result: 在多个内存敏感的 MoE 服务场景中，PagedWeight 相比现有的量化基线方法在质量-内存权衡上取得改进：达到 FP16 同等精度时节省高达 72.0% GPU 内存，吞吐量提升 1.94 倍；在相似内存预算下，质量相比量化方法提升高达 39.3%，且吞吐损失不超过 4.1%。

Conclusion: PagedWeight 有效解决了 MoE 模型在 KV 缓存密集场景中的资源竞争问题，为高性能、高精度的 LLM 推理服务提供了一种实用的动态量化方案。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) is a popular class of large language models (LLMs), offering high efficiency and accuracy. However, in KV-cache-intensive serving scenarios, MoEs often exhibit a tension between the GPU memory requirements of the model weights and the growing KV cache. We propose PagedWeight, a novel management method for MoE LLM serving that dynamically quantizes MoE model's weights at runtime and balances expert-weight precision with the KV cache sizes. PagedWeight exposes and effectively navigates the complex tradeoff between the model's task accuracy, memory consumption, and throughput/latency. Across several memory-sensitive MoE serving scenarios, PagedWeight improves the quality-memory tradeoff over several existing quantization baselines. PagedWeight achieves FP16-equivalent accuracy with up to 72.0% GPU memory savings and 1.94$\times$ throughput improvement, and improves quality over quantization methods by up to 39.3% at a similar memory budget with at most 4.1% throughput loss.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [178] [GraphDx: A Cost-Aware Knowledge-Enhanced Multi-Agent Framework for Sequential Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15280)
*Shaoting Tan,Ning Liu,Yuntao Du,Shuyue Wei,Wu Shuai,Qian Li,Yanyu Xu,Wei Zhang,Lizhen Cui,Haitao Yuan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GraphDx 是一个知识增强框架，通过构建医疗诊断知识图谱（MDKG）并引入三个协作智能体（感知、推理、决策），解决了大语言模型在顺序诊断中无法在成本约束下系统推理的问题。在 MedQA 和 MIMIC-IV 数据集上的实验表明，GraphDx 将诊断成功率从 50–68% 提升至 79–93%，同时将测试成本降低 20–54%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型虽编码了大量医学知识，但在资源成本约束下无法进行系统性的推理，常常过度测试，存在知识-推理鸿沟。因此需要一种能在诊断准确性和资源成本之间取得平衡的自动化临床诊断方法。

Method: 提出 GraphDx 框架，包含两项核心创新：（1）自动化管道利用 LLM 构建具有量化典型性、以行动为中心的拓扑结构以及双重目标属性（诊断相关性和成本敏感性）的医疗诊断知识图谱（MDKG）；（2）引入三个协作智能体——感知智能体和决策智能体负责语言理解与生成，推理智能体在 MDKG 上执行确定性证据评分和成本感知规划。

Result: 在 MedQA 和 MIMIC-IV 数据集上使用 DeepSeek-V3、Kimi-k2、Llama-3.3 三种 LLM 主干进行实验，GraphDx 将诊断成功率从 50–68% 提升至 79–93%，并将测试成本降低 20–54%。

Conclusion: GraphDx 提供了一种稳健、经济且可解释的自动化临床诊断解决方案，有效弥合了 LLM 在医学知识推理中的鸿沟，在提升诊断准确率的同时显著降低了医疗资源成本。

Abstract: Sequential diagnosis requires balancing diagnostic accuracy against resource costs through iterative information gathering. Existing Large Language Model (LLM) approaches exhibit a critical knowledge-reasoning gap: despite encoding extensive medical knowledge, they struggle to reason systematically under cost constraints, often resorting to excessive testing. We propose GraphDx, a knowledge-enhanced framework with two core innovations. First, we design an automated pipeline that leverages LLMs to construct Medical Diagnosis Knowledge Graphs (MDKGs) with quantized typicality, action-centric topology, and dual-objective attributes for both diagnostic relevance and cost-sensitivity. Second, we introduce three collaborative agents (Perception, Reasoning, and Decision) where the Perception and Decision Agents handle language understanding and generation, while the Reasoning Agent performs deterministic evidence scoring and cost-aware planning on the MDKG. Experiments on MedQA and MIMIC-IV across three LLM backbones (DeepSeek-V3, Kimi-k2, Llama-3.3) show that GraphDx improves diagnostic success rates from 50--68% to 79--93% while reducing test costs by 20--54%, providing a robust, economical, and interpretable solution for automated clinical diagnosis.

</details>


### [179] [Causal-Audit: Explicit and Auditable Graph-based Reasoning via Target-Aware Causal Chain Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15281)
*Su Lan,Xuefei Yin,Yanming Zhu,Alan Wee-Chung Liew*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Proposes an explicit and auditable causal reasoning framework for context-free intervention-based QA, using target-aware causal graph construction and path-level causal evidence aggregation, outperforming existing LLM methods.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing LLM-based methods for causal and intervention-based QA rely on implicit language-level reasoning, leading to opaque causal assumptions, unverifiable reasoning paths, and fragile predictions under complex interventions, especially in context-free settings.

Method: Formulates causal inference as structured reasoning over an explicit causal graph through four modular stages. Key innovations: (1) target-aware causal graph construction that treats the target variable as a core constraint to suppress irrelevant variables and spurious relations; (2) path-level causal evidence aggregation that combines multiple causal paths while modeling reinforcing and counteracting effects.

Result: Consistently outperforms existing LLM-based methods on three benchmarks while providing interpretable and auditable causal reasoning traces.

Conclusion: The proposed explicit causal reasoning framework enables robust, interpretable, and auditable intervention-based QA, surpassing implicit LLM reasoning approaches.

Abstract: Causal and intervention-based question answering is fundamental to advancing large language models (LLMs) toward reasoning beyond surface-level correlations and understanding underlying causal mechanisms. However, existing LLM-based methods often rely on implicit language-level reasoning, resulting in opaque causal assumptions, unverifiable reasoning paths, and fragile predictions under complex interventions, particularly in context-free settings. In this paper, we propose an explicit and auditable causal reasoning framework for context-free intervention-based question answering. Our method formulates causal inference as structured reasoning over an explicit causal graph through four modular stages, rather than implicit end-to-end prediction. A key innovation is a target-aware causal graph construction strategy that treats the target variable as a core constraint during graph expansion, effectively suppressing irrelevant variables, spurious causal relations, and reasoning noise. We further introduce a path-level causal evidence aggregation mechanism that combines multiple causal paths while modeling both reinforcing and counteracting effects, enabling robust decision-making beyond single-chain reasoning. Extensive experiments on three benchmarks demonstrate that our framework consistently outperforms existing LLM-based methods while providing interpretable and auditable causal reasoning traces.

</details>


### [180] [Cura 1T: Specialized Model for Agentic Healthcare](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15314)
*actAVA AI,:,Haolin Chen,Leon Qi,Steve Brown,Deon Metelski,Tao Xia,Joonyul Lee,Qixuan Wang,Kevin Riley,Frank Wang,Weiran Yao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Cura 1T is a healthcare-specialized LLM trained via a human-gated self-evolution loop that iteratively plans, trains, evaluates, and refines data mixtures to address diverse healthcare tasks (consultation, clinical reasoning, diagnosis, EHR tool use) without degrading other capabilities, achieving top performance on healthcare benchmarks while staying competitive on general reasoning and agentic tasks.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Healthcare LLMs need to handle multiple high-stakes tasks (communication, reasoning, diagnosis, EHR tool use) simultaneously, but narrow updates for one task often degrade others. Existing specialized LLMs lack coverage across these diverse use cases together.

Method: A human-gated self-evolution loop: in each round, a training agent plans a target capability, trains the model, evaluates benchmark trajectories, and refines the data mixture based on observed failures. The model improves via targeted synthetic and curated examples rather than a single generic medical-data update.

Result: Cura 1T ranks at or near the top among frontier baselines across the healthcare evaluation suite, and remains competitive on out-of-domain reasoning and agentic benchmarks.

Conclusion: The human-gated self-evolution loop enables effective multi-capability healthcare specialization without catastrophic forgetting, outperforming narrow single-update approaches.

Abstract: Healthcare spans high-stakes communication, expert reasoning, and workflow execution, yet specialized LLMs that cover these use cases together remain limited. A healthcare model must handle patient consultation, clinical reasoning over text and images, interactive diagnosis, and electronic health record (EHR) tool use. These capabilities fail in different ways, and a narrow update for one task can degrade another. We present Cura 1T, a healthcare-specialized LLM trained through a human-gated self-evolution loop. In each evolution round, a training agent plans a target capability, trains the model, evaluates benchmark trajectories, and refines the data mixture from observed failures. This data-centered loop improves the model through targeted synthetic and curated examples rather than a single generic medical-data update. Across the healthcare evaluation suite, Cura 1T ranks at or near the top among frontier baselines, while remaining competitive on out-of-domain reasoning and agentic benchmarks.

</details>


### [181] [AnovaX: A Local, Multi-Agent Voice Assistant with LLM Planning, Typed Executors, and Adaptive Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15367)
*Raunak B Sinha*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AnovaX is a local-first desktop voice assistant that runs entirely on the user's computer, using a single Python process to coordinate wake-word detection, speech recognition, an LLM planner (Gemini), a safety layer, a multi-agent orchestrator, and an adaptive recovery loop. It treats the desktop as its action surface, with specialized agent classes for various tasks, and provides a phone-friendly remote interface over local WiFi.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Desktop voice assistants are still dominated by cloud pipelines that ship raw audio off the machine and expose a fixed set of skills. The authors wanted to show that a legible, few-thousand-line assistant running locally can perform complex desktop tasks without sending audio to the cloud and without the LLM directly touching the keyboard.

Method: The system uses a single Python process that wires together: a wake-word gate, a speech pipeline, an LLM planner (Gemini) that emits a JSON plan of tool calls, a whitelist-and-denylist safety layer, a multi-agent orchestrator that translates each plan into typed child agents on a bounded thread pool, and an adaptive recovery loop that takes over when core steps fail. Each tool corresponds to a specialized agent class (AppAgent, TypingAgent, BrowserAgent, etc.) with its own timeout, retry policy, and shared-resource locks. A recursive MetaAgent allows the planner to delegate a sub-goal back to itself (capped at two levels of nesting). The recovery loop uses a compact ReAct-style prompt and hides Gemini's latency behind speculative execution of read-only tools. A companion Flask server exposes a phone-friendly remote over local WiFi, mirrors agent lifecycle events, and streams the laptop's screen as MJPEG.

Result: The system can open apps, type into them, run searches, coordinate concurrent actions, recover from single-step failures, and be driven entirely from a phone in another room without the LLM ever touching the keyboard. The project demonstrates that a few-thousand-line assistant is sufficient for these tasks.

Conclusion: The point of the project is to show that a legible, few-thousand-line assistant is enough to perform complex desktop tasks locally, without competing with commercial assistants like Siri or Alexa. It demonstrates that local-first voice assistants can be practical and transparent.

Abstract: Desktop voice assistants are still dominated by cloud pipelines that ship raw audio off the machine and expose a fixed set of skills. We describe AnovaX, a small local-first assistant that runs entirely on the user's computer and treats the desktop itself as its action surface. A single Python process wires together a wake-word gate, a speech pipeline, an LLM planner (Gemini) that emits a JSON plan of tool calls, a whitelist-and-denylist safety layer, a multi-agent orchestrator that translates each plan into typed child agents on a bounded thread pool, and an adaptive recovery loop that takes over whenever a core step fails. Every tool corresponds to a specialized agent class (AppAgent, TypingAgent, BrowserAgent and six others) with its own timeout, retry policy, and shared-resource locks. A recursive MetaAgent lets the planner delegate a sub-goal back to itself, capped at two levels of nesting. The recovery loop uses a compact ReAct-style prompt and hides Gemini's latency behind speculative execution of read-only tools. A companion Flask server exposes a phone-friendly remote over the local WiFi, mirrors every agent lifecycle event to the phone in real time, and streams the laptop's screen back over MJPEG so the user can watch remote commands land as they run. The point of the project is less to compete with Siri or Alexa than to show that a legible, few-thousand-line assistant is enough to open apps, type into them, run searches, coordinate concurrent actions, recover from single-step failures, and be driven entirely from a phone in another room -- without the LLM ever touching the keyboard.

</details>


### [182] [Precise but Uncoupled: Reviewer Precision Does Not Guarantee Critique Uptake in Multi-Agent Math Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15388)
*Chih-Hsuan Yang,Jingyan Jiang,Vikram Vasudevan,Cheng-Hau Yang,Huihuo Zheng,Le Chen,Eliu A. Huerta,Venkatram Vishwanath,Ian T. Foster,Rajeev Thakur*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究数学与科学领域的多智能体协作系统，质疑“专用评审阶段能有效修正错误答案”的普遍假设。基于4181道Omni-MATH问题，使用gpt-oss-120b进行实验，发现广播式同伴讨论在高难度层级上显著优于规划-执行-评审流水线（PER），且该差距并非源于评审者精度（PER评审者精度0.861高于广播的0.644），而是源于评审意见能否有效改变后续候选答案。评审者检测质量与批评采纳效果在实证中可分离。强制明确承认评审意见会降低最终准确率，而将评审指导直接嵌入求解上下文可部分改善跟进但无法消除差距。总体而言，以评审者为中心的评估可能高估系统质量：协议能很好发现错误，但若不基于批评采取行动，仍无法解决更多问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多面向数学和科学的智能体系统采用分层设计，包含专门的评审角色，认为评审阶段能够将错误候选转化为正确结果。本文旨在实证检验这一假设，探究评审环节在协作求解中的实际效用。

Method: 在4181道经过验证的Omni-MATH问题上，使用匹配的gpt-oss-120b模型作为智能体，对比广播式同伴讨论与规划-执行-评审流水线（PER）两种协作协议。通过控制评审者精度、批评采纳率等变量，分析不同协议的性能差异和评审环节的实际影响。

Result: 广播式讨论在高难度层级准确率更高；PER评审者精度更高（0.861 vs 0.644），但其有用批评更难改变后续候选答案，导致评审引导修复效果更差。强制评审意见确认降低最终准确率，将评审指导嵌入求解上下文可部分改善但无法消除差距。

Conclusion: 评审者检测质量与批评采纳效果在实证中可分离。以评审者为中心的评估可能高估系统质量：协议能很好发现错误，但若不基于批评采取行动，仍无法解决更多问题。设计协作协议时，应更关注批评如何被采纳并影响后续决策，而非仅提升评审精度。

Abstract: Many math- and science-oriented agent systems use hierarchical designs with specialized reviewer roles, assuming that a dedicated review stage should help turn wrong candidates into correct ones. We test this assumption on 4,181 verifier-grounded Omni-MATH problems using matched gpt-oss-120b actors. Collaboration adds little on the easiest tiers, but from tier 4 onward the gains open sharply; in this harder regime, broadcast-style peer discussion reaches higher final accuracy than a planner-executor-reviewer pipeline (PER). We ask whether this gap is explained by reviewer quality or by whether critique changes the next answer the protocol carries forward. It is not explained by reviewer precision alone: PER's reviewer is more precise than broadcast's (0.861 vs. 0.644), yet evaluator-verified useful critique is much less likely to change the next candidate and produces lower reviewer-guided repair. These results show that reviewer detection quality and critique uptake are empirically separable. Within matched PER interventions, forcing explicit acknowledgment lowers final accuracy, while embedding reviewer guidance directly in the solver's working context partially improves follow-through without closing the gap. Overall, reviewer-centric evaluation can overstate system quality: a protocol may spot errors well yet still fail to solve more problems if it does not act on those critiques.

</details>


### [183] [DrawingVQA: A Real-World Benchmark for Multi-Depth Visual-Textual Reasoning on Construction Drawings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15418)
*Yoonhwa Jung,Junryu Fu,Mani Golparvar-Fard*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DrawingVQA是首个面向真实建筑图纸评估多模态大语言模型（MLLM）的基准测试，包含33份施工图纸和92个专家设计的问答对，覆盖三种推理深度。评估表明当前最先进MLLM与专家表现存在显著差距，尤其在高层次推理上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准多聚焦自然图像或简单示意图，缺乏对建筑图纸这一融合抽象几何、符号标注、表格数据和领域文本的复杂视觉-文本领域的系统性评估，无法真实反映MLLM在工程实践中的能力。

Method: 构建了DrawingVQA基准，包含33份"签发施工"图纸和92个专家问答对，设计了三层推理深度（感知理解、上下文解释、领域专家推理），并提出双分类框架从7个建筑工程维度和4个MLLM能力维度联合分析性能。

Result: 评估多个最先进MLLM后发现模型与专家表现存在显著差距，尤其在需要深层领域推理的任务上表现不佳。

Conclusion: DrawingVQA为领域专用的多模态推理奠定了基础，有助于推动AI理解与真实工程工作流的深度融合。

Abstract: We introduce DrawingVQA, the first benchmark designed to evaluate multimodal large language models (MLLMs) on real-world construction drawings -- a core media in architecture, civil, and many other engineering practices. Unlike natural images or schematic floor plans, construction drawings fuse abstract geometry, symbolic notation, tabular data, annotations, and domain-specific text, forming a uniquely complex visual-textual domain core to engineering workflows. DrawingVQA bridges this gap with 33 "Issued for Construction" drawings and 92 expertly curated question-answer pairs, spanning three reasoning depths: perceptual understanding, contextual interpretation, and domain-expert reasoning. To evaluate model capabilities, we present a dual categorization framework to jointly analyze performance across seven construction-engineering and four MLLM capability dimensions -- the first to explicitly map engineering workflows to AI reasoning competencies. Evaluations of state-of-the-art MLLMs reveal a substantial gap between model and expert performance, particularly at higher reasoning depths. This benchmark lays a foundation for domain-specialized multimodal reasoning to allow for advancement on integration of AI-driven understanding and real-world engineering workflows.

</details>


### [184] [Do Coding Agents Need Executable World Models, Simplification, and Verification to Solve ARC-AGI-3?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15439)
*Sergey Rodionov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究通过四个嵌套的Codex智能体变体，系统归因了ARC-AGI-3智能体中可执行世界建模、计划简化与精确重放验证三个组件的贡献。主要实验在gpt-5.4和gpt-5.5上评估，发现所有变体随模型增强和推理努力增加而提升；完整验证处理在所有设置中排名第一但资源消耗更大；在gpt-5.6-sol上验证变体完全解决了所有公开游戏，但仅为公共集饱和而非泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前ARC-AGI-3智能体捆绑了多个组件，其各自对性能的贡献尚不明确，需要进行归因分析以理解每个组件的实际作用。

Method: 设计了四种嵌套Codex智能体变体：纯文本基线、无重放验证的可执行世界模型、带计划简化的可执行模型、保留简化并要求精确复现观测的固定接口验证变体。在gpt-5.4和gpt-5.5的高与极高推理努力设置下进行主实验，并在gpt-5.6-sol上进行探索性后续实验。

Result: 所有变体随模型增强和推理努力增加而提升；变体间差异小于预期；纯文本变体在gpt-5.5中优于可执行变体；计划简化在多数设置中提升性能；完整验证变体在所有设置中排名第一但资源消耗更大；在gpt-5.6-sol上验证变体完全解决所有公开游戏，RHAE约99%，行动数少于人类基线一半。

Conclusion: 完整验证处理效果最好但资源代价高；可执行世界模型并非普遍有益；计划简化有正面作用但受设置影响；gpt-5.6-sol上的完美表现仅反映公共集饱和，因模型在游戏之后训练且未测试保留集，故不能视为泛化能力。

Abstract: Our previous ARC-AGI-3 agent bundled executable world modeling, scheduled simplification, and exact replay verification, leaving unclear which idea accounted for its performance. We address this attribution question with four nested Codex-based agents: a textual baseline; a flexible-interface executable world model without replay verification; the same executable model with scheduled simplification; and a fixed-interface verification treatment that retains simplification and requires exact reproduction of recorded observations. The main study evaluates all four agents with gpt-5.4 and gpt-5.5 at high and xhigh reasoning effort on the public ARC-AGI-3 games. Exploratory follow-ups evaluate the textual and verification variants with gpt-5.6-sol at xhigh and max. The most robust result is that every agent variant improves with a stronger model and with greater reasoning effort. Within each model-effort setting, differences among variants are smaller than anticipated, while the effects of individual components vary across settings. Requiring a persistent executable deliverable is not universally beneficial: the textual variant outperforms the flexible-interface executable variant in both gpt-5.5 settings. Simplification improves performance in three of the four model-effort settings, with the weakest setting as the only exception. The complete verification treatment ranks first in all four settings, although it uses substantially more resources. In the gpt-5.6-sol follow-up, the verification variant fully solves every public game at both reasoning efforts, achieves about 99% RHAE, and uses fewer than half the total actions of the human baseline. Because the model postdates these games and held-out performance remains untested, this result should be interpreted as saturation of the public set only.

</details>


### [185] [Beyond a Joke: Multi-Angle Reasoning for Detecting and Explaining Harmful Humor in Memes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15442)
*Shanhong Liu,Pai Chet Ng,De Wen Soh,Malika Meghjani,Konstantinos N. Plataniotis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 MAR-12 框架，利用视觉-语言模型从 12 个结构化视角解释网络模因中的幽默与仇恨元素，通过角色感知软门控注意力与原型分类器实现可解释的模因检测，在 PrideMM 和 Memotion 数据集上达到 80.3% 幽默检测和 75.9% 仇恨检测准确率，并生成人类可理解的解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络模因融合视觉、文本与文化语境，幽默与恶意意图并存，现有多模态分类器忽视其相互依赖关系或仅提供有限可解释性，亟需可解释的模因理解系统以支持准确分类与人类可理解性。

Method: MAR-12 框架：(1) 利用视觉-语言模型从幽默与仇恨理论中提取 12 个结构化视角；(2) 采用角色感知软门控注意力机制学习各视角的贡献权重；(3) 使用原型分类器进行最终预测；(4) 结合视角特定推理与注意力权重合成可解释文本。

Result: 在 PrideMM 和 Memotion 数据集上，幽默检测准确率达 80.3%，仇恨检测准确率达 75.9%，优于现有方法。人类评估和 GPT-4 评估均证实 MAR-12 能生成连贯且有说服力的解释，尤其适用于幽默与有害线索共存的模因。

Conclusion: MAR-12 通过结构化视角与注意力机制有效提升了模因检测的可解释性与准确性，为幽默与仇恨并存的复杂场景提供了可靠的推理框架，验证了多视角融合在可解释模因理解中的有效性。

Abstract: Internet memes intertwine visual cues, textual content, and cultural context, making them particularly challenging to interpret in scenarios where humor, sarcasm, and harmful intent coexist. These complexities highlight the need for explainable meme understanding systems that can provide reliable and structured reasoning to support both accurate classification and human interpretability. However, existing multimodal classifiers either overlook these interdependencies or provide only limited interpretability. In this paper, we introduce MAR-12, a novel framework that leverages Vision Language Models (VLMs) for meme detection and understanding in settings where humorous and hateful elements may coexist. The framework first interprets each meme through twelve structured perspectives derived from humor and hate theories. It then applies a role-aware soft-gated attention mechanism to learn how much each perspective should contribute, followed by a prototype-based classifier for the final prediction. Finally, explanations are synthesized using both perspective-specific reasoning and learned attention weights, ensuring transparent and context-grounded justifications. We evaluate MAR-12 on the PrideMM and Memotion datasets, where it achieves up to 80.3% accuracy for humor detection and 75.9% accuracy for hate detection, outperforming state-of-the-art approaches. Furthermore, both human and GPT-4-based evaluations confirm that MAR-12 produces coherent and persuasive explanations, particularly for memes in which humorous and harmful cues co-occur.

</details>


### [186] [From Black Box to Executable Logic: Explainable Reinforcement Learning through Prolog Expert Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15459)
*Eduardo C. Garrido-Merchán*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种将深度强化学习策略（黑盒）转化为可解释的Prolog逻辑程序的方法，通过三阶段后处理：提取冻结的PPO教师策略、归纳有序规则列表、输出为可执行Prolog程序，再通过策略评估认证的编辑扩展阶段提升性能。证明了有限MDP中返回损失有界、单调改进和终止性；连续观测下转换的保真度与B成反比，返回差距同速率闭合，但下界显示对于倾斜决策边界代价随观测维度指数增长。实验在键门任务上达到最优回报，在连续控制任务上Acrobot匹配教师、CartPole恢复97%回报、LunarLander部分恢复符合指数下界预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度强化学习策略是黑盒，难以解释和理解。本文旨在通过将策略重写为可读、可执行、可编辑的逻辑程序（Prolog），使其变得可解释，同时保持或提升性能。

Method: 三阶段后处理：1) 提取冻结的PPO教师策略；2) 以经典关系归纳学习方式从决策中归纳有序规则列表；3) 输出为Prolog程序，由现成逻辑引擎执行。随后扩展阶段通过策略评估认证编辑规则库，仅接受能提升回报的编辑。提供四种理论保证：返回损失有界、单调改进、终止性、连续观测下的保真度与下界。

Result: 在键门任务（16,944状态）上扩展Prolog程序达到最优回报；预算受限下10/10种子超过随机教师。连续控制：Acrobot用11条规则匹配教师（噪声内），CartPole恢复约97%回报，LunarLander仅部分恢复，符合指数下界预测。

Conclusion: 将深度RL策略转化为可解释逻辑程序是可行的，在离散任务中能保持甚至超越原策略性能，连续任务中保真度受限于维度指数下界。该方法提供了可验证的决策逻辑，便于人类理解和编辑。

Abstract: A trained deep reinforcement learning policy is a black box, and we ask whether it can be made explainable by rewriting it as an executable logic program that reproduces its behaviour and that a person can read, a logic engine can run, and an optimizer can edit. We present a three-stage post-hoc transformation that extracts a frozen proximal policy optimization teacher, induces an ordered rule list from its decisions in the manner of classical relational learning, and emits the result as a Prolog program whose every decision is executed by an off-the-shelf logic engine; a subsequent expansion stage edits the rule base and accepts an edit only when policy evaluation certifies a return increase. We prove four guarantees. A return-loss bound makes the distilled program a machine-checkable certificate in a finite Markov decision process, and the expansion loop improves monotonically and terminates. For the continuous-observation setting we answer whether the conversion is possible at all: the propositional threshold instantiation converts the network to arbitrary fidelity as the resolution B grows, with disagreement O(1/B) and a return gap that closes at the same rate, and a matching lower bound shows the cost is exponential in the observation dimension for an oblique decision boundary. Empirically, on a two-room key-and-door task with 16,944 reachable states the expanded Prolog program attains exact optimal return in every seed and, in a budget-capped regime, exceeds the stochastic teacher on exact return in ten of ten seeds. On three continuous-control tasks the emitted program substitutes the network, matching the neural teacher within noise on Acrobot with eleven clauses and recovering about 97% of its return on CartPole, while on the finer-control LunarLander it recovers only partially, exactly the ceiling the exponential lower bound predicts.

</details>


### [187] [A Critical Analysis of Trustworthy AI Tools, Mark Frameworks, and the Implementation Chasms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15480)
*Michael Papademas,Xenia Ziouvelou,Kostas Karpouzis,Vangelis Karkaletsis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文基于OECD综合数据集，对可信人工智能（TAI）的实施工具和信任标记框架进行了批判性分析。通过实证映射和描述性比较分析，发现当前TAI工具在伦理焦点、生命周期覆盖、利益相关者定位和工具类型上存在显著不对称：重点关注公平性、透明度和鲁棒性，而可解释性、数字安全和环境可持续性被忽视；大多数工具集中于开发后阶段，缺乏对早期设计和数据收集的指导；教育和政策参与明显不足。研究主张弥合AI原则与实践之间的鸿沟需要扩展伦理目标、将伦理嵌入AI全生命周期并促进多利益相关方参与。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管出现了大量高层次伦理指南，但批评者认为这些框架仍然抽象，缺乏具体实施机制。因此，有必要对旨在将可信人工智能（TAI）付诸实践的工具和信任标记框架进行系统性批判分析，以识别当前实施中的差距和不足。

Method: 基于OECD综合数据集，采用实证映射和描述性比较分析的方法，对可信人工智能的实施工具和信任标记框架进行系统分析，识别伦理焦点、生命周期覆盖、利益相关者定位和工具类型方面的分布特征与不对称性。

Result: 研究发现：公平性、透明度和鲁棒性受到高度重视，而可解释性、数字安全和环境可持续性关注不足；大多数工具和认证集中在开发后阶段，对早期设计和数据收集阶段的指导有限；教育倡议和政策参与明显不足；当前TAI努力主要由行业背景下的技术性和程序性措施主导。

Conclusion: 弥合AI原则与实践之间的鸿沟需要：扩展伦理目标以覆盖更广泛的议题；将伦理考量嵌入AI全生命周期；促进更广泛的多利益相关方参与。本研究为现有实施缺口提供了诊断，并提出了推进更全面、包容和可执行的AI治理的可操作建议。

Abstract: As artificial intelligence (AI) systems increasingly impact society, ensuring their ethical and trustworthy deployment has become a global priority. While a myriad of high-level ethical guidelines have emerged, criticism persists that these frameworks remain abstract and lack concrete mechanisms for implementation. This paper conducts a critical analysis of tools and trust mark frameworks intended to operationalize trustworthy AI (TAI), drawing on a comprehensive dataset from the OECD. Through empirical mapping and descriptive comparative analysis, we identify significant asymmetries in ethical focus, lifecycle coverage, stakeholder targeting, and tool typology. Our findings show a strong emphasis on fairness, transparency, and robustness, with comparatively little attention paid to explainability, digital security, and environmental sustainability. Moreover, most tools and certifications concentrate on post-development stages, with limited guidance for early design or data collection phases. Educational initiatives and policy engagement are notably underdeveloped, suggesting that current TAI efforts are dominated by technical and procedural measures within industry contexts. We argue that bridging the persistent chasm between AI principles and practice requires expanding ethical objectives, embedding ethics across the AI lifecycle, and fostering broader multi-stakeholder participation. This study provides both a diagnosis of existing implementation gaps and actionable recommendations for advancing more holistic, inclusive, and enforceable AI governance

</details>


### [188] [Logic, Optimization, and Artificial Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15532)
*J. N. Hooker*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文综述了逻辑与优化在基于规则的AI中的协同作用，重点探讨了如何利用优化技术增强逻辑推理的可扩展性和透明度，涵盖了概率逻辑、贝叶斯逻辑、信念逻辑、Dempster-Shafer理论、非单调逻辑、多值逻辑、布尔回归等多种逻辑-优化结合领域，并展望了未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI透明度（可复现性、可解释性、可信度、公平性）日益受到关注，基于规则的AI因其天然透明性成为有吸引力的解决方案。然而规则推理的计算复杂性需要优化技术来支撑。逻辑提供规则编码和推理的媒介，优化则提供高效推理的计算技术，二者的结合在当前背景下具有新的现实意义。

Method: 本文采用综述方法，系统梳理了逻辑与优化合作的多个领域：概率逻辑、贝叶斯逻辑、信念逻辑与Dempster-Shafer理论、非单调（默认）逻辑、多值逻辑、基于布尔回归的噪声数据逻辑公式推断。还介绍了如何利用决策图和基于逻辑的Benders分解计算投影（逻辑与优化的基本问题），以及使用后最优性分析增强可解释性，并讨论了优化在回答集编程模理论中的作用。

Result: 本文展示了逻辑与优化结合在多种推理范式中的有效应用，指出先进的优化方法使基于规则的透明AI变得更加实用，并通过决策图、Benders分解等技术实现了核心推理任务（投影计算），后最优性分析进一步增强了结论的可解释性。

Conclusion: 逻辑与优化的融合为透明AI提供了坚实的技术基础，未来可进一步探索更高效的优化算法、更丰富的逻辑体系结合，以及在实际系统中提升可解释性与公平性。

Abstract: Logic and optimization can, in combination, make valuable contributions to rule-based AI. Logic is the obvious medium for encoding a rule base and drawing inferences from it, while optimization provides a powerful technology for computing inferences. Their combination has taken on new relevance amid a growing concern for transparency in AI. which is important for reproducibility, explainability, trustworthiness, and fairness. Rule-based AI provides a natural solution to transparency that is becoming increasingly practical due to today's highly advanced optimization methods. This article surveys several areas of logic-optimization partnership, including probabilistic logic, Bayesian logic, belief logics and Dempster-Shafer theory, nonmonotonic (default) logic, many-valued logics, and inference of logical formulas from noisy data based on Boolean regression. It shows how to compute projections, the fundamental problem of both logic and optimization, using decision diagrams and logic-based Benders decomposition. It describes the use of postoptimality analysis to explain how conclusions are reached, further enhancing transparency, as well as the role of optimization in answer set programming modulo theories. The paper concludes by suggesting possible future research directions.

</details>


### [189] [SeerGuard: A Safety Framework for Mobile GUI Agents via World Model Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15550)
*Xue Yu,Bo Yuan,Pengshuai Yang,Kailin Zhao,Hong Hu,Junlan Feng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SeerGuard 是一个面向移动 GUI 智能体的后果感知安全框架，通过指令级预执行筛选和动作级风险评估来缓解安全风险。该框架利用多任务学习构建统一的安全增强世界模型（SAWM），实现语义级下一状态预测与安全风险评估的集成。实验表明，SeerGuard 能有效提升智能体的安全-效用分数并降低风险成本分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动 GUI 智能体在自动化复杂任务时表现出色，但单个错误动作可能导致不可逆转的严重后果。现有安全机制主要采用反应式策略，缺乏在执行前评估风险的能力，亟需一种能够在动作执行前主动识别和预防风险的安全框架。

Method: 提出 SeerGuard 框架，包含两个核心组件：1) 指令级预执行筛选，在指令层面过滤潜在不安全操作；2) 动作级风险评估，在智能体提出动作后、执行前，分析当前 GUI 状态下的预期后果以识别风险。通过多任务学习构建统一的安全增强世界模型（SAWM），将语义级下一状态预测与安全风险评估任务整合，使模型能够同时预测动作的后续状态变化和对应的安全风险等级。

Result: 在 Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct 上，SeerGuard 将安全-效用分数从 0.191 提升至 0.596（ω=0.8），风险成本分数从 0.347 降至 0.130（α=0.8）。大量实验证明 SeerGuard 能够有效泛化到多种不同的移动 GUI 智能体上。

Conclusion: SeerGuard 作为一种后果感知的安全框架，通过预执行指令筛选和动作风险评估显著提升了移动 GUI 智能体的安全性。对 SAWM 的进一步分析验证了指令级筛选的有效性，以及动作风险评估和下一状态预测能力的可靠性，表明该方法具有良好的泛化能力和实用价值。

Abstract: Mobile graphical user interface (GUI) agents have demonstrated remarkable capabilities in automating complex tasks, yet they introduce critical safety risks where a single erroneous action can lead to irreversible consequences. Existing safety mechanisms are primarily reactive, lacking the ability to assess risks before execution. In this paper, we introduce SeerGuard, a consequence-aware safety framework designed to mitigate these risks through pre-execution instruction-level screening and action-level risk assessment. Specifically, the action-level assessment analyzes agent-proposed actions within current GUI states, anticipating likely outcomes to identify risks before they are executed. To enable these capabilities, we construct a unified safety-augmented world model (SAWM) via multi-task learning, integrating semantic next-state prediction with safety risk assessment. Extensive experiments demonstrate that SeerGuard generalizes effectively across diverse mobile GUI agents. On Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct, it increases the safety-utility score from $0.191$ to $0.596$ at $ω=0.8$ and reduces the risk-cost score from $0.347$ to $0.130$ at $α=0.8$. Further analyses on our SAWM validate the effectiveness of the instruction-level screening, alongside the capability of action risk assessment and next-state prediction.

</details>


### [190] [MGDT: MLLM-Guided Diffusion Transformer with Relation-Adaptive Mixture-of-Experts for Multimodal Knowledge Graph Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15592)
*Xu Hou,Meiyu Liang,Wei Huang,Yawen Li,Zhe Xue,Wu Liu,Guanhua Ye,Lei Shi,Kangkang Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MGDT proposes an align-then-diffuse paradigm for Multimodal Knowledge Graph Completion, using a Relation-Adaptive Mixture-of-Experts to select relevant multimodal features, a frozen MLLM to align them, and a Knowledge Graph Diffusion Transformer for denoising; outperforms baselines on three benchmarks.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing diffusion-based MKGC methods denoise directly on raw multimodal features, forcing the denoiser to simultaneously perform relation-dependent cue selection, cross-modal semantic alignment, and structure-aware entity generation, leading to noisy and semantically inconsistent conditions and suboptimal performance.

Method: MGDT is built on an align-then-diffuse paradigm: (1) RASR-MoE module selects relation-relevant multimodal semantic transformation paths; (2) frozen MLLM aligns routed multimodal representations into a unified latent space; (3) KGDT performs graph-conditioned denoising in the aligned space to produce the missing entity representation.

Result: Consistently outperforms strong baselines on three benchmark datasets.

Conclusion: The align-then-diffuse paradigm with relation-adaptive semantic routing and MLLM-guided alignment improves MKGC performance by reducing noisy and semantically inconsistent conditions for diffusion.

Abstract: Multimodal Knowledge Graph Completion (MKGC) requires inferring missing entities from structural, textual, and visual cues. Existing diffusion-based MKGC methods usually denoise directly on raw multimodal features. Such a design forces the denoiser to simultaneously perform relation-dependent cue selection, cross-modal semantic alignment, and structure-aware entity generation, which introduces noisy and semantically inconsistent conditions for diffusion and consequently leads to suboptimal completion performance. To address this limitation, we propose MGDT: MLLM-Guided Diffusion Transformer with Relation-Adaptive Mixture-of-Experts (MGDT), a novel MKGC framework built on an align-then-diffuse paradigm. MGDT first employs a Relation-Adaptive Semantic Routing Mixture-of-Experts (RASR-MoE) module to select relation-relevant multimodal semantic transformation paths and suppress irrelevant modality interference. MGDT then uses a frozen Multimodal Large Language Model (MLLM) as a semantic anchor to align the routed multimodal representations into a unified latent space and reduce cross-modal semantic heterogeneity. Finally, a Knowledge Graph Diffusion Transformer (KGDT) performs graph-conditioned denoising generation in the aligned space to produce the missing entity representation. Experiments on three benchmark datasets show that MGDT consistently outperforms strong baselines.

</details>


### [191] [Neuro-Symbolic AI for LEED compliance: Document-Centric Benchmarking, Deterministic Numeric Checking, and When Multimodal Hurts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15647)
*Aritro De,Juliana Felkner*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种神经符号流水线，用于LEED v4.1 BD+C认证文档的自动合规性筛查。该流水线结合基于关键词签名的证据检索、本地部署的4B参数语言模型（gemma3:4b）进行文本合规验证，以及确定性数值检查器处理定量阈值。在四栋大学建筑的484份PDF和153项信用决策测试中，纯文本核心验证器达到67.3%准确率，优于8B模型；完整神经符号配置达到61.6%准确率。研究表明，添加低分辨率图像会降低准确率，提示策略的有效性取决于项目的文档丰富度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LEED v4.1 BD+C认证过程文档密集，评审员需手动阅读数百页项目证据并应用信用阈值逻辑，效率低下且易出错。现有研究缺乏针对LEED自动化审查的可重复基准，小语言模型在此领域的实际效能尚不明确，神经符号方法与纯文本方法的优劣也未得到系统比较。

Method: 构建神经符号流水线：(1) 将项目PDF对齐到LEED信用章节；(2) 使用信用感知关键词签名检索证据； (3) 由本地部署的4B参数语言模型（gemma3:4b）进行合规验证； (4) 应用LEED特定数值检查器处理定量阈值。在四栋大学建筑（484份PDF，153项信用级别决策）上评测，并与更大模型（llama3.1:8b）及消融实验（含低分辨率图像、不同提示策略）进行系统比较。

Result: 4B参数模型（gemma3:4b）作为文本核心验证器达67.3%准确率，超越8B模型（llama3.1:8b）。确定性数值检查器将EA-p2从50%提升至100%准确率，并改进多项定量信用。完整神经符号流水线总体准确率61.6%，落后于最佳纯文本基线，原因在于证据提取失败和对定性类别的保守判断。添加低分辨率图像（150-300 dpi）一致降低准确率。提示有效性取决于项目真实通过率：rubric提示在文档丰富项目上最佳，思维链提示在文档稀少项目上最佳。

Conclusion: 在LEED v4.1 BD+C合规验证的特定范围内，该神经符号流水线及其基线提供了首个可重复的准确率和失败模式参考点。4B小语言模型在此任务上优于8B模型，确定性数值组件能有效修正算术错误，但整体神经符号配置受提取失败制约，且视觉信息未带来增益。

Abstract: LEED v4.1 BD+C certification remains a document-intensive process that requires reviewers to read hundreds of pages of project evidence and apply credit-specific threshold logic by hand. This paper investigates whether small, locally deployed language models can perform meaningful screening of LEED documentation and how deterministic symbolic components should share that work. A neuro-symbolic pipeline is introduced that aligns project PDFs to LEED credit sections, retrieves evidence with credit-aware keyword signatures, verifies compliance with a locally hosted 4-billion-parameter language model, and applies a LEED-specific numeric checker to quantitative thresholds. Experiments on four university buildings (484 PDFs, 153 credit-level decisions) show that a 4-billion-parameter model (gemma3:4b) is the strongest text-only core verifier, achieving 67.3% accuracy and outperforming a larger 8-billion-parameter model (llama3.1:8b) in this task. The deterministic numeric checker corrects arithmetic errors on key quantitative credits, moving EA-p2 from 50% to 100% accuracy and improving several other credits when required values are reliably extracted. At the same time, the full neuro-symbolic configuration achieves 61.6% overall accuracy, trailing the best text-only baseline due to extraction failures and conservative behavior on qualitative categories. Systematic ablations show that adding low-resolution drawing images (150-300 dpi) consistently reduces accuracy, and that prompt effectiveness depends on the building's ground-truth PASS rate: rubric prompts perform best on documentation-rich projects, while chain-of-thought prompts perform best on documentation-lean projects. Within the specific scope of LEED v4.1 BD+C compliance verification over raw project documentation, this pipeline and its baselines provide an initial reproducible reference point for both accuracy and failure modes.

</details>


### [192] [ToolVerse: Unlocking Massive Environments and Long-Horizon Tasks for Agentic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15660)
*Shuaiyu Zhou,Fengpeng Yue,Zengjie Hu,Yuanzhe Shen,Chenyang Zhang,feng hong,Cao Liu,Ke Zeng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了ToolVerse框架，通过自动构建大规模可执行代理训练环境（基于近400个现实世界MCP协议，约4500个工具），设计基于工具依赖图的任务生成策略（GUST数据集），以及细粒度Turn-Aware Relative Advantage算法解决信用分配问题，显著提升了LLM在长期工具使用和动态环境中的推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM代理在简洁明确的场景中表现出色，但在大规模、多样、动态的真实环境中难以保持鲁棒性和有效性，缺乏无缝工具集成能力。为了解决这一差距，需要一种可扩展的框架来支持代理在工具集成推理（TIR）任务中进行复杂的长期推理。

Method: 1) 环境构建：ToolVerse自动从近400个真实世界的模型上下文协议（MCP）中构建大规模可执行代理训练环境，包含约4500个工具。2) 任务设计：基于工具依赖图，利用动态解锁采样算法生成长期任务，构建GUST（图解锁采样任务）数据集。3) 信用分配：提出细粒度Turn-Aware Relative Advantage算法，缓解长期代理强化学习中的信用分配问题。最后进行大量代理强化学习训练。

Result: 在多个代理基准测试上进行评估，实验结果表明该框架显著增强了LLM在长期工具使用中的能力，取得了明显的性能提升，并在动态环境中展现出鲁棒的推理能力。

Conclusion: ToolVerse框架有效提升了LLM在复杂真实环境中的工具集成推理能力，为代理强化学习提供了可扩展的环境构建和任务生成方法，验证了其在动态、大规模场景下的有效性。

Abstract: While LLM agents demonstrate strong reasoning abilities in compact and well-defined scenarios, they struggle to maintain robustness and effectiveness when faced with large-scale, diverse, and dynamic real-world environments that demand seamless tool integration. To address this gap, we introduce ToolVerse, a comprehensive framework that scales up agentic RL environments and enables agents to perform complex long-horizon reasoning in Tool-Integrated Reasoning (TIR) tasks. First, ToolVerse automatically builds the massive executable agent training environments from nearly 400 real-world Model Context Protocols (MCPs) that contain about 4500 tools. Second, we propose a task design strategy based on a tool dependency graph, utilizing Dynamic Unlocking Sampling Algorithm to generate long-horizon tasks, and produce GUST (Graph Unlocking Sampling Tasks) dataset. Third, to alleviate the credit assigment problem in long-horizon agentic RL, we propose a fine-grained Turn-Aware Relative Advantage algorithm. We conduct extensive Agentic RL training using ToolVerse and evaluate our framework on serveral agentic benchmarks. Experimental results demonstrate that our framework significantly strengthens LLMs' capabilities in long-horizon tool use, achieving a marked performance boost and showcasing robust reasoning within dynamic environments.

</details>


### [193] [S1-Omni: A Unified Multimodal Reasoning Model for Scientific Understanding, Prediction, and Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15686)
*Jiahao Zhao,Junyi Liu,Lifeng Xu,Nan Xu,Qingli Wang,Qingxiao Li,Tianle Chen,Xiaoyu Wu,Yawen Zheng,Zikai Wang,Guanming Liu,Hequn Zhou,Jingyi Wang,Jingyuan Shu,Keqi Wang,Li He,Songyang Diao,Wenhui Xu,Xinyu Ren,Yaqin Fan,Yujin Zhou,Zhanao Yao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: S1-Omni是一个统一的科学多模态推理模型，通过统一表示、知识对齐和任务解码三大组件，整合了异构科学数据、科学定律和专家知识，在200个科学任务上训练，在60多个基准上超越GPT-5.5和Gemini-3.1-Pro，并匹配或超越领域专用模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI4S领域存在严重的碎片化问题：领域专用模型、工具增强LLM和科学语言模型各自为政，缺乏对异构数据、科学定律和专家知识的联合建模能力，限制了科学推理的整体性。

Method: 架构包含三大组件：1) 统一表示：将自然语言指令与多种科学对象（CIF、SMILES、蛋白质序列、光谱、科学图像）映射到共享表示空间；2) 自然世界知识对齐：在数据构建和训练中融入科学定律与专家知识；3) 任务解码：支持属性预测、光谱到分子生成、蛋白质位点与结构预测、科学图像生成与编辑等多种任务。

Result: 在S1-Omni-Corpus（200个任务，数百万推理样本）上训练，在60多个科学基准上评估，在大部分基准上超越GPT-5.5和Gemini-3.1-Pro，在多个基准上匹配或超越领域专用模型。

Conclusion: S1-Omni为统一的科学建模提供了一条实用路径，证明了跨任务、跨模态、跨领域的科学推理模型是可行的，有望弥合现有AI4S方法的碎片化鸿沟。

Abstract: We present S1-Omni, a unified multimodal reasoning model for scientific understanding, prediction, and generation. AI for Science (AI4S) has advanced significantly through domain-specific models, tool-augmented LLMs, and scientific language models. However, model capabilities remain highly fragmented, limiting the joint modeling of heterogeneous data, scientific laws, and expert knowledge. S1-Omni addresses this gap by consolidating these capabilities into a single, coherent scientific reasoning model. The architecture of S1-Omni is built upon three core components: unified representation of scientific data, natural-world knowledge alignment, and decoding for domain-specific tasks. First, S1-Omni maps natural-language instructions and scientific objects, including CIF, SMILES, protein sequences, spectra, and scientific images, into a shared representation space. Second, it incorporates scientific laws and expert knowledge into data construction and training, enabling the model to reason from scientific evidence. Third, it performs task-specific decoding to support a broad range of applications, including property prediction, spectrum-to-molecular generation, protein site and structure prediction, and scientific image generation and editing. S1-Omni is trained on S1-Omni-Corpus, which covers 200 scientific tasks and contains millions of reasoning samples, and is evaluated on over 60 scientific benchmarks. It outperforms GPT-5.5 and Gemini-3.1-Pro on most benchmarks and matches or surpasses domain-specific models on several benchmarks. Overall, S1-Omni provides a practical path toward unified scientific modeling.

</details>


### [194] [Behavioral Controllability of Agentic Models for Information Extraction: From Fixed Workflows to Reflective Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15715)
*Lujia Zhang,Xingzhou Chen,Hongwei Feng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文研究在学术PDF数据集提取任务中，反思和记忆等智能代理组件是否比固定LLM工作流带来可观测且可控的改进。通过对比固定基线、反思变体及优化代理条件（S2，含更丰富PDF工具和动态工具选择），重点评估过程级行为（工具执行、重试、反思、记忆使用、运行时间和故障恢复），以提取覆盖率和字段完整性为次要指标，探讨代理机制何时改变系统行为、是否改进任务完成以及故障模式如何驱动优化设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLM代理被广泛用于复杂信息提取任务，但反思和记忆等代理组件是否能带来可观测且可控的改进仍不明确。作者希望通过系统对比来阐明这一问题。

Method: 在会议论文数据集提取任务中，比较固定工作流基线、反思代理变体以及优化代理条件（S2，集成了更丰富的PDF工具和动态工具选择）。评估着重于过程级行为（工具执行、重试、反思、记忆使用、运行时间、故障恢复），同时将提取覆盖率和字段完整性作为次要结果指标。

Result: 论文描述了代理机制何时改变系统行为、这些改变是否改进了任务完成，以及观察到的故障模式如何在同一评估框架下推动优化代理设计。

Conclusion: 通过过程级行为分析，论文揭示了代理组件的影响条件，并展示了基于故障模式驱动的优化代理设计方法，为理解何时以及为何使用代理机制提供了实证依据。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are increasingly used for complex information-extraction tasks, yet it remains unclear whether agentic components such as reflection and memory lead to observable and controllable improvements over fixed LLM workflows. We study this question through conference-paper dataset extraction, where a system must identify datasets mentioned in scholarly PDFs and produce structured records. We compare a fixed workflow baseline with reflective agent variants and specify an optimized agent condition (S2) that extends the same task with richer PDF tools and dynamic tool selection. Our evaluation emphasizes process-level behavior--including tool execution, retries, reflection, memory use, runtime, and failure recovery--while treating extraction coverage and field completeness as secondary outcome measures. The paper characterizes when agentic mechanisms change system behavior, whether these changes improve task completion, and how the observed failure modes motivate an optimized agent design under the same evaluation harness.

</details>


### [195] [NeurOWL: An LLM-Based Neural-symbolic Framework for Incomplete OWL Ontology Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15776)
*Hui Yang,Jiaoyan Chen,Yiping Song,Renate Schmidt,Wen Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: NeurOWL是一个端到端的神经符号框架，利用大语言模型和本体嵌入，针对不完整OWL本体中的子类关系推理问题，同时进行语义验证和溯因推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界中的本体往往不完整，导致传统的基于形式的推理难以处理缺失的公理。现有工作缺乏一个统一框架来同时验证非蕴含子类关系的语义合理性，并解释缺失的潜在公理。

Method: 提出NeurOWL神经符号框架，联合使用形式语义（本体嵌入）和文本语义（大语言模型），端到端地同时执行子类关系验证和溯因推理，无需预定义缺失公理候选集。

Result: 在多个领域的真实本体上评估，NeurOWL展现出强大且鲁棒的跨域性能。

Conclusion: NeurOWL有效地统一了子类关系验证与本体溯因，通过融合形式与文本语义解决了不完整本体中的推理挑战。

Abstract: OWL ontologies provide a formal knowledge representation framework that enables semantic reasoning, and have been widely adopted across domains such as healthcare and bioinformatics. In practice, however, real-world ontologies are often incomplete, which pose challenges for reasoning. In this work, we focus on a fundamental subsumption reasoning problem: given an incomplete ontology and a candidate (non-entailed) subsumption, determine whether the subsumption is semantically plausible and, if so, providing a logically sound explanation containing potential missing axioms. This task unifies subsumption verification with ontology abduction, and generalizes the latter by removing the need for a predefined candidate set of missing axioms. To address this subsumption reasoning problem, we propose NeurOWL, an end-to-end neuro-symbolic framework that jointly performs verification and abduction, leveraging both formally defined semantics and textual semantics through Large Language Models and ontology embeddings. We evaluate NeurOWL on real-world ontologies across multiple domains, demonstrating strong and robust performance across different domains.

</details>


### [196] [AgentFAIR: A Multi-Agent Collaborative Framework for FAIRness Evaluation of Geospatial Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15781)
*Ming Chen,Pranav Pai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AgentFAIR，一个多智能体框架，用于评估地理空间数据集的FAIR合规性。该框架结合结构化元数据提取和13个LLM评估器，分别对FAIR子原则进行0-3分成熟度评分，并附证据和建议；一个批评者检查证据一致性，必要时请求重新评估。实验显示，在50个数据集上，FAIR评分均值分别为可发现性79.7%、可访问性70.4%、互操作性45.3%、可重用性72.0%；与四个基线工具的秩相关系数在0.31-0.61之间；子原则一致性达89%，专家研究Fleiss' kappa为0.71，API成本约每数据集0.054美元。结果支持可审计性和可行性，但有限基准、不完整消融和单一模型家族验证限制了关于准确性和泛化性的结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有FAIR合规评估工具使用不同的评分标准和证据来源，且在处理JavaScript渲染页面或特定仓库标识符时可能失败，导致评估不一致。对于50个数据集，不同工具归一化得分的标准差平均达15.0个百分点，最高达30.3。因此需要一种更一致、可审计的评估方法。

Method: 提出AgentFAIR多智能体框架，包含：结构化元数据提取模块，13个针对FAIR子原则的LLM评估器（每个输出0-3成熟度评分、引用证据和改进建议），以及一个批评者（检查证据一致性并请求针对性重新评估）。

Result: 在50个数据集上，平均可发现性79.7%、可访问性70.4%、互操作性45.3%、可重用性72.0%。与四个基线工具的秩相关系数0.31-0.61；FAIR-enough比较无统计显著性。10数据集重复运行中，子原则一致性平均89%（标准差3个百分点），无批评者时为71%。15数据集专家研究显示Fleiss' kappa为0.71，与专家共识对齐82%。API成本约0.054美元/数据集。

Conclusion: AgentFAIR在可审计性和可行性方面表现良好，但受限于有限基准、不完整消融实验和单一模型家族验证，关于准确性和泛化性的结论需谨慎。框架可用于FAIR评估，但需进一步验证。

Abstract: Geospatial datasets support applications from urban planning to climate modeling, yet consistent assessment of FAIR compliance is difficult. Existing evaluators use different rubrics and evidence sources and may fail on JavaScript-rendered pages or repository-specific identifiers. For 50 datasets from 10 repositories, the standard deviation of normalized scores across available tools averages 15.0 percentage points and reaches 30.3 for one dataset. Because these outputs are not equivalent measurements, we use them to characterize disagreement and failure modes, not comparative accuracy. We present AgentFAIR, a multi-agent framework combining structured metadata extraction with 13 sub-principle-specific LLM evaluators. Each produces a 0-3 maturity score, cited evidence, and recommendations; a critic checks evidence and consistency and can request targeted re-evaluation. Mean Findability, Accessibility, Interoperability, and Reusability scores are 79.7%, 70.4%, 45.3%, and 72.0%.
